## Long-term Knowledge

### Action

<!-- lore:019fb9ae-845b-729a-909d-ef2d0bbde2c5 -->
* **Deslop fix/verify compound hotkey CGEvent suppression**: Steps: 1. Apply minimal patch to macos-key-listener.swift: emit KEY\_DOWN:\<name> in tap callback before returning nil for suppressed hotkey events. 2. Rebuild native binary (swiftc) and TypeScript typecheck. 3. Verify full build passes (turbo build). 4. Test compound hotkey (Option+Space or similar) — dictation should activate. 5. Confirm no duplicate KEY\_DOWN events for normal typing. 6. Open PR #396 and link to issue. Gotchas: - Do NOT change KEY\_UP handling. - Globe/Fn and modifier-only hotkeys use different code path and are unaffected. - Ensure no other tap/monitor ordering assumptions break.

### Animation

<!-- lore:019fb971-5553-77e2-b874-45c7f1575d68 -->
* **Pill exit animation: shrink + fade without text**: After transcription completes, the pill animates via DOM ref (no React state change): 1. Capture \`pillRef.current\` 2. Animate \`opacity: 1 → 0\` over 200ms 3. Animate \`min-width: 32px → 0\`, \`padding: 0 10px → 0\`, \`gap: 3 → 0\` over 300ms 4. On \`onExited\`, call \`hidePill()\` to hide the Electron window 5. No "done" text, no "pasted" state, no 1200ms delay Key fixes: - Removed \`hidePill()\` from main-process \`paste:text\` handler so window stays open - Removed always-mounted orb \`width: 0\` hack; restored conditional rendering - Removed \`style.cssText = ""\` resets that wiped React inline styles - Removed unused \`Check\` icon, \`message\` state, \`done\` state, \`doneTimerRef\` - Animation uses \`requestAnimationFrame\` for smooth 60fps shrink/fade Files changed: \`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx\`

<!-- lore:019fb96f-4135-7d7d-bcb3-875a939dc166 -->
* **Pill initializing animation on first hotkey press**: When the hotkey is first pressed, the pill transitions to an 'initializing' state (pulsing glow) while waiting for mic access and streamer setup. This replaces the previous behavior where the pill would just show a static spinner or no feedback. The animation uses a CSS keyframe (\`pulse-glow\`) and is shown only on the first press of a session; subsequent rapid presses show the normal recording state. The initializing state is also reflected in the accessible label and the \`aria-busy\` attribute.

### Architecture

<!-- lore:019fd6b4-645f-74f1-bfcb-0461cce49f1d -->
* **"Transcriptions" tab is history.tsx — dual legacy+server dismiss pattern**: The UI's "Transcriptions" tab is implemented in apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/history.tsx (there is no transcriptions.tsx). Its dismissible tutorial hero (TutorialDemo + X button, history.tsx:576-587) uses a dual-write dismiss pattern: legacy \`usePersistentState<"0"|"1">("today.heroDismissed")\` (localStorage) OR-combined with server-backed \`useDismissible(KNOWN\_NOTIFICATION\_KEYS.TODAY\_TUTORIAL\_HERO)\` — \`heroDismissed = storedHeroDismissed || legacyHeroDismissed === "1"\`. A one-time migration effect (guarded by \`migrationAttemptedRef\`) calls \`persistHeroDismissal()\` to push legacy localStorage dismissals to the server on first load. New dismissible-notification keys are added to \`KNOWN\_NOTIFICATION\_KEYS\` in packages/validations/src/notifications.ts (e.g. TODAY\_TUTORIAL\_HERO = "today.tutorial\_hero").

<!-- lore:019fd228-c409-76a4-98f5-c3780b292008 -->
* **/api/health check is independent of auth/render, not a query gate**: \`checkServerHealth()\` (api.ts:65-80, calls \`hc\<AppType>(base).api.health.$get()\`) is invoked from \`refreshApiBase()\` (api.ts:124), which runs from \`initApiBase()\` at module load inside \`dashboard.tsx:63\` — synchronously before React mounts, independent of \`useCloudAuth\`'s \`loading\` state and unrelated to any React Query hook. \`getClient()\` does not await health. Don't assume \`/api/health\` gates the dashboard or client initialization — it's purely a reachability probe fired once on window load.

<!-- lore:019fd20b-fa22-7740-8cbf-33393867907c -->
* **/api/health check runs at module load, not React-Query-gated**: checkServerHealth() (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/api.ts:65-80) is not a useQuery hook and doesn't gate rendering. It's called from refreshApiBase()/initApiBase() at module load (dashboard.tsx), synchronously before React mounts — fires on window load independent of auth state or component tree. It's unauthenticated and only checks reachability: healthy iff \`res.ok && data.status === 'ok' && data.name === 'freestyle'\`. getClient() does not await health before returning a usable client.

<!-- lore:019fd234-f844-7e40-b246-a096b98e6e23 -->
* **/api/health fetch is not render-gated — fires at module load via initApiBase**: \`checkServerHealth()\` (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/api.ts:65-80) hits unauthenticated \`/api/health\` and is only invoked from \`refreshApiBase()\` (api.ts:124), which runs from \`initApiBase()\` at module load (dashboard.tsx:63) — synchronously before React mounts, independent of the auth \`loading\` gate or any React Query. \`getClient()\` does not await health. Do not assume \`/api/health\` blocks dashboard content — it's a reachability check, unrelated to the render-gating chain (auth loading → HistoryPage spinner). \`checkServerAuth()\` separately verifies the bearer token by hitting \`/api/settings\`.

<!-- lore:019fd20b-6209-7b76-851b-7d4a68a2d3fd -->
* **/api/health is a fire-and-forget reachability probe, not render-gating**: \`checkServerHealth()\` in \`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/api.ts:65-80\` hits unauthenticated \`/api/health\` via \`hc\<AppType>(base).api.health.$get()\` with a 3s \`AbortSignal.timeout\`, only to confirm reachability (\`res.ok && data.status==="ok" && data.name==="freestyle"\`). It's invoked from \`refreshApiBase()\` (api.ts:124), which runs from \`initApiBase()\` at module load (\`dashboard.tsx:63\`) — synchronously before React mounts, independent of the auth \`loading\` gate. \`getClient()\` (api.ts:127-129) does NOT await health, so it never blocks rendering or query fetches. Don't mistake this for a render-blocking preflight check when tracing dashboard startup latency.

<!-- lore:019fd6f7-4d99-74ef-8d92-bec2e38aa93d -->
* **/api/remix/agent proxies to cloud, injects session token server-side**: When the default LLM provider is Freestyle Cloud, apps/server/src/routes/remix/agent.ts becomes a pure streaming proxy to \`${freestyleCloudUrl()}/v2/remix\`: it fetches the desktop's local session token via getSessionToken() and injects it as the Authorization Bearer header server-side — the renderer never holds or sees the cloud token. The UI message stream passes through byte-for-byte (header x-vercel-ai-ui-message-stream: v1); upstream 401→invalidateSession+cloud\_auth\_required, 429→usage\_exceeded+resetsAt, other failures→502 failed. On BYOK, the identical agent loop instead runs in-process via runRemixAgentLocally() + createChatModel(). Because cloud=proxy-only vs BYOK=in-process, any new SERVER tool (with execute) must be added independently in both the cloud repo's routes/v2/remix/tools.ts AND apps/server/src/lib/remix-agent.ts — they are not the same execution path. See also cloudJson() \[\[019fb9e6-a611-7c15-97d5-ec1bf4dbed15]] for the general renderer-never-calls-cloud-directly pattern.

<!-- lore:019fd731-4003-716f-936e-f2af6530f746 -->
* **/onboarding route sits outside LoginGate, uses useCloudAuth directly**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/dashboard.tsx, the \`/onboarding\` route (lines ~115-118) is deliberately placed outside \`\<LoginGate>\` (lines 120-126) so logged-out users can reach onboarding. \`CloudAuthProvider\` still wraps the entire app (line 106), so \`onboarding.tsx\` calls \`useCloudAuth()\` directly to branch its own UI on signed-in vs signed-out state instead of relying on LoginGate's gating (which only applies to the main app routes). Any auth-dependent onboarding behavior must read \`cloudUser\`/\`loading\` from \`useCloudAuth()\` itself — do not assume LoginGate has already gated access.

<!-- lore:019fd236-2a39-7377-8bd7-0f038faeea48 -->
* **/today dashboard mount gating: auth loading + HistoryPage spinner, not fetch chains**: First paint of /today is blocked by two sequential RENDER gates, not serialized fetches. (1) login-gate.tsx:9-17 renders only AuthLoadingFrame while auth-context's \`loading\` is true — AppShell/HistoryPage don't exist in the tree yet, so their useQuery hooks haven't fired. (2) Once auth resolves, history.tsx:549-555 shows a full-page spinner gated on isLoading of the history query, which resolves via Promise.all(\[GET /api/history, GET /api/history/stats]) (history.tsx:381-385) — genuinely parallel. Once mounted, settings/plugins/history/history-stats/history-daily/dismissed-notifications all fire in the same tick with no \`enabled\` dependency on each other. Only /api/usage, /api/org/active, /api/org/list are gated on \`enabled: !!user\`. /api/health is NOT query-gated at all — fired at module load via initApiBase()→refreshApiBase() (dashboard.tsx:63), independent of auth/render. \`placeholderData: keepPreviousData\` (history.tsx:396) only helps warm navigations, not cold first mount.

<!-- lore:019fd204-7eb2-73c8-b599-7546c62f2194 -->
* **/today dashboard: two serialization points delay first paint**: Critical path to first content on the /today (HistoryPage) route: GET /api/auth/status resolves \`reached\` (auth-context.tsx retry loop, up to 15 attempts w/ 1s backoff, can take ~15s on cold/slow server) → LoginGate's \`loading\` clears (login-gate.tsx:9-17, shows only AuthLoadingFrame until then) → AppShell+HistoryPage mount → HistoryPage's full-page spinner (history.tsx:549, gated on isLoading) waits for Promise.all(\[GET /api/history, GET /api/history/stats]) (history.tsx:381-385, already parallel not serial) → spinner clears, feed paints. All other mount-time queries (settings, plugins, history/daily, dismissed-notifications, usage, org/active, org/list) fire in the same tick with genuine parallelism — no serial \`await A; await B\` chains or data-dependent \`enabled\` queries exist anywhere in the mount path except the boolean \`!!user\` gate on usage/org/active/org/list (use-cloud-usage.ts:122, use-profile.ts:176,195). The two bottlenecks are render-gates, not fetch serialization: (1) auth loading gate, (2) HistoryPage's own full-page spinner rather than a skeleton.

<!-- lore:019fafba-bbae-78aa-8679-b0f830f3c774 -->
* **Account disconnect: Better Auth unlinkAccount() chain**: Account disconnect feature uses Better Auth's built-in \`authClient.unlinkAccount({providerId})\` — prevents unlinking the last auth method by default (avoids lockout) unless server sets \`allowUnlinkingAll:true\`; no extra server-side wiring needed for the base capability. Desktop chain: packages/validations/src/auth.ts unlinkAccountSchema → apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts unlinkCloudAccount() (cloudJson POST /auth/unlink-account) → apps/server/src/routes/auth.ts POST /api/auth/unlink-account → use-profile.ts useUnlinkSocial() mutation (invalidates cloud-accounts query) → profile.tsx 'Disconnect' button, disabled+tooltip when it's the user's only sign-in method. Note: Better Auth's account table stores provider tokens encrypted at rest (decrypted on authClient.listAccounts()); linking a provider never overwrites the user's email/emailVerified, so linking cannot rebind account identity.

<!-- lore:019faf56-de93-7445-bf12-3e7fde8eb741 -->
* **Account unlink feature: better-auth unlinkAccount routed through local proxy**: Account-disconnect added across: packages/validations/src/auth.ts (\`unlinkAccountSchema\`), apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts (\`unlinkCloudAccount()\` → POST cloud \`/auth/unlink-account\` via \`cloudJson()\`), apps/server/src/routes/auth.ts (\`POST /unlink-account\`), apps/electron renderer use-profile.ts (\`useUnlinkSocial()\` mutation, invalidates accounts query on success), profile.tsx (Disconnect ghost button, disabled with tooltip when it's the user's only sign-in method). Better Auth's \`authClient.unlinkAccount({providerId})\` prevents unlinking the last linked account by default (\`allowUnlinkingAll\` opts out) — that guard is cloud-side, but the client still must disable the button locally so users don't hit a failed request with no explanation.

<!-- lore:019fd201-eac5-74f4-8ac4-92e13ed50b41 -->
* **api.ts /api/health fetch runs at module load, unrelated to auth/render gating**: \`/api/health\` is NOT a React Query / render-gated fetch. It's called by \`checkServerHealth()\` (api.ts:65-80, unauthenticated \`hc\<AppType>(base).api.health.$get()\` with 3s AbortSignal.timeout, healthy only if \`res.ok && data.status==='ok' && data.name==='freestyle'\`), invoked from \`refreshApiBase()\` (api.ts:124), which runs from \`initApiBase()\` at module load (dashboard.tsx:63) — synchronously before React mounts, independent of the LoginGate auth gate. \`getClient()\` does not await health. Don't confuse this with \`checkServerAuth()\` which verifies the bearer token via \`api.settings.$get()\`.

<!-- lore:019fba10-6454-7f13-af78-cf2c5d0518a6 -->
* **api.ts getClient() is primary local API path; apiFetch() reserved for non-typeable requests**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/api.ts centralizes local embedded server HTTP. getApiBase() resolves configured serverUrl (remote) or getLocalApiBase() (http://127.0.0.1:\<port>, default 4649). getClient() returns a typed Hono RPC client (hc\<AppType>) and is the primary path for all API calls — always prefer it. apiFetch(path, init) is a raw-fetch wrapper intentionally reserved only for requests the typed client can't express (binary WAV upload in app.tsx, fire-and-forget beacons in lib/report-error.ts and lib/analytics.ts) — it additively injects bearer auth headers without clobbering caller-set headers. refreshApiBase() re-reads serverUrl/token via IPC and re-verifies reachability (checkServerHealth/checkServerAuth) after the server:changed broadcast.

<!-- lore:019fd0d2-856a-7392-ac98-84237036673a -->
* **api.ts renderer client: getClient/getApiBase/apiFetch, additive headers**: apps/electron renderer's lib/api.ts exposes three ways to talk to the local server: getClient() returns a fresh Hono RPC client (hc\<AppType>) per call for typed CRUD; apiFetch() is a raw fetch wrapper reserved for binary uploads/streaming/beacons (WAV upload, remix streaming, telemetry beacons) — not a queryFn replacement; checkServerHealth/checkServerAuth are standalone reachability probes, fine outside React Query. Header merging in apiFetch is additive via \`new Headers(init.headers)\` — documented convention: never clobber a header the caller set explicitly (line 48 comment). Any \`getClient().api.X.$get()\` call made outside a queryFn/mutationFn bypasses the TanStack Query cache entirely — treat these as bug candidates unless they're one-off mutation writes ($put/$post/$delete in a handler).

<!-- lore:019fb9e6-a617-7c7d-b9fb-04c6f07c442c -->
* **api.ts: getApiBase/apiFetch/getClient local server fetch layering**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/api.ts is the renderer's local-server fetch layer (no \`endpoint.ts\` exists anywhere in the repo). \`getApiBase()\` resolves configured serverUrl or \`getLocalApiBase()\` (http://127.0.0.1:4649). \`getClient()\` (typed Hono RPC via \`hc\<AppType>\`) is the primary path for nearly all calls. \`apiFetch()\` is intentionally narrow — only for requests the typed client can't express (binary WAV upload, fire-and-forget beacons like telemetry/client-error); it additively injects bearer auth headers without clobbering caller-set headers. \`refreshApiBase()\` re-reads serverUrl/token via IPC and re-verifies reachability; called after \`server:changed\` events.

<!-- lore:019fd2e7-b62b-78ce-a0fc-51b3c3cc7e90 -->
* **apiFetch header merge is additive; getClient() unmemoized per call**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/api.ts (~line 51) documents a hard convention: header merging when building requests must be additive — never clobber a header the caller set explicitly (custom Content-Type/Authorization overrides). Separately, getClient() (api.ts:140) constructs a fresh Hono \`hc\<AppType>()\` client on every call (reads latest server URL/token, but re-parses/rebuilds each time) — called inline ~9x in settings.tsx alone plus tone/history/vocabulary/dictionary pages. Not catastrophic but wasteful on interactive paths. Recommended fix (not yet applied): memoize the client, rebuilding only on the \`server:changed\` IPC event.

<!-- lore:019fd2de-890f-7166-910e-107437c22a38 -->
* **app.tsx (pill controller) is 3976 lines — pill hot-path god-object**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx is the pill overlay window's entire controller (recording, streaming, visualizer RAF loop, remix flow) — 3,976 lines, 44 useMemo/useCallback, ~30 useEffect. It is THE latency-critical hot path (mic capture, transcription submit, hotkey response), rendered by pill.tsx which has no QueryClientProvider \[\[019fd0cb-e476-7236-816a-9f9f22be7cd0]]. Any future refactor split must preserve that isolation and keep the runBars RAF loop's 'never bail out of the rAF chain' invariant (line ~1102: when analyser/freqData missing, still schedules next frame rather than stopping).

<!-- lore:019fcbf5-37bc-724c-a261-be4063ee82fc -->
* **app.tsx pill: dismissPill exit system + RemixChat remix-card coexist**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx's pill component has two independently-built systems merged together (PR #534): main's exit-animation system (\`dismissPill(kind: PillExit)\`, PillExit states delivered/cancelled/quiet, check-mark draw, flatline-for-silence, elapsed label, \`.pill-capsule\[data-exit=...]\` CSS) and the remix-prototype branch's remix-card feature (RemixChat, \`showRemixCard\`, waveform morphing between capsule and remix card via \`riseBy\`/\`--pill-rise\`, \`.pill-surface\`/\`.pill-rise\` CSS). Both must be considered together: \`showCard = showErrorCard || showRemixCard\`, \`active\` includes \`showRemixCard\`, capsule element carries both \`pill-surface\` and \`pill-capsule\` classes. Enter animation uses remix's blur/rise/overshoot (\`.pill-surface\`); exit animation uses main's \`.pill-capsule\[data-exit]\` system (chosen per user: 'remix's summon, keep main's exit'). Any future pill work must account for both systems on the same DOM elements.

<!-- lore:019fcbf1-d901-761d-8c28-203f55575ea7 -->
* **app.tsx pill: dismissPill exit system vs pill-surface summon enter system**: The Electron pill component (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx) has two independently-built animation systems that had to be reconciled when merging remix-prototype into main (PR #534): main's exit system (\`dismissPill(kind: PillExit)\`, \`.pill-capsule\[data-exit="cancelled"|"quiet"|"delivered"]\`, check-mark draw, bar-collapse, CANCELLED\_MS/QUIET\_MS/DELIVERED\_TOTAL\_MS) and remix-prototype's enter/"summon" system (\`.pill-surface\[data-show]\` blur+rise+overshoot via \`--pill-rise\`/\`riseBy()\`, staggered \`.pill-rise\` content, \`.pill-chat-morph\` for the remix chat card). User-confirmed resolution: remix's summon animation wins for enter, main's exit system is kept as-is — enter and exit apply to different CSS states on the same element (\`className="pill-surface pill-capsule"\`). Main's competing enter transform (\`.pill-capsule\` scale(0.88), \`pill-bar-enter\` keyframes, \`ENTER\_MS\`/\`ENTER\_BAR\_DUR\_MS\` constants) was deleted as dead code because it would cascade-override remix's \`--pill-rise\` transform (later CSS rule wins).

<!-- lore:019fcc31-5c1a-7daf-8c6a-0d096970ecd8 -->
* **app.tsx pill: dismissPill/PillExit vs riseBy/pill-rise summon**: Two independent feature branches added competing animation systems to the same pill DOM element in app.tsx: main added an exit-animation system (dismissPill(kind: PillExit), CANCELLED\_MS/QUIET\_MS/DELIVERED\_TOTAL\_MS timers, drawn check-mark, .pill-capsule\[data-exit]); remix-prototype added an enter/summon system (.pill-surface blur+rise+overshoot via riseBy()/--pill-rise) plus the Remix-card feature (RemixChat, showRemixCard, .pill-chat-morph). * **app.tsx pill: remix .pill-surface enter + main .pill-capsule exit coexist**: The dictation pill in apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx has two separately-authored animation systems merged (PR #534, Aug 2026): remix-prototype's \`.pill-surface\` (blur+rise+overshoot 'summon' enter via \`riseBy()\`/\`--pill-rise\` CSS var) is canonical for ENTER; main's \`.pill-capsule\[data-exit="cancelled"|"quiet"|"delivered"]\` (dismissPill/PillExit, check-mark draw, bar-collapse keyframes) is canonical for EXIT. Both classes are applied to the same DOM element. Rejected: main's \`.pill-capsule\` enter transform (scale 0.88) and \`pill-bar-enter\` keyframes were deleted because, being later in the stylesheet, they'd override remix's \`.pill-surface\` enter transform via cascade, making the two enter animations visually fight. <!-- lore:019fd766-f2be-722d-839f-3078417fb25e -->
* **applyFinalRewrites decouples dictionary+afterCleanup from postProcess**: apps/server/src/lib/post-process.ts's applyFinalRewrites() (dictionary replacement + afterCleanup plugin hook) was pulled out of postProcess() because Freestyle Cloud's combined STT+cleanup path never calls postProcess (cleanup happens server-side on the cloud) — so dictionary replacements and the afterCleanup hook silently never ran for cloud-combined dictations, even though they're supposed to run on every dictation regardless of where cleanup happened. Fix: applyFinalRewrites is now called explicitly by every path (local LLM cleanup, cloud combined, no-cleanup) so behavior stays consistent. Dictionary replacement is skipped for empty text, but afterCleanup always fires so plugins observe a uniform lifecycle. Any new dictation path must call applyFinalRewrites directly instead of assuming postProcess covers it.

<!-- lore:019f227e-a37b-76e3-9b7d-f2696e5b1de3 -->
* **apps/docs: Mintlify docs site — excluded from default turbo tasks, knip, and CI build checks**: apps/docs: Mintlify docs site — excluded from default turbo tasks, knip, and CI build checks. \`knip.jsonc\` excludes it as a standalone Mintlify project, not a pnpm workspace member. \`pnpm dev\`/\`pnpm build\` use \`--filter=!docs\`. Separate \`dev:docs\` script: \`turbo dev --filter=docs\`. \`.github/workflows/build.yml\` bypass pattern includes \`apps/docs/\*\`. Logo: SVG files at \`apps/docs/logo/freestyle-light.svg\` and \`apps/docs/logo/freestyle-dark.svg\`. Navbar: Discord link uses \`type: 'discord'\`, GitHub uses \`type: 'github'\`. No Download button. DISTINCT from root \`specs/\` folder (internal engineering specs/audits, renamed from \`docs/\` in commit \`b4b8d13\`). \`AGENTS.md\` documents this distinction; \`CLAUDE.md\` at repo root is a pointer/reference file to \`AGENTS.md\`.

<!-- lore:019fb9e7-97d1-7fd6-b624-df2ec25ba444 -->
* **apps/electron api.ts: apiFetch() vs getClient() for local server calls**: Local server fetch layer lives in apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/api.ts (no file named endpoint.ts exists anywhere in repo). getApiBase() resolves configured serverUrl or falls back to getLocalApiBase() (http://127.0.0.1:\<port>, default 4649). getClient() returns a typed Hono RPC client (hc\<AppType>) and is the PRIMARY path for local API calls. apiFetch(path, init) is a raw-fetch wrapper intentionally reserved only for requests the typed client can't express (binary WAV upload, fire-and-forget beacons like /api/client-error, /api/telemetry) — it adds headers additively, never clobbering caller-set headers. refreshApiBase() re-reads serverUrl/token via IPC after the \`server:changed\` broadcast and re-verifies reachability via checkServerHealth().

<!-- lore:019fb9fb-b564-7107-ae33-a2b2c1bed934 -->
* **apps/electron api.ts: local server fetch helper structure**: Local-server equivalent of cloud's freestyleCloudUrl()/cloudJson() lives in apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/api.ts (129 lines), not a file named endpoint.ts (none exists in repo). Key exports: getLocalApiBase() (http://127.0.0.1:\<port>, default 4649), getApiBase() (configured serverUrl || local), getServerToken(), isRemoteServer(), apiFetch(path, init) (raw fetch wrapper, additive headers, JSDoc says it's ONLY for requests the typed client can't express — binary WAV upload, fire-and-forget beacons), checkServerHealth()/checkServerAuth(), refreshApiBase() (re-reads serverUrl/token via IPC after \`server:changed\`, verifies reachability), and getClient() (typed Hono RPC client via \`hc\<AppType>\` — the primary path for everything else). apiFetch consumers: app.tsx (/api/transcribe), report-error.ts, analytics.ts. When adding new server calls from the renderer, prefer getClient() over apiFetch unless it's binary/fire-and-forget.

<!-- lore:019ef818-39a8-7346-bc15-eb09a03bbc2e -->
* **apps/electron env file loading: only .env.local is loaded, .env.example is onboarding template**: \`apps/electron/src/main/index.ts:31-38\` dev-only block calls \`proc.loadEnvFile?.('.env.local')\` — loads ONLY \`.env.local\`, never \`.env\` or \`.env.example\`. \`.gitignore\` rules (lines 60-64): \`.env\`, \`.env.local\`, \`.env.\*.local\` are ignored; \`!.env.example\` is explicitly whitelisted. \`.env.example\` is tracked in git, documented in \`CONTRIBUTING.md:105/115\` as the onboarding template (copy → \`.env.local\`). Do NOT remove \`.env.example\`. Any stray \`apps/electron/.env\` file is gitignored and never loaded — likely created by mistake instead of \`.env.local\`.

<!-- lore:019fba19-04ed-7671-95ca-e6da1e5835ad -->
* **apps/mobile cloud fetch lacks centralized wrapper (no cloudJson equivalent)**: apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/\*.ts (config.ts, preferences.ts, usage.ts, cloud-config.ts, profile.ts) is a separate, parallel cloud-fetch layer from apps/server's freestyle-cloud.ts. Mobile has its own URL resolver \`cloudUrl()\` (config.ts:19, mirrors \`freestyleCloudUrl()\`, same DEFAULT\_CLOUD\_URL fallback) but NO equivalent to the server's private \`cloudJson()\` wrapper \[\[019fb9e6-a611-7c15-97d5-ec1bf4dbed15]] — every mobile cloud call is a raw \`fetch()\` with no shared retry/timeout/error-classification logic. If adding new mobile cloud calls, don't assume centralization exists; either follow the raw-fetch pattern already used or introduce a shared wrapper deliberately (not yet done as of this investigation).

<!-- lore:019fb9e7-9808-730c-9b36-8dfd41dd361e -->
* **apps/mobile cloud fetch layer is separate from server, has no cloudJson equivalent**: apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/\* mirrors apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts conceptually — cloudUrl() (apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/config.ts:19) is mobile's equivalent of freestyleCloudUrl(), same DEFAULT\_CLOUD\_URL fallback ('https://service.freestylevoice.com') — but there is NO shared fetch wrapper analogous to server's cloudJson() \[\[019fb9e6-a611-7c15-97d5-ec1bf4dbed15]]. Each mobile file (preferences.ts, usage.ts, cloud-config.ts, profile.ts) calls raw fetch() directly against \`${cloudUrl()}/...\`. This means cloudJson's retry-on-stale-socket and error classification (401→auth error, 429→usage error w/ resetsAt) are NOT applied on mobile. If adding/debugging mobile cloud calls, don't assume server-side hardening exists — it doesn't.

<!-- lore:019f2224-433f-78f6-bfca-64ea017a7d75 -->
* **apps/server HTTP API: 55 endpoints across 17 route files, 15 MCP tools**: Full API surface: 55 HTTP endpoints + WebSocket upgrade + MCP catch-all, 15 MCP tools, 17 route files in \`apps/server/src/routes/\`. Key domains: \`/api/settings\`, \`/api/keys\`, \`/api/auth\`, \`/api/models\`, \`/api/history\`, \`/api/transcribe\`, \`/api/formats\`, \`/api/dictionary\`, \`/api/vocabulary\`, \`/api/plugins\`, \`/api/health\` (always auth-exempt). \`GET /\` returns \`"Freestyle API"\`. \`POST /api/telemetry\` relays renderer events. \`POST /api/client-error\` logs renderer crashes. MCP mounted at \`/mcp\`, stream at \`/stream\`. Full inventory documented in \`apps/docs/api-reference.mdx\`.

<!-- lore:019f6a3c-4c57-7bb8-b8a5-fcd40f7edeb8 -->
* **apps/server index.ts: TIMEOUT\_PREFIXES, pluginMiddlewareDispatcher, and request pipeline**: \`REQUEST\_TIMEOUT\_MS = 30\_000\`. \`TIMEOUT\_PREFIXES\` = 8 prefixes: \`/api/settings\`, \`/api/keys\`, \`/api/dictionary\`, \`/api/vocabulary\`, \`/api/history\`, \`/api/models\`, \`/api/plugins\`, \`/api/usage\`. Transcription, post-process, model downloads, and auth device-flow poll are intentionally excluded. \`pluginMiddlewareDispatcher\` (line 67-78): reads live registry on every request via \`plugins().collectMiddleware()\` — runtime \`reloadServerPlugins()\` takes effect immediately without reconstructing the app. Middleware stack: \`trustedOriginMiddleware\` → \`pluginApiGuard\` → \`cors()\` → \`requestId()\` → \`logger\` → per-prefix \`timeout()\` → \`pluginMiddlewareDispatcher\` → routes.

<!-- lore:019ef818-39a6-7919-b8ee-1d465f3e7bb6 -->
* **apps/server uses process.env (not c.env) — it is a Node/Hono app, not a Cloudflare Worker**: \`apps/server\` is a Node-based Hono app embedded in Electron (port 4649). All config is via \`process.env\`. \`c.env\` (Hono's Cloudflare Workers bindings pattern) appears zero times — it belongs to the separate \`cloud\` repo Worker only. Trap: Hono is used in both places, so \`c.env\` looks applicable. Fix: never use \`c.env\` in \`apps/server\`; always use \`process.env\`. Entry point: \`apps/server/src/index.ts\`; default export is \`createApp\` factory (NOT an app instance). \`startServer()\` initializes plugins then calls \`createApp(pluginMiddleware)\`. Env vars: PORT, HOST, FREESTYLE\_AUTH\_TOKEN, FREESTYLE\_DB\_PATH, FREESTYLE\_ENV, FREESTYLE\_ANALYTICS\_DEV, DO\_NOT\_TRACK, FREESTYLE\_MLX\_ASR\_\*, HF\_HOME, FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL.

<!-- lore:019f5b15-7bd7-76e7-a6d0-2d4e924772bf -->
* **ASR server shutdown: SIGTERM+SIGKILL fallback, process.once('exit') best-effort, both awaited async**: Whisper server (apps/server/src/lib/whisper/server.ts): stopServer() sends SIGTERM, then SIGKILL after 5000ms. process.once('exit') calls stopServerOnExit() (best-effort). scheduleUnload() skips if activeUses>0 or startPromise pending. MLX-ASR server (apps/server/src/lib/mlx-asr/server.ts): graceful shutdown (lines 551–563) writes JSON {type:'shutdown'} to proc.stdin, calls proc.stdin.end(), then proc.kill(SIGTERM). SIGKILL fallback at lines 309 and 539. process.once('exit') calls stopWorkerOnExit() (lines 60–73) — sends shutdown JSON + SIGTERM, both try/catch swallowed (best-effort only). Neither stop is synchronously awaited on quit — both are best-effort fire-and-forget from the exit handler.

<!-- lore:019fd6c6-ce17-7e01-aed0-424510c19154 -->
* **beforeCleanup hook is the plugin seam for overriding tone/destination/prompt**: apps/server/src/lib/post-process.ts wires plugins().run("beforeCleanup", {text, appContext, destination}, {system:\[], destination}, api) immediately before buildRewritePrompt(). Plugins can: push extra system\[] fragments, override the resolved destination, replace the assembled prompt outright (promptHook.prompt ?? prompt), or set skip:true to bypass cleanup entirely. If skip is set OR api.control.state !== "running" (plugin called consume()/abort()), cleanedText falls back to normalizedRawText — deliberate, since the dictation is already terminal at that point and spending an LLM call would be wasted. This is the correct integration point for a plugin/agent that needs to inject or override tone config dynamically, rather than reimplementing prompt assembly from scratch.

<!-- lore:019fd75f-1ecb-7a5d-acc1-db973661d81d -->
* **buildRewritePrompt pipeline: prompt-config.ts cloud override with bundled fallback**: Cleanup prompt assembly chain: \`getEffectiveCleanupTones()\` (apps/server/src/lib/post-process.ts) batch-reads settings → \`getRewritePromptContext(appContext, assignments)\` (rewrite-context.ts) resolves \`destination\` (user app-assignment > built-in app-name list > pattern substring match > 'overall' default) and \`personalSurface\` (e.g. 'discord') → \`buildRewritePrompt()\` (editor/prompts.ts) assembles \`{system, prompt}\` from base preset + destination tone block + language block. Content source of truth is \`CleanupPromptConfig\` in editor/prompt-config.ts: resolution order is fresh-cloud (\`GET {freestyleCloudUrl}/v2/config\`, 6h TTL) → stale cached cloud copy → \`BUNDLED\_CLEANUP\_PROMPT\_CONFIG\` fallback. \`isValidConfig()\` defensively checks shape before accepting cloud data — explicit design goal: 'a malformed or newer-shaped cloud response can never degrade cleanup / break prompt assembly'.

<!-- lore:019fd760-ac49-701b-9fcb-b81c4c2dfb5e -->
* **Cleanup config settings keys & getEffectiveCleanupTones batched read**: Tone/cleanup config splits across 3 packages/validations modules: settings.ts (cleanupIntensitySchema: low|medium|high|custom, default medium; cleanupCustomPromptSchema max 20000 chars), cleanup-tones.ts (per-destination tone enums for personal/work/email/overall, all default 'off', plus cleanupAppAssignmentSchema app→destination routing, max 200 entries), cleanup-presets.ts (LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH prompt bodies; 'custom' has no fixed body — UI seeds it from the current preset on switch). Keys mapped in apps/electron/src/shared/settings-keys.ts SETTINGS\_KEYS (llmCleanup, cleanupIntensity, cleanupCustomPrompt, cleanupPersonalTone/WorkTone/EmailTone/OverallTone, cleanupAppAssignments), stored in SQLite \`settings\` table. Server reads all 6 cleanup keys in one batched call: apps/server/src/lib/post-process.ts getEffectiveCleanupTones() via readSettings(\[...]) — don't read them individually.

<!-- lore:019fd747-9284-7825-8b60-af531c0d2440 -->
* **Cleanup config: intensity vs per-destination tone axes**: Cleanup config has two independent axes, each with its own storage/schema. (1) Intensity: low|medium|high|custom (packages/validations/src/settings.ts cleanupIntensitySchema, DEFAULT="medium") — has NO "off" value; enable/disable of cleanup entirely is a separate setting \`llm\_cleanup\` on the Models page. Preset bodies live in packages/validations/src/cleanup-presets.ts (LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH); "custom" seeds from the current preset text in the UI. (2) Per-destination tone (personal/work/email/everythingElse, packages/validations/src/cleanup-tones.ts) — each DOES have an "off" value (default), independent of intensity. All persisted as SQLite \`settings\` rows via SETTINGS\_KEYS (apps/electron/src/shared/settings-keys.ts): cleanup\_intensity, cleanup\_custom\_prompt, cleanup\_personal\_tone/work\_tone/email\_tone/overall\_tone, cleanup\_app\_assignments, llm\_cleanup. \`getEffectiveCleanupTones()\` (apps/server/src/lib/post-process.ts) does one batched readSettings() for all six.

<!-- lore:019fd6f1-c398-7381-be65-ca8302e016f1 -->
* **Cleanup destination routing precedence (rewrite-context.ts)**: getRewritePromptContext() (apps/server/src/lib/editor/rewrite-context.ts) resolves destination in strict precedence: (1) user cleanupAppAssignments — app-kind exact match beats site-kind substring match; (2) built-in exact app-name match against routing.emailAppNames/workAppNames/personalAppNames; (3) substring pattern match against emailPatterns/workPatterns/personalPatterns; (4) fallback 'overall'. buildRewritePrompt() (apps/server/src/lib/editor/prompts.ts) then composes system = base preset (resolveBaseCleanupPrompt: 'custom' with empty text falls back to 'low') + destination tone block (empty string when that destination's tone is 'off') + language block. Routing tables come from getCleanupPromptConfig().routing, not hardcoded.

<!-- lore:019fd716-da59-70e6-8721-48fd70e66561 -->
* **Cleanup intensity has no 'off'; per-destination tones do**: cleanupIntensitySchema (packages/validations/src/settings.ts) is \`low|medium|high|custom\` — no 'off' value; base cleanup on/off lives separately on the Models page via \`llm\_cleanup\` setting. In contrast, the four per-destination tone schemas in cleanup-tones.ts (personal/work/email/overall) each include \`off\` as a valid value, defaulting to it. \`areAllCleanupTonesOff()\` checks only the 4 tone fields (not intensity) and gates whether app-context capture is needed (\`needsAppContextForCleanup()\` in post-process.ts = llm\_cleanup enabled AND not all tones off). Don't conflate the two enums when reasoning about 'cleanup is disabled' — intensity=custom with an empty prompt still runs cleanup; only \`llm\_cleanup=false\` or all-tones-off states skip work.

<!-- lore:019fd750-2f1b-76a2-b799-280a782493c0 -->
* **Cleanup prompt assembly chain: post-process.ts → rewrite-context.ts → prompts.ts → prompt-config.ts**: Server prompt assembly: apps/server/src/lib/post-process.ts getEffectiveCleanupTones() does one batched readSettings() of all 6 cleanup keys → apps/server/src/lib/editor/rewrite-context.ts getRewritePromptContext(appContext, assignments) resolves {destination, personalSurface} with precedence: user cleanup\_app\_assignments (app-kind exact match wins over site substring) > built-in app-name match > substring pattern match > "overall" fallback → apps/server/src/lib/editor/prompts.ts buildRewritePrompt() assembles {system, prompt} = base preset + destination tone block (empty string when that destination's tone is "off") + language block. Content source of truth is apps/server/src/lib/editor/prompt-config.ts's bundled defaults, overridable by Freestyle Cloud's GET /v2/config (6h TTL, fire-and-forget refresh). Design invariant: a malformed/newer-shaped cloud response can never degrade cleanup — isValidConfig() gates acceptance; falls back to last-fetched-good copy, then bundled defaults, never throws.

<!-- lore:019fd70e-bf88-765f-acfd-5e42413d7f0f -->
* **Cleanup prompt assembly: getEffectiveCleanupTones→getRewritePromptContext→buildRewritePrompt**: Server cleanup prompt assembly (apps/server/src/lib/post-process.ts) is 3 stages: getEffectiveCleanupTones() batches all cleanup\_\* settings from SQLite in one readSettings() call; getRewritePromptContext(appContext, assignments) (rewrite-context.ts) resolves destination (user app-assignment > built-in app-name list > pattern substring match > "overall") and personalSurface (discord detection); buildRewritePrompt() (prompts.ts) assembles {system, prompt} from base preset (cleanup-presets.ts LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH) + destination tone block (empty string if tone="off") + language block. Content tables (routing lists, tone-block text, preset bodies) live in prompt-config.ts, overridable by Freestyle Cloud's GET /v2/config (body.prompts, 6h TTL); resolution order is fresh cloud > stale cloud > bundled. Invariant: isValidConfig() rejects malformed cloud shapes before acceptance so "cloud config shape can never break prompt assembly."

<!-- lore:019fd6c5-a30f-7cb1-9665-cc96accc5885 -->
* **cleanup\_intensity has no 'off' — llm\_cleanup setting toggles cleanup on/off**: cleanupIntensitySchema (packages/validations/src/settings.ts) = 'low'|'medium'|'high'|'custom' — there is no 'off' value. Whether cleanup runs at all is controlled by the separate llm\_cleanup setting on the Models page, not by intensity. In contrast, the four per-destination tone schemas (cleanup-tones.ts: personal/work/email/overall) DO each include 'off' (all default to 'off'), meaning destination-specific styling is skipped while the base intensity preset still runs. Don't conflate the two — 'custom' intensity has no fixed preset body; the UI seeds the custom-prompt field from the currently active preset when switching to Custom (tone.tsx).

<!-- lore:019fd765-5f50-7d88-abb3-a3c345e76fed -->
* **cleanup\_intensity has no 'off' — llm\_cleanup toggle vs per-destination tone 'off'**: cleanup\_intensity (packages/validations/src/settings.ts) is enum low|medium|high|custom only — no "off" value. Whether cleanup runs at all is the separate boolean setting llm\_cleanup (Models page). This differs from the 4 per-destination tone settings (personal/work/email/overall, packages/validations/src/cleanup-tones.ts) which each DO have "off", meaning: base preset still runs, but no destination-specific tone/structure block is applied (buildDestinationToneBlock returns ""). Don't conflate: llm\_cleanup=false disables cleanup entirely; tone="off" only disables per-destination styling on top of cleanup.

Per-destination tones (personal/work/email/overall, packages/validations/src/cleanup-tones.ts) each include an 'off' value meaning: base preset still runs but no destination tone/structure block layers on top. <!-- lore:019fd0d6-d655-7470-8c57-ba46ae98156e -->
* **cleanup-app-context.ts caches needsAppContextForCleanup, misses cleanupAppAssignments**: Pill's startRecording hot path replaced synchronous GET /api/settings with getNeedsAppContextForCleanup() (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/cleanup-app-context.ts) — a module-level cache primed at pill mount via applyNeedsAppContextForCleanup(settings), kept fresh via IPC event settings:cleanup-context-changed (sent from tone.tsx saveSetting and use-models.ts setCleanup, relayed in main/index.ts). Cache derives 'needs frontmost app' from llm\_cleanup + the 4 cleanup tone keys (personalTone/workTone/emailTone/overallTone) via areAllCleanupTonesOff — matches tone.tsx's CLEANUP\_CONTEXT\_KEYS exactly. Gotcha: neither the cache nor CLEANUP\_CONTEXT\_KEYS considers cleanupAppAssignments (per-app/site tone overrides) — if all 4 base tones are 'off' but the user has app assignments configured, areAllCleanupTonesOff() returns true, frontmost-app capture is skipped, and app assignments can never fire. Pre-existing behavior, not introduced by this cache; unconfirmed whether intentional.

<!-- lore:019fd0e6-f0ae-79f8-a8e5-4ba0d5084add -->
* **cleanup-app-context.ts: fail-open cache, IPC invalidation, appAssignments gap**: Pill's getNeedsAppContextForCleanup() (apps/electron/.../lib/cleanup-app-context.ts) returns cachedNeedsAppContext ?? true — fails open until primed, so a cold-start recording does one extra frontmost-app capture but never silently skips cleanup routing. Cache is primed async at pill mount via a GET /api/settings fetch (not synchronous — small race, deemed safe) and invalidated via IPC event settings:cleanup-context-changed, fired by every writer of llm\_cleanup + the 4 cleanup tone keys (tone.tsx saveSetting, use-models.ts setCleanup — confirmed fires on both enable and disable). Known gap: applyNeedsAppContextForCleanup only inspects the 4 base tones via areAllCleanupTonesOff(); it never reads cleanupAppAssignments (per-app/site tone overrides). If all 4 base tones are 'off' but app assignments exist, frontmost-app capture is skipped and those overrides can never fire. Pre-existing behavior, not a regression from this changeset — flagged for confirmation, not yet fixed.

<!-- lore:019fd0d9-70b8-772f-8691-3b6a09a239f2 -->
* **cleanup-app-context.ts: module cache + settings:cleanup-context-changed IPC**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/cleanup-app-context.ts implements the pill's own in-memory cache (predicted by \[\[019fd0cb-e476-7236-816a-9f9f22be7cd0]] since the pill renderer has no QueryClientProvider). \`getNeedsAppContextForCleanup()\` synchronously reads a module-level cache (fail-open default \`true\` until primed) so app.tsx's startRecording hot path no longer does a per-recording \`GET /api/settings\`. \`applyNeedsAppContextForCleanup(settings)\` derives the cached bool from \`llm\_cleanup\` + the 4 tone settings (personal/work/email/overall), primed once at pill mount via an async settings fetch. Freshness comes from a dedicated IPC event \`settings:cleanup-context-changed\`, fired from tone.tsx's \`saveSetting\` and use-models.ts's \`setCleanup\` (on both enable AND disable), relayed through main/index.ts, received by app.tsx's \`onCleanupContextChanged\`. Cold-cache race (recording before mount-prime resolves) causes one unnecessary frontmost-app capture — deemed safe, not a correctness bug.

<!-- lore:019fd6e6-ad88-7ebb-a36b-8ea7966eb14c -->
* **Cleanup/tone config read path: settings → prompt-config → buildRewritePrompt**: Tone UI (apps/electron/.../pages/tone.tsx) writes SQLite settings via PUT /api/settings/:key using keys from SETTINGS\_KEYS (apps/electron/src/shared/settings-keys.ts): cleanup\_intensity, cleanup\_custom\_prompt, cleanup\_personal\_tone, cleanup\_work\_tone, cleanup\_email\_tone, cleanup\_overall\_tone, cleanup\_app\_assignments, llm\_cleanup. Server batches all 6 cleanup keys in post-process.ts's getEffectiveCleanupTones(). Destination is resolved by getRewritePromptContext() (rewrite-context.ts): user app-assignments first, then built-in app-name lists, then substring patterns, else "overall". buildRewritePrompt() (prompts.ts) assembles {system, prompt} from base preset (low/medium/high/custom, cleanup-presets.ts) + destination tone block (empty if tone="off") + language block, content sourced from prompt-config.ts. Plugins intercept via the beforeCleanup hook (before assembly) and afterCleanup (after). For a plugin/agent to read this config: capture ctx.settings in setup(ctx), then call ctx.settings.get("cleanup\_intensity" | etc.) inside a hook.

<!-- lore:019fd6c6-ce09-755e-96ea-2fca70b70ffb -->

<!-- lore:019fd722-768a-7c6f-be48-d4cedcfd65b0 -->
* **Cleanup/tone settings: storage keys and schema map**: Tone/cleanup settings are a subset of the SQLite \`settings\` table (layer A, \[\[019fb9c9-7283-720c-9120-a8c1c8d415f3]]). Keys (apps/electron/src/shared/settings-keys.ts SETTINGS\_KEYS): llm\_cleanup (on/off toggle, separate from intensity), cleanup\_intensity (low|medium|high|custom — no 'off'), cleanup\_custom\_prompt (max 20000 chars), cleanup\_personal\_tone/work\_tone/email\_tone/overall\_tone (each has 'off'), cleanup\_app\_assignments (JSON array, max 200, match field lowercased+trimmed for app/site routing). <!-- lore:019fd737-171d-7ab2-a99f-3d082656f932 -->
* **cleanupIntensitySchema has no 'off' — llm\_cleanup is separate enable toggle**: packages/validations cleanupIntensitySchema = low|medium|high|custom — deliberately NO "off" value. Enabling/disabling LLM cleanup entirely is a separate setting, \`llm\_cleanup\` (set on the Models page), not part of intensity. By contrast, per-destination tone schemas (personal/work/email/overall in cleanup-tones.ts) each DO include "off", meaning "run the base intensity preset but skip the destination-specific tone block" (see buildDestinationToneBlock returning "" when tone==="off"). Don't conflate the two: areAllCleanupTonesOff() only checks the 4 tone fields and is used to decide whether app-context capture is needed — it says nothing about whether cleanup itself (llm\_cleanup) is on.

<!-- lore:019fc2c4-89f9-7647-99f5-8e975015586f -->
* **Cloud member\_preferences is source of truth (payload ?? stored ?? default)**: Freestyle Cloud's v2 transcribe/post-process routes AND the streaming Durable Object (stt-session.ts) all read the stored \`member\_preferences\` row (per member, via resolveUsageOwnerId) before processing, then merge via \`resolvePreferences()\`: payload field wins when present, else stored value, else built-in default. Applies to intensity, customPrompt, tones, appAssignments, languages, vocabulary. \`systemFragments\`, \`appContext\`, \`skipPostProcess\` are NEVER stored — always payload-only, no stored fallback. This overturns older lore claiming 'v2 is payload-only, no DB read' — that was true only before the Jul 30 2026 cloud change making member\_preferences authoritative. Fails open (null prefs) to built-in defaults, not an error.

<!-- lore:019faf89-ebff-7924-9ae2-03cf0c2a4528 -->
* **Cloud OAuth token confined to apps/server SQLite sessions table**: Two distinct token systems: (1) Cloud Session Token (OAuth access\_token/refresh\_token) — stored in SQLite \`sessions\` table via apps/server/src/lib/sessions.ts, managed entirely by the embedded server process, NEVER serialized into any HTTP response or IPC channel to the renderer. GET /api/auth/status returns only \`{authenticated, user}\` (id/email/name/image) — getSessionUser() strips token fields. (2) Server bearer Token — stored in settings.json, deliberately exposed to renderer via \`server:token\` IPC (see \[\[019f6be5-ccf9-7839-94c0-4ba8ecc3693e]]). This asymmetry is intentional security-by-design: the cloud OAuth token would let a compromised plugin/XSS in the renderer exfiltrate the user's cloud identity, so all cloud calls (auth, usage, billing, org, transcription) are proxied server-side via cloudJson()/better-auth client with Authorization: Bearer injected from SQLite. No cookies are used anywhere — disableDefaultFetchPlugins:true on the better-auth client.

<!-- lore:019faf91-ce07-7e39-a8f5-788aaef1692b -->
* **Cloud OAuth token never exposed to renderer — server-side SQLite sessions**: Freestyle Cloud OAuth token (access\_token+refresh\_token) is stored server-side in apps/server's SQLite \`sessions\` table (single row, id=1) and NEVER included in any HTTP response to the renderer. Renderer only gets \`{authenticated, user:{id,email,name,image}}\` via GET /api/auth/status. All cloud calls (sign-out, accounts, link/unlink, usage, billing, org) happen server-side via cloudJson()/better-auth client with the token injected as a Bearer header — renderer never talks to Freestyle Cloud directly. This is deliberate security-by-design: exposing the token to renderer (like \`serverToken\` is) would let a compromised plugin or XSS exfiltrate it. Rejected alternative: expose cloud token via IPC like serverToken and call cloud directly from renderer — rejected for security downgrade, required CORS config on cloud side, and split token-refresh complexity across renderer+server. See also \[\[019f6be5-ccf9-7839-94c0-4ba8ecc3693e]].

<!-- lore:019fc2c7-f09b-775b-a268-04664b9796c3 -->
* **Cloud resolvePreferences merge: payload ?? stored ?? default**: In the Freestyle Cloud repo, \`member\_preferences\` (D1) is the source of truth for cleanup/language/vocabulary prefs. \`utils/preferences.ts\` resolvePreferences() merges per-field: payload value wins if present, else falls back to the stored row, else built-in default. Applies uniformly to /v2/transcribe, /v2/post-process, and the streaming Durable Object (stt-session.ts, read before the Soniox handshake). Fields NEVER stored/no fallback: systemFragments, appContext, skipPostProcess, translate — these must always be sent inline per-request. To explicitly CLEAR a stored field for one request, the client must send the built-in default value; omitting the field means 'use stored value', not 'clear it'. This overturns stale prior lore claiming v2 endpoints are payload-only with no DB read — verify cloud-repo behavior directly rather than trusting old lore.

<!-- lore:019fb9ae-e07d-7fad-8ee2-1c77c149a105 -->
* **Cloud-sync settings schema and conflict resolution**: Schema: allowlist of synced keys — dictionary\_entries, vocabulary\_terms, tone\_preferences (per-destination), cleanup\_tone\_settings, portable\_general\_settings (e.g. locale). Each entry carries a server\_version and client\_version vector-clock style timestamp (last\_modified). Conflict rule: server wins on mismatch, but merge dict/voc by key (add/update/remove) so union semantics are preserved for dictionary+vocabulary. Tombstones for deletes (tombstone record with deleted:true + timestamp) to avoid resurrecting deleted entries. Encryption: values stored as-is in cloud DB; no extra encryption at rest beyond cloud provider.

<!-- lore:019fd72d-6536-7f04-8910-a25ab9093ac7 -->
* **CloudAuthProvider/useCloudAuth/LoginGate auth gating (Electron dashboard)**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/auth-context.tsx defines CloudAuthContext/CloudAuthProvider (wraps whole app at dashboard.tsx:106) and \`useCloudAuth()\` hook. Login state comes from \`refreshInternal()\` calling \`getClient().api.auth.status.$get()\` and setting \`user = data.user ?? null\`; retried up to 15x on mount until server reachable, revalidated on window \`focus\` and every 5 min. \`login-gate.tsx\`'s \`LoginGate\` component reads \`{user, loading}\` from useCloudAuth: loading→AuthLoadingFrame, !user→LoginPage, else→children. All routes except \`/onboarding\` are nested under \`\<LoginGate>\<AppShell/>\</LoginGate>\`. \`LoginPage\` and onboarding's \`CloudStep\` both render the shared \`SignInSplit\`/\`SignInButton\` (components/sign-in-split.tsx) — same sign-in UI, minus onboarding's step machinery. Sign-in is device-code-flow style (poll token endpoint until browser auth completes); sole production path — dev/E2E has a 'Skip for now' escape hatch only.

<!-- lore:019f2e7b-c8d7-796c-acd6-b994c31a9b9d -->
* **cloudJson retry loop: RETRIABLE\_CONNECTION\_CODES, 2 attempts, 150ms delay**: \`cloudJson\` in \`apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts\` retries up to \`CLOUD\_FETCH\_ATTEMPTS=2\` total attempts with \`CLOUD\_RETRY\_DELAY\_MS=150ms\` pause. Retries only on \`isRetriableConnectionError\` (walks \`.cause\` chain; matches codes in \`RETRIABLE\_CONNECTION\_CODES\`: ECONNRESET, ECONNREFUSED, ENOTFOUND, EAI\_AGAIN, EPIPE, UND\_ERR\_CONNECT\_TIMEOUT, UND\_ERR\_SOCKET). Excludes UND\_ERR\_HEADERS\_TIMEOUT, UND\_ERR\_BODY\_TIMEOUT, AbortError, TimeoutError — response-phase errors where the request may already be processing server-side. Stale-socket ECONNRESET is safe to retry for non-idempotent POSTs because the request never reached the server. Generates a fresh \`AbortSignal.timeout(CLOUD\_TRANSCRIBE\_TIMEOUT\_MS)\` per attempt when caller didn't supply a signal. \`network.ts\` keep-alive tuning was explicitly rejected — lowering \`keepAliveMaxTimeout\` adds constant TLS handshake latency to avoid a rare reset.

<!-- lore:019fb9e6-a611-7c15-97d5-ec1bf4dbed15 -->
* **cloudJson() is the private canonical cloud fetch wrapper**: \`cloudJson\<T>()\` (apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts:334-385, not exported) is the central fetch wrapper for Freestyle Cloud calls: builds URL via \`freestyleCloudUrl()\`, attaches bearer token, retries once on stale-socket errors, uses \`AbortSignal.timeout()\`, classifies errors (401→FreestyleCloudAuthError, 429→FreestyleCloudUsageError, other→FreestyleCloudRequestError). ~15 exported \`cloud\*\` functions wrap it. Documented raw-fetch exceptions (still via \`freestyleCloudUrl()\`): \`fetchCloudConfig()\` (unauthenticated /v2/config), \`signOutCloud()\` (best-effort), \`prewarmFreestyleCloudConnection()\` (fire-and-forget TLS prewarm while user speaks), \`plugins.ts\` GET /catalog (unauthenticated plugin registry), \`prompt-config.ts\` cached /v2/config refresh, and the Better Auth device-flow client. Renderer never calls cloud directly — path is renderer → local apps/server → Freestyle Cloud.

<!-- lore:019fd6a5-c0f3-7b57-9165-2fb7e1330144 -->
* **CloudStep/LoginGate share SignInSplit; Continue button only shown when signed in**: sign-in-split.tsx's SignInSplit+SignInButton are shared verbatim between onboarding's CloudStep (first onboarding screen) and login-gate.tsx's LoginPage (post-onboarding auth gate) — same left-column sign-in UI + right-column video-demo carousel. In CloudStep, the "Continue" button (variant="ink", ArrowRight icon) only renders when \`user\` is truthy (already signed in); when signed out there is no Continue button, only "Sign in via browser" and a dev/E2E-only "Skip for now (dev)" ghost button (comment confirms production has no sign-in bypass). Login/auth state is read via useCloudAuth() (auth-context.tsx), backed by getClient().api.auth.status.$get(), polled on mount (retry loop), on window focus, and every 5 minutes. LoginGate (login-gate.tsx) renders children only if \`user\` is set, else shows LoginPage or an AuthLoadingFrame while \`loading\`.

<!-- lore:019efae2-439a-75b0-b41c-7066fb1885bd -->
* **Craft independent release pipelines: per-package .craft.yml with tagPrefix**: Monorepo uses separate \`.craft.yml\` per releasable unit for independent versioning. App: \`/.craft.yml\` (no tagPrefix, no npm). SDK: \`packages/sdk/.craft.yml\` (tagPrefix \`freestyle-voice@\`, releaseBranchPrefix \`release-sdk\`). Audio plugin: \`plugins/audio-transcription/.craft.yml\` (tagPrefix \`plugin-audio-transcription@\`, releaseBranchPrefix \`release-plugin-audio-transcription\`). Craft's \`findConfigFile()\` walks CWD upward — running from a package subdirectory finds that package's config first. \`tagPrefix\` on the \`github\` target prevents git tag collisions across packages. All package pipelines use \`versioning: { policy: manual }\` (explicit semver per release). OIDC trusted publishing requires one-time manual npm publish to bootstrap each package before OIDC can be configured.

<!-- lore:019fd0f2-2c6b-7786-9e5b-34d97b55f8cf -->
* **createQueryClient() defaults: staleTime 1h, refetchOnWindowFocus false, retry 1**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/query.ts (createQueryClient(), ~line 97-107) sets defaultOptions.queries: refetchOnWindowFocus:false (desktop SPA, no focus-refetch needed), staleTime: ONE\_HOUR, retry:1. No gcTime override exists anywhere in the renderer — every query inherits TanStack's 5min default gcTime. Implication: most renderer queries that appear to be 'missing staleTime' are NOT actually under-cached — they inherit the 1h default. Freshness for those comes from explicit invalidateQueries() calls on mutations plus IPC events (e.g. onTranscriptionDone), not staleTime tuning. Don't flag a query missing an explicit staleTime as a bug unless it needs something SHORTER than 1h (e.g. volatile download-status queries, which correctly override with staleTime:0 + refetchInterval).

<!-- lore:019fd6e1-a71b-7820-a533-8d32a933b070 -->
* **Cross-repo file mirroring: freestyle desktop repo vs cloud repo**: Freestyle Cloud's backend lives in a separate repo (freestyle-voice/cloud, ~/dev/freestyle-voice/cloud) from the desktop monorepo (freestyle-voice/freestyle). Several files must be kept byte-for-byte identical between them by hand — no shared package: packages/validations/src/remix.ts (REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS + schemas), packages/validations/src/cleanup-presets.ts (LLM cleanup prompts), and the Remix agent system prompt (desktop: apps/server/src/lib/editor/remix-prompts.ts ↔ cloud: apps/server/src/routes/v2/remix/prompt.ts). Gotcha: the two repos alias the same shared validations source differently — desktop imports \`@freestyle-voice/validations\`, cloud imports \`@freestyle/validations\`; using the wrong scope name in either repo looks like a typo but is actually a cross-repo alias mismatch. Before editing a mirrored file, diff both copies first to confirm they still match, then apply identical edits to both, then typecheck+test both repos.

<!-- lore:019fd736-702f-7e0a-8d22-9d0993a5ade9 -->
* **Cross-repo mirrored files: desktop freestyle repo <-> cloud repo, alias name differs**: Several files must be kept byte-for-byte identical between freestyle-voice/freestyle (desktop) and freestyle-voice/cloud (separate repo, Cloudflare Worker): packages/validations/src/remix.ts (REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS + schemas), packages/validations/src/cleanup-presets.ts (LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH prompts), and the Remix agent system prompt — desktop's apps/server/src/lib/editor/remix-prompts.ts mirrors cloud's apps/server/src/routes/v2/remix/prompt.ts (different filename/location per repo, same content). Both repos consume the shared \`validations\` package, but under different workspace aliases: desktop imports \`@freestyle-voice/validations\`, cloud imports \`@freestyle/validations\` — same source package, different alias name. Any change to client-tool declarations or cleanup/prompt text must be manually ported to both repos; there is no shared package publish step.

<!-- lore:019fd6f1-5a77-71e4-a234-f24dc10348a0 -->
* **Cross-repo mirrored files: remix.ts, cleanup-presets.ts, remix-prompts.ts**: Several files must stay byte-for-byte identical between the desktop repo (freestyle-voice/freestyle) and the Freestyle Cloud repo (freestyle-voice/cloud), since the cloud Worker's /v2/remix route and the desktop BYOK agent loop share one tool-calling wire contract: packages/validations/src/remix.ts (REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS), packages/validations/src/cleanup-presets.ts, and the agent system prompt (desktop: apps/server/src/lib/editor/remix-prompts.ts; cloud: apps/server/src/routes/v2/remix/prompt.ts). Any edit to client-tool declarations or the agent prompt in one repo must be manually ported to the other — there is no shared package. The two repos import the same validations source under different workspace aliases: desktop uses @freestyle-voice/validations, cloud uses @freestyle/validations.

<!-- lore:019fd215-b4e2-78fd-b5d3-d7691c81ed51 -->
* **Dashboard /today double-gated by auth loading + history spinner, not slow fetches**: apps/electron renderer's /today page has two serial render-gates (not fetch chains) blocking first paint: (1) login-gate.tsx:9-17 renders only \<AuthLoadingFrame/> while auth-context.tsx \`loading\` is true — starts true, cleared only after /api/auth/status retry loop succeeds (up to 15 attempts, 1s backoff, so up to ~15s on a slow/cold server). HistoryPage/AppShell don't mount until then. (2) Once mounted, history.tsx:549 shows a full-page spinner gated on Promise.all(\[/api/history, /api/history/stats]) (history.tsx:381-385) — genuinely parallel, not serial. All other mount-time queries (settings, plugins, history/daily, dismissed-notifications, usage, org/active, org/list) fire in the same tick; only \`!!user\` gates usage/org/active/org/list. /api/health is unrelated — fired by initApiBase() at module load, never blocks render.

<!-- lore:019fd209-ac35-7dbc-97c0-1d31855c49a0 -->
* **Dashboard /today first paint: auth render-gate + HistoryPage spinner, not serial fetches**: All 11 dashboard mount-time queries (settings, plugins, history, history/stats, history/daily, dismissed-notifications, usage, org/active, org/list, auth/status, health) fetch in genuine parallel — no serial await chains, no dependent \`enabled\` queries. Perceived latency comes from two RENDER gates, not fetch serialization: (1) \`login-gate.tsx:9-17\` mounts only \`\<AuthLoadingFrame/>\` until \`auth-context.tsx\`'s retry loop (up to 15x, 1s backoff, lines 91-97) resolves \`/api/auth/status\` — nothing in AppShell/HistoryPage subtree exists in the tree until then, so no query fires. (2) After auth resolves, \`history.tsx:549\` shows a full-page spinner gated on \`Promise.all(\[/api/history, /api/history/stats])\` (history.tsx:381-385), no skeleton. \`/api/health\` fires at module load via \`initApiBase()\` (dashboard.tsx:63), independent of auth/render. Only usage/org-active/org-list are \`enabled: !!user\`-gated; settings/plugins/history have no \`enabled\` clause.

<!-- lore:019fd23c-0364-7272-bfcd-d0a348fe85aa -->
* **Dashboard /today first-paint critical path: auth gate + history Promise.all**: Investigation (Aug 2026, no code changed) confirmed: all mount-time queries (settings, plugins, history, history/stats, history/daily, dismissed-notifications, usage, org/active, org/list) fire in parallel — no serial \`await A; await B;\` chains, no data-dependent \`enabled\` queries anywhere. history.tsx:381-385 fetches \`/api/history\` + \`/api/history/stats\` via \`Promise.all\` in one queryFn. Only two things block first content paint of /today: (1) \`login-gate.tsx:9-17\` renders only \`\<AuthLoadingFrame/>\` (a bare drag strip) while \`useCloudAuth().loading\` is true — the entire AppShell/HistoryPage subtree (and its queries) doesn't exist in the React tree until loading clears; (2) \`history.tsx:549-555\` — once mounted, HistoryPage shows a full-page spinner (no skeleton) until the Promise.all resolves. \`/api/health\` is not render-gated — fired independently by \`checkServerHealth\`/\`refreshApiBase\` at module load in dashboard.tsx before React mounts.

<!-- lore:019fd20b-61e2-7802-8d21-27ceee6cdce1 -->
* **Dashboard /today first-paint has two serial gates, not fetch chains**: First content paint of the electron dashboard's /today (HistoryPage) route is delayed by two sequential RENDER gates, not by fetch chaining — all ~11 mount-time queries (settings, plugins, history, history/stats, history/daily, dismissed-notifications, usage, org/active, org/list) fire in parallel with no \`enabled: !!otherQueryData\` dependencies. Gate 1: \`login-gate.tsx:9-17\` renders only \`\<AuthLoadingFrame/>\` (blank 36px strip) while \`auth-context.tsx\` \`loading\` is true — children (AppShell+HistoryPage) don't exist in the React tree yet, so none of their useQuery hooks even mount. Gate 2: after auth resolves, \`history.tsx:549\` still shows a full-page spinner (no skeleton) gated on \`isLoading\` of \`Promise.all(\[GET /api/history, GET /api/history/stats])\` (history.tsx:381-385, fetched together in one queryFn, not two separate awaits). AppShell sidebar chrome renders immediately regardless of plugins/settings query state — only the Outlet content area blocks.

<!-- lore:019fd201-4e48-7bde-be7f-362c7096301a -->
* **Dashboard /today mount critical path: auth gate then HistoryPage spinner gate**: Two sequential render gates block first paint of the /today (History) page in apps/electron renderer, not fetch chains — all mount-time queries fire in parallel once unblocked. Gate 1: \`login-gate.tsx:9-17\` renders only \`AuthLoadingFrame\` while \`useCloudAuth().loading\` is true; \`auth-context.tsx\` loading starts true and only clears after \`/api/auth/status\` succeeds (retry loop, up to 15 attempts, 1s backoff — cold/slow server can add ~15s). No children (AppShell/HistoryPage) exist in the tree until this clears, so settings/plugins/history/history-stats/history-daily/dismissed-notifications queries don't mount yet. Gate 2: once mounted, \`history.tsx:549\` shows a full-page spinner (no skeleton) until \`Promise.all(\[/api/history, /api/history/stats])\` resolves (fetched together in one queryFn, genuinely parallel, not serial). \`/api/usage\`, \`/api/org/active\`, \`/api/org/list\` are the only queries with \`enabled: !!user\`; settings/plugins/history are unconditional. \`/api/health\` runs independently at module load via \`initApiBase()\`, before React mounts — not query-gated at all.

<!-- lore:019fd202-c15f-753e-8fc9-6df89ff6c0a3 -->
* **Dashboard /today mount critical path: two render gates, no serial fetches**: In apps/electron renderer, \`/today\` (HistoryPage) content is blocked by two sequential RENDER gates, not serialized fetches: (1) \`login-gate.tsx:9-17\` renders only \`\<AuthLoadingFrame/>\` until \`useCloudAuth().loading\` clears — \`auth-context.tsx\`'s retry loop awaits \`/api/auth/status\` up to 15x with 1s backoff (~15s worst case on cold/slow server) before \`setLoading(false)\`; (2) once mounted, \`history.tsx:549\` shows a full-page spinner (no skeleton) gated on \`Promise.all(\[/api/history, /api/history/stats])\` (history.tsx:381-385, genuinely parallel, not sequential awaits). All other mount-time queries (settings, plugins, history/daily, dismissed-notifications) run with no \`enabled\` dependency chains. Only \`/api/usage\`, \`/api/org/active\`, \`/api/org/list\` are gated on \`enabled: !!user\` (used only in cloud-profile.tsx, not the critical path). \`/api/health\` fires at module load via \`initApiBase()\`→\`refreshApiBase()\` before React mounts — independent of auth, not query/render-gated.

<!-- lore:019fd244-b900-7ced-b0bf-c6fde021a756 -->
* **Dashboard /today mount fetch graph — auth gate + double paint-block**: Electron dashboard (apps/electron renderer): entire AppShell+HistoryPage subtree is unmounted until auth resolves — login-gate.tsx:9-17 renders only AuthLoadingFrame while \`loading\` is true; auth-context.tsx retries \`/api/auth/status\` up to 15x with 1s backoff (up to ~15s on cold/slow server) before \`setLoading(false)\`. Once mounted, ALL co-located queries (settings, plugins, history, history/stats, history/daily, dismissed-notifications) fire in parallel with no serial awaits or data-dependent \`enabled\` chains — only \`/api/usage\`, \`/api/org/active\`, \`/api/org/list\` are gated by \`enabled: !!user\`. \`/api/history\` + \`/api/history/stats\` resolve together via one \`Promise.all\` queryFn (history.tsx:381-385), not two round trips. \`/api/health\` is independent — fired at module load via \`initApiBase()\`→\`refreshApiBase()\`, not React-Query-gated. Second paint-blocker after auth clears: HistoryPage shows a full-page spinner (history.tsx:549) gated on the history+stats Promise.all, no skeleton/chrome shown — \`placeholderData: keepPreviousData\` only helps subsequent nav, not cold mount.

<!-- lore:019fd228-c3e0-777f-80d4-d42bc31c325b -->
* **Dashboard /today mount fetch graph: two serial gates, else all-parallel**: On /today mount, all 11 endpoints (health, auth/status, usage, org/active, org/list, dismissed-notifications, history, history/stats, settings, history/daily, plugins) fire with genuine parallelism — no serial \`await A; await B\` chains and no \`enabled: !!otherQueryData\` dependent queries exist anywhere in the mount path. The only blockers are two RENDER gates, not fetch gates: (1) \`login-gate.tsx:9-17\` — while \`useCloudAuth().loading\` is true, only \`\<AuthLoadingFrame/>\` renders; AppShell+HistoryPage (and their queries: settings/plugins/history/stats/daily/dismissed-notifications) don't exist in the tree yet. (2) \`history.tsx:549-555\` — even after auth resolves, HistoryPage shows a full-page spinner (no skeleton) until \`Promise.all(\[/api/history, /api/history/stats])\` (history.tsx:381-385) resolves. \`/api/usage\`, \`/api/org/active\`, \`/api/org/list\` are further gated by \`enabled: !!user\` (use-cloud-usage.ts:122, use-profile.ts:176/195); settings/plugins/history have no enabled clause.

<!-- lore:019fd234-f7e9-7ccf-a20c-bf8662434c0f -->
* **Dashboard /today mount-time fetch dependency graph — two serialization points, no dependent queries**: Investigation (apps/electron/src/renderer) of the 11 endpoints firing on dashboard mount confirmed: NO serial \`await A; await B\` chains and NO data-dependent (\`enabled: !!otherQueryData\`) queries exist anywhere. The only dependency is a boolean \`!!user\` gate (usage, org/active, org/list use \`enabled: signedIn\`; settings/plugins/history/history-stats/history-daily/dismissed-notifications are ungated). \`/api/history\` + \`/api/history/stats\` fetch via \`Promise.all\` inside one queryFn (history.tsx ~381-385), not sequential. Two things serialize perceived latency without being real fetch chains: (1) \`login-gate.tsx:9-17\` renders only \`\<AuthLoadingFrame/>\` until \`useCloudAuth().loading\` clears (auth-context.tsx retries \`/api/auth/status\` up to 15x/1s backoff before setLoading(false), can take ~15s cold), which prevents the entire AppShell/HistoryPage subtree — and all its useQuery hooks — from mounting at all; (2) even after auth resolves, \`history.tsx:549\` shows a full-page spinner (no skeleton) gated on \`isLoading\` until the Promise.all resolves. AppShell sidebar chrome itself renders immediately regardless of settings/plugins query state.

<!-- lore:019fd205-9bfa-77d2-8bfc-cd8b3649a585 -->
* **Dashboard /today mount: auth gate + history spinner are the only serialization points**: Investigated (Aug 2026, read-only) apps/electron dashboard /today mount: all 11 fired queries (health, auth/status, usage, org/active, org/list, dismissed-notifications, history, history/stats, settings, history/daily, plugins) run in genuine parallel — no serial await chains, no enabled-dependency between queries. Two render-gates cause perceived latency instead: (1) login-gate.tsx's \`loading\` (from auth-context.tsx) blocks the whole AppShell+HistoryPage subtree from mounting until /api/auth/status resolves — up to ~15s via a 15-attempt/1s-backoff retry loop; (2) HistoryPage shows a full-page spinner (no skeleton) gated on Promise.all(\[/api/history, /api/history/stats]) even after auth resolves. AppShell sidebar renders immediately regardless of plugins/settings state. /api/health is unrelated to render — fired at module load via initApiBase(), independent of auth.

<!-- lore:019fd23f-b3fd-75a5-b029-edc8db0b64e2 -->
* **Dashboard /today mount: auth gate + parallel query dependency graph**: Electron dashboard.tsx → CloudAuthProvider → LoginGate → AppShell → HistoryPage (/today). LoginGate (login-gate.tsx:9-17) renders only \<AuthLoadingFrame/> while auth-context \`loading\` is true — AppShell/HistoryPage don't exist in the React tree yet, so none of their useQuery hooks fire. auth-context.tsx \`loading\` starts true; a retry loop (up to 15 attempts, 1s backoff) awaits refreshInternal() until /api/auth/status responds, then setLoading(false) — a cold/slow server can delay content mount up to ~15s. Once mounted, ALL queries (settings, plugins, history, history/stats via Promise.all, history/daily, dismissed-notifications, usage, org/active, org/list) fire in parallel in the same tick — no serial await chains, no \`enabled: !!otherQueryData\` dependencies anywhere in the mount path. Only \`!!user\`-gated queries are usage, org/active, org/list (via cloud-profile.tsx). /api/health is NOT render-gated — it fires from initApiBase() at module load, independent of auth/React tree.

<!-- lore:019fd210-ed36-7bc6-b270-b41b23fb4e98 -->

* **Dashboard mount queries are genuinely parallel — no serial awaits or query chaining**: Exhaustive audit (Aug 2026) of the ~11 endpoints firing on Electron dashboard mount (/today) found zero serial \`await A; await B;\` chains and zero data-dependent \`enabled: !!fromOtherQuery\` gates anywhere. \`/api/history\` + \`/api/history/stats\` resolve via one \`Promise.all\` inside a single queryFn (history.tsx:381-385), not sequential fetches. \`settings\`/\`plugins\`/\`history\`/\`history/daily\`/\`dismissed-notifications\` fire unconditionally on mount; only \`/api/usage\` (use-cloud-usage.ts:122), \`/api/org/active\`, \`/api/org/list\` (use-profile.ts:195,176) are additionally gated on \`enabled: !!user\`. \`/api/health\` is NOT part of the React Query mount path — it fires from \`initApiBase()\`→\`refreshApiBase()\`→\`checkServerHealth\` at module load (dashboard.tsx:63), independent of auth/render; \`getClient()\` doesn't await it. Any perf proposal to 'parallelize dashboard fetches' is targeting a problem that doesn't exist — the real bottleneck is the two render gates.

User-gated (\`enabled: !!user\` / \`signedIn\`): \`/api/usage\` (use-cloud-usage.ts:122, called as \`useCloudUsage(!!user)\` in cloud-profile.tsx:43,80), \`/api/org/active\` (use-profile.ts:195, \`useActiveOrganization(!!user)\`), \`/api/org/list\` (use-profile.ts:176, \`useListOrganizations(!!user)\`) — all consumed only inside cloud-profile.tsx. NOT user-gated (fire unconditionally once their component mounts): \`settings\` (shell.tsx:213), \`plugins\` (shell.tsx:204), and \`history\`/\`history/stats\`/\`history/daily\`/\`dismissed-notifications\` (history.tsx) — none have an \`enabled\` clause. \`/api/health\` is separate — see \[\[api.ts health fetch entry]].
