* **Remix chatView mount/unmount is React-state-driven, independent of pill window hide/show**: hidePill()/showPill() (apps/electron/src/main/index.ts:1558, 1084) only hide/show the pill's \`mainWindow\` — they never destroy it (\`win.hide()\`/\`.showInactive()\`). RemixChat's mount/unmount is driven entirely by renderer state: \`chatView\` (app.tsx:3032-3039, derived from \`remixView.phase === 'chat'\`) toggling truthy/null, itself downstream of \`remix\`/\`setRemix(null)\` via \`closeRemix()\`→\`endRemix()\`. \`\<RemixChat>\` (app.tsx:3955) has no \`key\` tied to session id, so reopening while still mounted just patches state rather than remounting; only its inner \`\<RemixThread key={thread.threadId}>\` (remix-chat.tsx:181) remounts on a different thread id. Any fix touching 'when does the Remix session reset' must go through this render-state chain, not window visibility — the window being hidden/shown is orthogonal to whether RemixChat is mounted.

<!-- lore:019fd6f1-d9bd-730d-bc71-e81839187099 -->
* **Remix client tools & agent prompt mirrored byte-for-byte to cloud repo**: packages/validations/src/remix.ts (REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS registry) and apps/server/src/lib/editor/remix-prompts.ts (buildRemixAgentSystem / agent standing brief) are each duplicated verbatim into the separate Freestyle Cloud repo (cloud's remix client-tool consumer and routes/v2/remix/prompt.ts respectively) — there is no shared package between desktop and cloud for Remix. Any edit to either file (new tool, changed tool description, prompt wording) must be manually ported to cloud or the cloud path silently drifts (missing tool, stale prompt behavior). Same class of duplication as the mobile/desktop cloud-streaming split \[\[019fc2d6-cd1f-7f8f-b2d3-07aee02b41f6]]. New prompt content that's cloud-only-available (like web\_search/image\_search) must be gated behind a flag analogous to \`hasWebSearch\` (false for BYOK, true for cloud) rather than assumed universally available.

<!-- lore:019fd74a-a87a-79fd-b41f-1aa8fd823de0 -->
* **Remix conversation history lives on dashboard remix.tsx, not the pill**: Remix thread history is server-backed: remix-store.ts exposes listThreads(limit,offset)/getThread(id) over the remix\_threads/remix\_messages tables; routes/remix/thread.ts exposes GET /api/remix/thread/list and GET /api/remix/thread/:id. The UI (HistoryList using useInfiniteQuery, 30/page + Load more; ThreadTranscript read-only view) lives on the Remix dashboard page (apps/electron/.../pages/remix.tsx) — NOT the floating pill (remix-chat.tsx). The dashboard page has full react-router + QueryClientProvider, so 'Settings' is just \<Link to="/settings#remix">; no IPC needed. First attempt built this in the pill and had to be fully reverted — pill has neither router nor QueryClientProvider \[\[019fd0cb-e476-7236-816a-9f9f22be7cd0]].

<!-- lore:019fcea0-efec-7b73-a27f-ec8a0c7e38fe -->
* **Remix is architecturally distinct from dictation — no queue, no plugin pipeline, no audio ducking**: Per code comments in apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx (~1880-1882, ~2118-2119, ~2131): a remix session never enters the transcription queue, never reaches the plugin output pipeline, and its result replaces a text selection directly (not pasted like dictation output). Remix instruction speech (typed/spoken chat instructions) is never pasted anywhere — partials are discarded, streaming output is thrown away, only the final instruction is used. Remix never ducks/restores system audio the way dictation does (no restoreSystemAudioSafely() call needed on entry). apps/electron/src/main/index.ts's ipcMain.handle('remix:paste') deliberately bypasses deliverOutput — no trailing space, no plugin pipeline (see /api/remix route). Treat remix as a parallel, separately-implemented pipeline, not a variant of the dictation flow.

<!-- lore:019fd764-a512-753d-9424-84d6026981f5 -->
* **Remix pill thread lifecycle: mount-driven freshness check, last\_active\_at write points**: RemixChat's mount effect (remix-chat.tsx:106-131, empty dep array — runs once per mount) is the ONLY place that consults server-side thread freshness (remix-store.ts isFresh()/REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS, currently 15min). GET /api/remix/thread never mutates; returns null threadId if stale, never creates a thread. last\_active\_at is bumped ONLY inside saveThreadMessages (remix-store.ts:131-133), called only via POST /api/remix/thread/sync from useChat's onFinish (remix-chat.tsx:441-450) — opening/showing the pill does NOT bump it, only a completed turn does. The pill window (mainWindow) is hidden, not destroyed, by hidePill() (main/index.ts:1558); RemixChat mount/unmount is driven purely by renderer state (\`chatView\` toggling truthy/null, app.tsx:3032-3039), not window lifecycle. \`\<RemixChat>\` has no key prop tied to session id. Related: \[\[019fd763-c750-7ec8-9ac1-9e5f1a47a73e]]

<!-- lore:019fd74d-632e-7ff2-a883-7ea228a4755d -->
* **Remix pill: chatView mount drives session freshness check**: RemixChat mounts/unmounts based on \`chatView\` derived state in app.tsx (from \`remix.phase === "chat"\`), NOT on the Electron pill window showing/hiding — hidePill() only calls mainWindow.hide(), it never destroys RemixChat. The server-side idle-freshness check (isFresh() + REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS, apps/server/src/lib/remix-store.ts:8,45-55) is consulted only inside RemixChat's mount effect (remix-chat.tsx:106-131, empty deps array, runs once per mount) via GET /api/remix/thread. So 'new session after idle' only happens if RemixChat actually unmounts and remounts between events. The session-reuse path (app.tsx onRemixOpenChat, \`patchRemix({phase:"chat"})\` when remixRef.current already exists, ~line 2604) skips the mount effect and thus skips the freshness check entirely.

<!-- lore:019fd748-e401-7e54-abe9-026aadf0d3ee -->
* **Remix pill: RemixChat mount cycle gates server idle-freshness check**: The Remix session-freshness check (GET /api/remix/thread → isFresh() vs REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS, remix-store.ts:45-55) only runs inside RemixChat's mount effect with an empty dep array (remix-chat.tsx:106-131) — once per mount. The pill window itself (mainWindow) is only hidden by hidePill() (win.hide()), never destroyed; RemixChat mounts/unmounts purely from renderer state (chatView going null↔truthy, driven by app.tsx's endRemix()→setRemix(null)). Every dismiss path (Escape, minimize+settle 3s \`MINI\_SETTLED\_DISMISS\_MS\`, minimize+load-fail 7s \`MINI\_IDLE\_DISMISS\_MS\`, explicit close) unmounts RemixChat, so the next \`remix:open-chat\` event remounts it and re-triggers the freshness check. \`last\_active\_at\` is bumped ONLY in \`saveThreadMessages()\` (on turn completion via POST /sync, called from \`useChat.onFinish\`) — opening the pill or a bare GET never bumps it.

<!-- lore:019fd731-f2f5-7b3c-acc9-9c359167c463 -->
* **Remix pill: RemixChat mount tied to chatView truthiness, not window lifecycle**: RemixChat (remix-chat.tsx) mounts only when app.tsx's \`chatView\` state is non-null; \`\<RemixChat>\` (app.tsx:3953-3976) has no \`key\` prop tied to session id, so mount/unmount is driven purely by \`chatView\` toggling truthy/null, not by window destruction. \`hidePill()\` (main/index.ts:1558) only calls \`mainWindow.hide()\` — the pill window is never destroyed. RemixChat's mount effect (remix-chat.tsx:106-131, empty deps array) reruns only when \`chatView\` cycles through null, i.e. after a full close via \`endRemix()\`→\`setRemix(null)\`→320ms \`chatView\`-clear timeout (app.tsx:3007-3039). Minimizing does NOT unmount RemixChat — only close/dismiss paths (Escape, onClose, settled/failed dismiss timers) do.

<!-- lore:019fd739-c07f-7c3c-b70d-ce7ca797bb9c -->
* **Remix routes (transform/agent/thread/runs) + cloud mirroring of tools.ts/prompts.ts**: apps/server/src/routes/remix/index.ts mounts 4 sub-routes: /transform (one-shot edit via remix-transform.ts, records to remix\_runs for Revert, no tools), /agent (tool-using chat loop: BYOK runs in-process via remix-agent.ts streamText+tool()+stepCountIs(16); Freestyle Cloud path proxies to cloud's /v2/remix, injecting server-side session Bearer token so renderer never holds the cloud token), /thread (local SQLite chat history), /runs (write-history powering Revert). Tool registry packages/validations/src/remix.ts (REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS, 13 no-execute tools consumed by renderer's executeTool in remix-chat.tsx) and system prompt apps/server/src/lib/editor/remix-prompts.ts (buildRemixAgentSystem, hasWebSearch flag gates web\_search/image\_search mentions) are BOTH mirrored byte-for-byte into the cloud repo's routes/v2/remix/{tools,prompt}.ts — no shared package exists. Any edit to either file must be manually ported to cloud or cloud silently drifts out of sync.

<!-- lore:019fd761-be5f-74f1-8952-56678c48733d -->
* **Remix routes: /transform, /agent, /thread, /runs under apps/server**: apps/server/src/routes/remix/index.ts mounts 4 sub-routes at /api/remix: /transform (one-shot LLM edit over a selection/preset, no tools, via remix-transform.ts→buildRemixPrompt/buildRemixSystem), /agent (tool-using chat loop, cloud proxy or BYOK, via remix-agent.ts), /thread (pill's chat thread, local SQLite), /runs (one row per document write, powers Revert). SQLite tables (schema.ts:585-627): remix\_threads, remix\_messages (FK thread\_id), remix\_runs (FK thread\_id); store layer is remix-store.ts. apps/mobile has zero Remix code (desktop+cloud only). Companion to \[\[019fd6c7-0be3-749e-af92-34c79cacaa61]].

<!-- lore:019fd6f3-f445-7c05-aa5d-c98267b19a37 -->
* **Remix routes: transform/agent lanes + SQLite tables**: apps/server/src/routes/remix/index.ts mounts 4 sub-routes under /api/remix: /transform (one-shot LLM edit over a selection/preset, no tools, backed by apps/server/src/lib/remix-transform.ts), /agent (tool-using chat loop via AI SDK streamText, apps/server/src/lib/remix-agent.ts, REMIX\_MAX\_STEPS=16 — rationale: primitive tools mean more/smaller steps, a canvas-app edit costs 6-8 calls), /thread (pill's chat thread, local SQLite), /runs (one row per document write, powers Revert). SQLite tables: remix\_threads, remix\_messages, remix\_runs (apps/server/src/lib/schema.ts:585-627), store layer remix-store.ts. On Freestyle Cloud, /api/remix/agent is a streaming proxy to Cloud's /v2/remix — Bearer token injected server-side since the renderer never holds the cloud token. On BYOK, runRemixAgentLocally runs the identical loop in-process via createChatModel(). apps/mobile has zero Remix code — desktop + cloud only.

<!-- lore:019fd74c-4dc3-73a7-bdb5-358ebaef6b38 -->
* **Remix thread freshness — REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS gates session reuse via RemixChat remount**: apps/server/src/lib/remix-store.ts REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS (15min, bumped to 20min per user request) controls whether GET /api/remix/thread returns an existing threadId or null (via isFresh()/getActiveThread(), never mutates). Freshness is only re-checked when \<RemixChat> remounts (its GET-on-mount effect has empty deps, runs once per mount). Hotkey-based session start (beginRemix in app.tsx) always calls endRemix() before creating a new session, so it always remounts and re-checks freshness correctly. Gap: the persistent-bar hover path (onRemixOpenChat in app.tsx) reuses an existing mounted RemixChat via patchRemix() without remounting, so it never re-checks freshness — an expanded chat left open indefinitely never times out. Fix direction: track last-activity client-side and force endRemix()+remount before reuse if idle beyond threshold.

<!-- lore:019fd761-9063-7c8c-9083-45d1a18dbd51 -->
* **Remix thread freshness check is mount-gated (remix-chat.tsx mount effect)**: REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS (remix-store.ts:8, 15min) is enforced via isFresh()/getActiveThread(), but only ever consulted inside RemixChat's mount effect (remix-chat.tsx:106-131, empty dep array — runs once per mount). The pill window (mainWindow) is hidden by hidePill(), never destroyed; RemixChat mounts/unmounts based on renderer state (app.tsx chatView toggling truthy/null via closeRemix→endRemix→setRemix(null)), not window lifecycle. Reopening via app.tsx's onRemixOpenChat (2602-2616) REUSES the existing session (patchRemix, no remount, no freshness check) whenever remixRef.current is already set — e.g. an EXPANDED chat left open indefinitely. So 'new session after N min idle' only actually happens for sessions that self-dismiss via minimize/settle timers first; an indefinitely-open expanded chat bypasses the whole idle mechanism.

<!-- lore:019fd752-ef60-7952-a63d-fbf6b2e179b0 -->
* **Remix thread freshness gate runs only on RemixChat mount effect**: REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS (apps/server/src/lib/remix-store.ts:8) is checked only inside getActiveThread()/isFresh(), reached via GET /api/remix/thread — which RemixChat's mount effect (remix-chat.tsx:106-131, empty deps \`\[]\`) calls exactly once per mount. \`last\_active\_at\` is bumped ONLY by saveThreadMessages() (remix-store.ts:131-133), called from POST /api/remix/thread/sync on useChat's onFinish (remix-chat.tsx:441-450) — opening the pill or a bare GET never bumps it. RemixChat mount/unmount is driven by app.tsx's \`chatView\` state (derived from \`remix\` session going null→non-null), NOT by the pill window's show/hide: \`hidePill()\` (main/index.ts:1558) only hides the window, it never destroys RemixChat. A 'fresh' session check therefore only re-fires when chatView transitions from null to non-null after a full close (\`endRemix\`→\`setRemix(null)\`).

<!-- lore:019fd747-d716-7bd8-ae51-dd3e0e03a15e -->
* **Remix thread freshness: last\_active\_at only bumped on message sync, not open**: Server-side remix thread freshness lives in apps/server/src/lib/remix-store.ts. REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS (20min, was 15) gates isFresh()/getActiveThread(): GET /api/remix/thread never creates/mutates rows, just returns the latest thread if fresh else {threadId:null}. last\_active\_at is written in exactly one place — saveThreadMessages()'s UPDATE inside POST /api/remix/thread/sync — fired only from RemixChat's useChat onFinish (a completed turn). Opening the pill or GET-ing the thread does NOT bump last\_active\_at; staleness is measured from last completed turn, not from window-open time.

<!-- lore:019fd74b-8044-7201-ac82-0bb35ea66d81 -->
* **Remix thread freshness: REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS gates GET, only saveThreadMessages bumps last\_active\_at**: Remix agent thread continuity (apps/server/src/lib/remix-store.ts) is governed by REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS (line 8, 15min as of Aug 2026). \`getActiveThread()\`/\`isFresh()\` (lines 45-55) check \`last\_active\_at\` against this window and never create a thread; \`GET /api/remix/thread\` (routes/remix/thread.ts:33-43) returns \`{threadId:null}\` if stale, else resumes with saved messages. \`last\_active\_at\` is bumped ONLY inside \`saveThreadMessages()\`'s transaction (remix-store.ts:131-133), called from \`POST /api/remix/thread/sync\` which fires only from RemixChat's \`useChat.onFinish\` (remix-chat.tsx:441-450) on turn completion — opening the pill or a bare GET never bumps it. Critically, the freshness check runs ONLY inside RemixChat's mount effect (remix-chat.tsx:106-131, empty deps \`\[]\`) — it re-executes only when RemixChat actually remounts, not on every remix event.

<!-- lore:019fd736-7020-72db-9adb-461d1a062bc6 -->
* **Remix thread history: /api/remix/thread/list + /:id, read-only transcript**: Remix conversation history is exposed via \`GET /api/remix/thread/list\` (paginated, backed by \`listThreads(limit, offset)\` in apps/server/src/lib/remix-store.ts) and \`GET /api/remix/thread/:id\` (backed by \`getThread(id)\`). Consumed by the Remix dashboard page (pages/remix.tsx) via react-query keys \`queryKeys.remixThreads\` / \`queryKeys.remixThread(id)\`. \`HistoryList\` shows preview (first user message text, truncated) + relative time + message count; threads with zero messages are skipped from the list (not labeled "empty"). \`ThreadTranscript\` renders only text parts (\`isTextUIPart\`) — tool-call/tool-step chatter is deliberately omitted for readability. Both endpoints are GET-only; thread creation only happens via \`POST /api/remix/thread/new\` (\`getActiveThread()\` explicitly never creates one).

<!-- lore:019fd734-53ed-726c-9498-9c4e202be67d -->
* **Remix thread idle freshness: REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS only re-checked on RemixChat mount**: apps/server/src/lib/remix-store.ts:8 defines REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS (15min, bumped to 20min). getActiveThread() (remix-store.ts:50-55) never creates a thread — GET /api/remix/thread (thread.ts:33-43) returns null/empty if the latest thread isn't fresh via isFresh(). last\_active\_at is written ONLY by saveThreadMessages (remix-store.ts:131-133), called from POST /api/remix/thread/sync, itself only invoked by useChat's onFinish (remix-chat.tsx:441-450) — opening the pill alone never bumps it. Critically, freshness is checked ONLY inside RemixChat's mount effect (remix-chat.tsx:106-131, empty deps — runs once per mount). The pill window (mainWindow) is hidden via hidePill() (index.ts:1558-1583), never destroyed; RemixChat mount/unmount is a separate, renderer-state-driven mechanism (chatView truthy/null in app.tsx:3032-3039), not tied to window visibility. Closing a session (onClose→closeRemix→endRemix→setRemix(null)) unmounts RemixChat; the next remix:open-chat event remounts it and re-triggers the freshness check.

<!-- lore:019fd758-ba0c-7543-a962-1f7880aff9da -->
* **Remix thread idle timeout: REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS / isFresh / getActiveThread**: Remix pill session persistence uses a single freshness constant \`REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS\` (apps/server/src/lib/remix-store.ts:8, currently 15min). \`isFresh(thread)\` compares \`Date.now()\` against \`last\_active\_at\`; \`getActiveThread()\` returns the latest thread only if fresh, else null — it never creates a thread (GET must not mutate). \`GET /api/remix/thread\` (routes/remix/thread.ts:33-43) returns \`{threadId:null,resumed:false}\` if stale. \`last\_active\_at\` is written in exactly one place: \`saveThreadMessages()\`'s transaction (remix-store.ts:131-133), called only from \`POST /api/remix/thread/sync\`, itself only invoked from RemixChat's \`useChat.onFinish\` (remix-chat.tsx:441-450). Opening the pill (GET, or POST /new) does NOT bump last\_active\_at — only a completed turn does. The freshness check itself only runs inside RemixChat's mount effect (remix-chat.tsx:106-131, empty deps — runs once per mount), so a fresh check only happens when RemixChat actually remounts.

<!-- lore:019fd730-8bc1-7844-a248-5eaf22c1475c -->
* **Remix thread idle-freshness check (REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS, remix-store.ts)**: Remix thread continuity is governed by REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS (remix-store.ts:8, 15min→20min). isFresh() compares Date.now() against thread.last\_active\_at; getActiveThread() returns the latest thread only if fresh, else null — it never creates one. GET /api/remix/thread (thread.ts:33-43) returns {threadId:null} if idle, else resumes with messages. last\_active\_at is written in exactly ONE place: saveThreadMessages() (remix-store.ts:131-133), called only from POST /api/remix/thread/sync, itself only invoked from RemixChat's useChat.onFinish (remix-chat.tsx:441-450) — i.e. only on completed turns, never on mere GET/open. Critically, the freshness check is consulted only inside RemixChat's mount effect (remix-chat.tsx:106-131, empty deps array) — it runs exactly once per mount and never re-checks while the component stays mounted.

<!-- lore:019fd718-61e5-7245-8ea2-2fa63dfdf320 -->
* **Remix thread persistence — no thread-list endpoint exists**: apps/server/src/lib/remix-store.ts (SQLite tables remix\_threads, remix\_messages, remix\_runs) only exposes getActiveThread() (latest thread within REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS=15min freshness window; GET must never mutate — creation is startNewThread()'s job), startNewThread(), getThreadMessages() (capped at MAX\_THREAD\_MESSAGES=40), saveThreadMessages(). Routes at apps/server/src/routes/remix/thread.ts expose only GET /api/remix/thread, POST /api/remix/thread/sync, POST /api/remix/thread/new — there is no GET /api/remix/thread/list or /:id. /api/remix/runs (runs.ts) lists document-write/paste history (powers Revert), is unrelated to chat history, and is unused anywhere in the renderer. purgeExpiredRemixData() only purges by retention, so old threads persist and are queryable — a history feature needs a new listThreads(limit,offset) in remix-store.ts plus new GET /api/remix/thread/list and GET /api/remix/thread/:id endpoints; none of this exists yet.

<!-- lore:019fd70f-7780-7e27-87b4-5a9b8dcddc44 -->
* **Remix Transform lane vs Agent lane; cloud proxy injects Bearer token**: The Remix agent has two lanes under /api/remix (routes/remix/index.ts). Transform (transform.ts→remix-transform.ts): one-shot LLM edit over a selection/preset/spoken instruction — no tools, no loop, logs to remix\_runs for Revert. Agent (agent.ts→remix-agent.ts): tool-using chat loop via streamText+tool(), REMIX\_MAX\_STEPS=16 ('primitive tools mean more, smaller steps: a canvas-app edit costs 6-8 calls'), tools built by remixClientTools() with no execute — AI SDK pauses and streams calls to the renderer. When the selected LLM provider is Freestyle Cloud, /api/remix/agent becomes a streaming proxy to cloud's /v2/remix, injecting the server-side session Bearer token — the renderer never holds the cloud token. On BYOK, runRemixAgentLocally() runs the identical loop in-process via createChatModel(). thread.ts/runs.ts back the pill's local SQLite chat history and Revert log.

<!-- lore:019fcea2-0008-735f-8f96-6c5744502eba -->
* **Remix vs dictation: no shared mode enum, two independent state pairs**: Electron app tracks dictation and remix as two independent state pairs, not a unified mode enum. Main process: two separate NativeKeyListener processes — registerHotkey() (dictation, main/index.ts:4235, has globalShortcut fallback) and registerRemixHotkey() (main/index.ts:3999, no fallback) — are separate OS processes that cannot see each other; booleans hotkeyPressed/remixPressed (main/index.ts:379,391) track press state independently. Renderer (app.tsx): dictation state = state/stateRef (PillState) + pillActiveRef (set true only in startRecording, app.tsx:1482); remix state = remix/remixRef.current (RemixSession|null with phase union), set via setRemix in beginRemix/openRemixChat — never touches pillActiveRef. remixRef !== null and pillActiveRef are NOT mutually exclusive; discriminator is remixRef.current !== null (showRemixCard at app.tsx:2598). On macOS, dictation (bare Fn) and remix (Fn+Control) share a home key.

<!-- lore:019fd059-3335-7614-9c4d-4d3e29a0e11b -->
* **REMIX\_AGENT\_PROMPT mirrored byte-for-byte into cloud repo**: apps/server/src/lib/editor/remix-prompts.ts's buildRemixAgentSystem()/REMIX\_AGENT\_PROMPT is imported identically by the cloud repo's routes/v2/remix/prompt.ts, so BYOK/local and cloud remix-agent runs assemble the exact same system prompt string — not a shared package, a manually-maintained parity contract. Any change to this prompt (e.g. gating web\_search availability per path) must be verified against how the cloud repo consumes the mirrored builder before merging, similar to the independent mobile/desktop cloud split \[\[019fc2d6-cd1f-7f8f-b2d3-07aee02b41f6]]. Forgetting to check the cloud side risks silently breaking cloud parity or shipping a prompt divergence.

<!-- lore:019fd762-e353-7c6e-87b8-06747dbb48b0 -->
* **REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS + remix-prompts.ts require byte-for-byte cloud mirror**: packages/validations/src/remix.ts (REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS) and apps/server/src/lib/editor/remix-prompts.ts (Remix agent system prompt) in the desktop repo must be mirrored byte-for-byte into the cloud repo (freestyle-voice/cloud) at packages/validations/src/remix.ts and apps/server/src/routes/v2/remix/prompt.ts. Tool names are 'the wire contract' shared by the cloud route, local BYOK loop, and renderer's tool executor. Desktop imports the shared package as @freestyle-voice/validations; cloud imports the same source file as @freestyle/validations (different workspace alias, same content). Any new REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS entry or prompt change needs a matching PR in both repos. No cloud executor code is needed for new client tools — they carry no execute(); calls stream down to the desktop renderer regardless of which backend (BYOK or Cloud Worker) runs the agent loop.

<!-- lore:019fd6e0-9a07-7828-b4d5-3328b93bac36 -->
* **REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS execute only in renderer, regardless of BYOK vs Cloud**: REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS (packages/validations/src/remix.ts) declares tools with no \`execute\`. Both the Freestyle Cloud Worker's agent loop and the desktop BYOK loop (remix-agent.ts) pause on these tool calls and stream them down to the renderer's executeTool() (remix-chat.tsx via useChat's onToolCall), which maps each to a window.api.remixX() IPC call. Implication: any new agent capability needing local machine or local-server access (e.g. reading Tone settings via get\_tones) must be a CLIENT tool, not a server tool with \`execute\` — a server-side execute only works for BYOK (in-process) and is invisible on the Cloud path, since the cloud Worker can't reach the desktop's local SQLite/local server. Add new client tools to REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS in both repos plus a renderer executor case only — no cloud-side executor needed.

<!-- lore:019fd6f7-4d8d-766f-9652-0e9391cd5c29 -->
* **REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS have no execute — paused and streamed to renderer**: packages/validations/src/remix.ts defines REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS (13 tools: get\_context, select\_all, select\_text, read\_document, collapse\_selection, copy, set\_clipboard, set\_clipboard\_image, paste, undo, redo, press\_key, get\_clipboard) as \`{description, inputSchema}\` with NO \`execute\`. When streamText hits one of these calls, the AI SDK pauses the loop and streams the tool call to the desktop renderer via useChat's onToolCall. The renderer's executeTool (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/components/remix-chat.tsx:299-386) implements the real action via window.api.remix\* IPC bridge calls (keyboard/clipboard/selection manipulation) and returns the result to resume the loop. This file is mirrored byte-for-byte into the cloud repo (comment at remix.ts:12-14) — no shared package exists, so any new client tool must be hand-ported to both repos or cloud drifts.

<!-- lore:019fd6d9-e2c7-76bb-b7b0-adc049eb37ba -->
* **REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS is the wire contract mirrored across desktop+cloud repos**: packages/validations/src/remix.ts's REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS (get\_context, read\_document, select\_all, select\_text, collapse\_selection, copy, set\_clipboard, set\_clipboard\_image, paste, undo, redo, press\_key, get\_clipboard, get\_tones) declares client-executed tools with NO \`execute\` — both the cloud Worker's agent loop and the desktop BYOK loop (runRemixAgentLocally) pause at these calls and stream them to the renderer, which executes them via window.api.remixX() IPC (remix-chat.tsx executeTool(), wired in preload/index.ts). This file, plus the standing system prompt (apps/server/src/lib/editor/remix-prompts.ts / cloud's apps/server/src/routes/v2/remix/prompt.ts), must stay byte-for-byte identical between the desktop repo (freestyle-voice/freestyle) and the separate cloud repo (freestyle-voice/cloud) — same parity convention as cleanup-presets.ts \[\[019fb9c5-46b7-753e-9617-d09929eb3608]]. Desktop imports validations as \`@freestyle-voice/validations\`; cloud imports the identical source under alias \`@freestyle/validations\`.

<!-- lore:019fd6d3-b148-798b-a55d-10523579d74a -->
* **REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS mirrored byte-for-byte between freestyle and cloud repos**: packages/validations/src/remix.ts and the Remix agent system prompt (desktop: apps/server/src/lib/editor/remix-prompts.ts; cloud: apps/server/src/routes/v2/remix/prompt.ts) must stay byte-for-byte identical across two separate git repos: freestyle-voice/freestyle (desktop) and freestyle-voice/cloud. REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS entries declare no \`execute\` — they're only the wire contract; actual execution always happens in the desktop renderer (remix-chat.tsx's executeTool), regardless of whether the agent loop runs locally (BYOK, runRemixAgentLocally) or on the Cloud Worker (desktop's /api/remix/agent proxies to cloud's /v2/remix). To add a new client tool: (1) add the entry to remix.ts in BOTH repos, (2) add an executor case in desktop remix-chat.tsx only — cloud never executes, (3) mirror prompt-doc lines in both prompt files. Desktop imports validations as \`@freestyle-voice/validations\`; cloud imports the same source package as \`@freestyle/validations\` — same package, different workspace alias, easy import-name mixup (caused a build error mid-session).

<!-- lore:019fd70e-52e4-758b-b1b6-3de851e446f0 -->
* **REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS vs cloud server tools (web\_search/image\_search) split**: packages/validations/src/remix.ts defines REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS: 13 tools (get\_context, select\_all, select\_text, read\_document, collapse\_selection, copy, set\_clipboard, set\_clipboard\_image, paste, undo, redo, press\_key, get\_clipboard), each {description, inputSchema} with NO execute. streamText pauses the agent loop at these calls; useChat's onToolCall streams them to the renderer, where remix-chat.tsx:299-386 executeTool() maps each to a window.api.remix\* IPC call against the live OS. Separately, web\_search/image\_search ARE server tools (have execute, Exa-backed) but exist only in the cloud repo's routes/v2/remix/tools.ts, run entirely on the Worker, never surfaced to the client — gated by hasWebSearch in buildRemixAgentSystem (false for BYOK's runRemixAgentLocally, true for cloud). REMIX\_MAX\_STEPS=16 because primitive client tools cost more steps per edit (6-8 calls is typical for one canvas-app edit). Remix has no apps/mobile support (zero grep matches) — desktop + cloud only.

<!-- lore:019fd6e5-a888-7907-924a-61ddf389f7b9 -->
* **REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS wire contract mirrored: freestyle-voice/freestyle vs cloud repo**: packages/validations/src/remix.ts (REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS) and the Remix agent's system prompt (apps/server/src/lib/editor/remix-prompts.ts in the freestyle-voice/freestyle desktop repo; apps/server/src/routes/v2/remix/prompt.ts in the cloud repo) must be kept byte-for-byte identical across both repos — same rule as cleanup-presets.ts. Desktop imports the shared package as \`@freestyle-voice/validations\`; cloud imports the identical source as \`@freestyle/validations\` (different workspace alias, same package). Client tools declared here (get\_context, get\_clipboard, get\_tones, etc.) have no \`execute\` — only the desktop renderer's executeTool switch (remix-chat.tsx) implements them. Adding a new client tool needs: (1) tool schema in both repos' remix.ts, (2) executor case in remix-chat.tsx (desktop only), (3) prompt update in both repos' prompt files. Always diff-compare before editing the cloud copy to guarantee parity.

<!-- lore:019fd731-5073-7928-855b-ee4bb3c36053 -->
* **REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS freshness check runs only at RemixChat mount**: Server-side thread freshness (remix-store.ts \`isFresh()\`, gated by \`REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS\`, default 15min) is only consulted inside RemixChat's mount effect (remix-chat.tsx:106-131, empty deps — runs once per mount). It does GET /api/remix/thread, falling back to POST /new if stale. \`last\_active\_at\` is bumped ONLY in \`saveThreadMessages()\` (remix-store.ts:131-133), called from POST /api/remix/thread/sync on \`useChat.onFinish\` (remix-chat.tsx:441-450) — i.e. only when a turn completes, never on mere GET/open. So merely opening the pill does not extend session life; only a completed turn does.

<!-- lore:019fd735-4062-7244-88b1-321be50529bb -->
* **remix\_threads.last\_active\_at bumped only on turn completion, not on open**: last\_active\_at is written in exactly one place: saveThreadMessages()'s transaction (apps/server/src/lib/remix-store.ts:131-133), called only from POST /api/remix/thread/sync, fired from RemixChat's useChat.onFinish (remix-chat.tsx:441-450) after each completed AI turn. Opening the pill, GET /api/remix/thread (getActiveThread — never mutates), or creating a new thread (startNewThread's INSERT DEFAULT VALUES) does not bump it. So a thread's freshness clock measures 'time since last completed AI turn', not 'time since pill was last shown or thread was last resumed'.

<!-- lore:019fd731-f31e-7329-9ad5-16a568acf40d -->
* **remix\_threads.last\_active\_at only bumped on thread sync, not on GET/mount**: \`remix\_threads.last\_active\_at\` is written in exactly one place: \`saveThreadMessages()\`'s transaction (remix-store.ts:131-133, \`UPDATE ... SET last\_active\_at = datetime('now')\`), called only from \`POST /api/remix/thread/sync\` (thread.ts:48-54), invoked only from RemixChat's \`useChat.onFinish\` (remix-chat.tsx:441-450) after a turn completes. Opening the pill or \`GET /api/remix/thread\` (thread.ts:33-43) is read-only and never mutates or creates a thread — only a completed conversational turn advances the idle clock. \`getActiveThread()\` (remix-store.ts:50-55) never creates a thread; it returns the latest thread only if \`isFresh()\`, else null.

<!-- lore:019fd71e-3b17-7d82-84fe-10e8e1ad4943 -->
* **remix-chat.tsx toolbar (.remix-chat-actions) — where to add panel buttons**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/components/remix-chat.tsx renders a fixed-size card (REMIX\_CHAT\_SURFACE 408×560, sizes defined in companion file remix-chat-surface.ts, .ts not .tsx) with two faces toggled by data-minimized: \`.remix-chat-face-strip\` (mini) and \`.remix-chat-face-full\`. Full card layout top-to-bottom: header \`.remix-chat-head\` (left \`.remix-chat-title\` logo+wordmark, right \`.remix-chat-actions\` button cluster), \`\<MessageScroller>\` message list, error notice, quick preset chips (pre-message only), composer (auto-grow textarea + send/stop). \`.remix-chat-actions\` currently holds exactly 2 buttons — 'New thread' and 'Close', both styled \`.remix-chat-icon\`, both call \`stop()\` then their action. Any new toolbar button (History, Settings) belongs here, same class/pattern. ThreadState \`{threadId, resumed, messages}\` drives \`useChat({id:'remix-thread-\<id>', messages})\`; persisted via GET /api/remix/thread on mount, POST /api/remix/thread/sync onFinish.

<!-- lore:019fd738-a637-72c8-b581-6fc57a2646cb -->
* **remix-chat.tsx toolbar: .remix-chat-actions holds only New Thread + Close**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/components/remix-chat.tsx (1613 lines), the full-card face \`.remix-chat-face-full\` has header \`.remix-chat-head\` → left \`.remix-chat-title\` (wordmark) and right \`.remix-chat-actions\` button cluster. Currently only 2 buttons exist there, both styled \`.remix-chat-icon\`: 'New thread' (calls stop() then onNewThread()) and 'Close' (calls stop() then onClose()). Below the header: \`\<MessageScroller>\` message list, error notice, quick preset chips (shown only pre-first-message), then composer (auto-grow textarea + send/stop). Companion file \`remix-chat-surface.ts\` (note: .ts, not .tsx — no .tsx version exists) exports size constants REMIX\_CHAT\_SURFACE {408x560, full card} and REMIX\_CHAT\_STRIP {320x44, minimized strip} plus the RemixChatAnchor type, kept separate so app.tsx can size the pill window without importing chat animation deps. Any new toolbar button (e.g. History, Settings) goes in \`.remix-chat-actions\`.

<!-- lore:019fcea3-1021-7085-bf62-aef8981e949a -->
* **Remix/dictation share one pill window — remixRef.current is the mode discriminator, no mode enum**: apps/electron runs two independent native key listeners (dictation Fn, remix Fn+Control on macOS) as separate OS processes that can't see each other (main/index.ts), both feeding one shared pill window (renderer/src/pages/app.tsx). There is no single 'mode' enum; the whole renderer branches on \`remixRef.current !== null\` (remix active) vs \`stateRef\`/\`pillActiveRef\` (dictation active). \`remixRef.current\` is set in 3 places (beginRemix, openRemixChat, onRemixOpenChat) and reset ONLY inside \`endRemix()\` (app.tsx:1920→1933 \`setRemix(null)\`). \`failRemix()\` deliberately leaves the session set (error phase awaits explicit Escape/Dismiss/hotkey). Known asymmetry: \`remix:supersede\` (main/index.ts:3901) lets a remix chord cancel in-flight dictation, but there is no inverse letting a dictation press cancel/refocus away from an active remix chat at the native-listener level — this asymmetry underlies several focus-stealing/hotkey-routing bugs (see \[\[endRemix hide:false gotcha]]).

<!-- lore:019f6be5-ccf0-7c15-a740-a1d4c1b2f9e0 -->
* **Remote server config: stored in settings.json via IPC, NOT SQLite — chicken-and-egg constraint**: Pattern: \`broadcastServerChanged()\` in \`main/index.ts\` sends \`server:changed\` to \`mainWindow\` and \`settingsWindow\` webContents. Renderer windows listen via \`onServerChanged\` in a \`useEffect\` (app.tsx): calls \`refreshApiBase()\`, tears down \`streamerRef.current\` (destroy + null), resets \`supportsSessionTransportRef.current = false\`, then calls \`getStreamer()\` to reconnect. Modeled after existing \`onPillPositionChanged\`/\`onOutputModeChanged\` IPC broadcast pattern. Prevents unnecessary Electron app restarts when server URL/token changes — config changes propagate via IPC + React Query cache invalidation. Prefer this over restart-based flows per user directive 'no app restart for server URL/token changes'.

<!-- lore:019fd0b2-a731-763a-b32e-4506634b0b54 -->
* **Renderer API client: getClient() (Hono RPC) vs apiFetch() (raw escape hatch)**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/api.ts exposes two access patterns: getClient() (line ~127) returns a fresh \`hc\<AppType>(getApiBase(), {headers})\` Hono RPC client per call — used for all typed requests, and should be wrapped in useQuery/useMutation (or the shared options helpers in lib/query.ts) whenever the result is cacheable. apiFetch() (line ~43) is a raw fetch wrapper, documented as the escape hatch for binary uploads (WAV), streaming (/api/transcribe, /api/remix/agent), and beacons (telemetry/client-error) that can't go through the RPC client or React Query. checkServerHealth/checkServerAuth are standalone reachability probes, also legitimately outside React Query. When auditing data-fetching, only flag apiFetch usages that are one-off \*reads\* in effects (e.g. remix-chat.tsx's mount-time thread fetch) as candidates for useQuery — writes/streams/beacons are legitimate exceptions, not violations.

<!-- lore:019faf4f-7e0a-702a-8657-6ae58e34f13f -->
* **Renderer never calls Freestyle Cloud directly — always proxied through apps/server**: Architecture: Renderer (UI) → local embedded server (apps/server) → Freestyle Cloud (service.freestylevoice.com). Renderer calls local routes (apps/server/src/routes/auth.ts, usage.ts, billing.ts, transcribe.ts, stream.ts) via Hono RPC client (getClient().api...). Local server functions in apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts (listCloudAccounts, linkCloudSocial, unlinkCloudAccount, fetchCloudUsage, transcribeWithFreestyleCloud, createCheckoutSession, etc.) make the actual fetch() calls to cloud using the server-side session token. This centralizes token management, retry logic for stale-socket resets (via cloudJson), and error classification, and keeps the OAuth token out of the renderer entirely. When adding any new cloud-backed feature, follow this same layering (schema → cloud-lib fn → route → renderer hook) rather than having renderer fetch cloud directly — this is the established convention, not an ad hoc choice.

<!-- lore:019fd0b2-a700-7719-8d7b-fd812ced4cfb -->
* **Renderer QueryClient defaults: staleTime 1h, retry 1, no gcTime override**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/query.ts's createQueryClient() sets defaultOptions.queries: refetchOnWindowFocus:false, staleTime:ONE\_HOUR, retry:1. No query anywhere in the renderer sets gcTime — all inherit TanStack's 5-min default. This means queries flagged as having 'missing staleTime' during an audit are usually fine (they inherit the 1h default), not actual bugs — freshness comes from explicit invalidateQueries on mutations plus IPC events (e.g. onTranscriptionDone), not from refetch-on-focus. Genuine volatile-data queries (whisper/mlx download status in use-models.ts, onboarding.tsx) explicitly override with staleTime:0 + refetchInterval. Check this default before flagging a query's cache config as suspect.

<!-- lore:019f5b5e-9261-7919-91e2-2a3d63a05dc8 -->
* **replace raw fetch calls with typed getClient() RPC**: Replaced 3 raw \`fetch\` calls with typed \`getClient()\` RPC across \`tutorial-demo.tsx\`, \`today.tsx\`, and \`app.tsx\`. Kept 2 raw fetches in \`app.tsx\` (transcribe WAV POST) and \`lib/analytics.ts\` (telemetry) as they involve binary bodies or fire-and-forget semantics. Updated imports accordingly. All changes pass project typecheck.

<!-- lore:019fd309-6c35-7396-972c-bbbb769f809c -->
* **resolveAccessibilityPermission is read-only, never overrides macOS trust**: resolveAccessibilityPermission() (apps/electron/src/main/permission-checks.ts:16) and related calls only report the current macOS Accessibility trust status — they never call an API that would set/override the OS trust value. A comment in main/index.ts (~line 394) explicitly documents this: the check result is never used to override the current macOS Accessibility trust result. Any fix for stale/stuck permission state must route through the OS Settings deep link (openAccessibility IPC handler), not by trying to force-set trust in-process — there is no such API.

<!-- lore:019fb9b5-1597-72dc-92db-60b1eb7f07af -->
* **route-level code splitting in dashboard.tsx (React.lazy + Suspense)**: Converted page imports in \`renderer/src/dashboard.tsx\` to dynamic \`import()\` via \`React.lazy\`. Wrapped routes in \`\<Suspense fallback={\<PagePlaceholder />}>\`. Added \`build.rollupOptions.output.manualChunks\` hint in \`electron.vite.config.ts\` to split vendor chunk (\`react\`, \`react-dom\`, \`@tanstack/react-query\`). This reduces initial JS payload and aligns with Vercel React best practices. No changes to route logic or hooks.

<!-- lore:019f2428-af19-7c78-add5-a93bb2508fd1 -->
* **SDK AfterTranscribeOutput: consumed flag and PluginLlm capability**: \`packages/sdk/src/hooks.ts\`: \`afterTranscribe\` returns \`AfterTranscribeOutput { text: string; consumed?: boolean }\`. When \`consumed: true\`, both \`transcribe.ts\` and \`stream.ts\` skip LLM cleanup and return empty text (triggering \`deliverOutput\` empty-text suppression). \`packages/sdk/src/context.ts\`: \`PluginContext\` has optional \`llm?: PluginLlm\` (\`getModel(): unknown\`, \`providerId: string\`, \`modelId: string\`). \`buildPluginLlm()\` in \`apps/server/src/lib/plugins/context.ts\` constructs it from host providers. ALWAYS guard with \`if (ctx.llm)\` — present only in server mode when default LLM is configured. Plugins never manage their own credentials — LLM access routes through host endpoint only.

<!-- lore:019fb97a-a419-7ee8-9e7c-0186c9dd1772 -->
* **Server bearer auth middleware + checkServerHealth hc client**: Add optional bearer auth to the embedded server via a \`BearerAuth\` Hono middleware configured with \`AUTH\_TOKEN\` env var. When set, the middleware protects all \`/api/\*\` routes (except \`/api/health\`). The \`checkServerHealth()\` helper should use the \`hc\` client (not raw fetch) so it participates in the same connection pool, respects timeouts, and works with the middleware when \`AUTH\_TOKEN\` is present. Pass the token via \`hc\` client headers; keep the existing \`/api/health\` bypass for liveness probes. This keeps auth opt-in and consistent with the existing \`getClient()\` pattern.

<!-- lore:019f6a3c-4c52-75a3-ac10-eb38761d3708 -->
* **Server: add consolidated pre-warm endpoint**: Added \`POST /api/transcribe/pre-warm\` to \`apps/server/src/routes/transcribe.ts\`. Calls \`prewarmWhisperServer()\` and \`prewarmMlxAsrServer()\` (renamed from \`autoStart\*\`), returning which provider was warmed. Both functions now live in their respective lib modules and return booleans. Endpoint is safe for cloud/BYOK providers (no-op). Electron fires this fire-and-forget on record start to warm models in parallel with recording, reducing first-submit latency. Server decides whether warming is needed (no-op for cloud/BYOK).

<!-- lore:019fb9b5-1578-7305-a9ba-d98eef6c0bcc -->
* **server: add requestId, logger, scoped timeout middleware in index.ts**: Added \`requestId()\`, \`logger()\`, and scoped \`timeout()\` middleware to \`apps/server/src/index.ts\`. Applied to lightweight CRUD routers (settings, keys, dictionary, vocabulary, history, models, plugins, usage); excluded heavy transcription/download routes. Order: trustedOrigin → cors → requestId → logger → secureHeaders → \[scoped timeout] → plugin middleware → routes. Aligns with existing onError/captureException pattern.

<!-- lore:019fb9b5-1588-780b-aa15-8cd369ac9869 -->
* **server: add validation schemas to packages/validations and use zValidator in routes**: Added validation schemas for new/updated routes: \`diagnostics.ts\` (telemetry/client-error), \`auth.ts\` (deviceToken), \`post-process.ts\` (postProcessSchema with .refine for trim), \`models.ts\` (serverStartSchema + historyQuerySchema), \`dictionary.ts\` (importDictionarySchema), \`query.ts\` (historyQuerySchema). Replaced manual \`c.req.body\` parsing with \`zValidator('json', schema)\` in: telemetry, client-error, auth/device-token, models/configured, dictionary/import, post-process, whisper/start, mlx-asr/start, history GET, stats GET, dictionary GET, vocabulary GET. Kept lenient handling for dictionary import to preserve skip-of-empty-entries behavior. All schemas exported from \`packages/validations/src/index.ts\`.

<!-- lore:019fb9c2-efe2-7f21-85dc-a464060b36ee -->
* **Server: output/deliver endpoint and events relay**: Added \`POST /api/output/deliver\` in \`apps/server/src/routes/output.ts\` that runs \`beforeOutput\` hook, then pastes/copies based on mode. Added \`relayServerEvent\` in \`apps/server/src/lib/events.ts\` for structured event forwarding to renderer. \`deliverOutput\` in \`apps/server/src/index.ts\` updated to use new output route and emit \`outputDelivered\` event. Events include appName, destination, and hasAppContext; exclude sensitive fields.

<!-- lore:019fb9c2-efda-78c8-9b84-4d323754126e -->
* **Server: post-process with HookApi and app assignments**: Extended \`postProcess\` in \`apps/server/src/lib/post-process.ts\` to accept optional \`api: HookApi\` and \`appAssignments\`. Refactored \`applyFinalRewrites\` to support \`skip\` (return empty), \`suppress\` (return empty with suppression), and \`rewrite\` modes. Thread \`api\` through \`transcribe.ts\` and \`stream.ts\` so plugins can intercept final output. Empty-transcript guard added before \`applyFinalRewrites\` in both batch and streaming paths.

<!-- lore:019f6b7b-1b9c-70ce-b6e6-c10bbef9fffb -->
* **Server: remove switch pre-warm block from models route**: Removed the whisper switch pre-warm block (lines 498-506) from \`apps/server/src/routes/models.ts\`. The pre-warm now happens via the new \`/api/transcribe/pre-warm\` endpoint. Removed unused imports \`startInBackground\`, \`stripProviderPrefix\`, \`isServerBinaryAvailable\`. \`WHISPER\_PROVIDER\_ID\` kept for model catalog.

<!-- lore:019f6a56-d8db-7198-9e92-3fe702b2c6e2 -->
* **Server: remove whisper switch pre-warm from models route**: Removed the whisper switch pre-warm block from \`apps/server/src/routes/models.ts\`. Pre-warming now centralized in the new \`/api/transcribe/pre-warm\` endpoint. Removed unused imports \`startInBackground\`, \`stripProviderPrefix\`, \`isServerBinaryAvailable\`. \`WHISPER\_PROVIDER\_ID\` kept for model catalog.

<!-- lore:019fb9c2-eff0-7f82-940b-9e37bf17f2f9 -->
* **Server: validations package and zValidator usage**: Added \`packages/validations/src/index.ts\` exporting zod schemas for settings, auth, dictionary import, post-process, and models. Replaced manual \`c.req.body\` parsing with \`zValidator('json', schema)\` in routes: telemetry, client-error, auth/device-token, models/configured, dictionary/import, post-process, whisper/start, mlx-asr/start, history, stats, dictionary, vocabulary. Keeps lenient handling for dictionary import.

<!-- lore:019fb1fd-8df7-7f0e-b85a-5626802123d7 -->
* **sessions table keyed by host (schema v17) — dev/prod token coexistence**: \`sessions\` table was a singleton row (\`CHECK(id=1)\`) storing one token regardless of which cloud host issued it. Switching \`FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL\` between dev (e.g. localhost:8787) and prod clobbered the other environment's token because \`getSession()\`/\`setSession()\` always operated on id=1, ignoring the stored (but unused) \`host\` column. Fixed in schema v17: \`sessions\` rebuilt with \`host TEXT PRIMARY KEY\` instead of \`id\`; all queries in \`apps/server/src/lib/sessions.ts\` (getSession/clearSession/touchSessionExpiry/setSession) now scope by \`host = freestyleCloudUrl()\`. \`invalidateSession()\` side effects (resetCloudIdentity, revertFreestyleCloudDefaults) remain global/not per-host — acceptable since only one host is operated on per process run. All existing call-sites (auth.ts, stream.ts, transcribe.ts, session-keepalive.ts) already passed \`host\` on setSession, so they needed no changes.

<!-- lore:019fb22f-6368-791f-af20-2696050f94a6 -->
* **sessions table keyed by host (TEXT PRIMARY KEY), not singleton id=1**: \`sessions\` table in apps/server/src/lib/schema.ts was rebuilt in migration v17 (SCHEMA\_VERSION 16→17): \`host TEXT PRIMARY KEY NOT NULL\` replaces \`id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY CHECK(id=1)\`. Reason: the old singleton row meant signing into dev (\`FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL=http://localhost:8787\`) overwrote the same row holding the prod token — switching envs clobbered the other session. Now dev and prod sessions coexist as separate rows. sessions.ts (getSession/clearSession/touchSessionExpiry/setSession) all resolve the host via the existing \`freestyleCloudUrl()\` helper (freestyle-cloud.ts) rather than reading \`process.env.FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL\` directly — this preserves default/prod behavior since \`freestyleCloudUrl()\` returns the same default everywhere. Sign-out (\`clearSession\`/\`invalidateSession\`) is now per-host: signing out of dev preserves the prod session. \`invalidateSession()\`'s PostHog identity reset and cloud-defaults revert remain global (acceptable — only one host is active per run).

<!-- lore:019fbc97-18f6-7f76-acef-c2f5dbb5b820 -->
* **settings.ts PUT route: no per-key validation branch for \`language\` key**: \`apps/server/src/routes/settings.ts\`'s generic \`PUT /api/settings/:key\` handler has dedicated validation branches for specific keys (plugins, disabled\_plugins, openai\_stt\_base\_url, proxy\_url, ca\_cert\_path, history\_retention\_days) but NOT for \`language\` — it falls through to the generic \`settingValueSchema = z.object({value: z.string()})\` with no format/enum validation. There is also no dedicated \`languageSettingSchema\` anywhere in packages/validations/src/settings.ts. Any string value is accepted and written via the generic \`INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO UPDATE\`. If \`SYNCED\_SETTING\_KEYS.has('language')\`, \`pushSettingToCloud\` fires (fire-and-forget, errors swallowed by design so a failed cloud sync never disrupts the local write).

<!-- lore:019f5b00-e8ad-7747-85b9-68ffd271406b -->
* **settings.tsx refactored into per-tab RHF components under pages/settings/**: As of commit \`415f6c6\` (branch \`refactor/settings-tab-forms\`), \`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/settings.tsx\` (1515 lines, 20+ useState hooks) was deleted and replaced with \`pages/settings/\` directory containing: \`constants.ts\` (option lists, parse helpers, SYSTEM\_DEFAULT\_MIC), \`components.tsx\` (Row, SettingsPanel, Segment, PermissionControl, StatusDot, SettingsSidebar), \`use-settings-form.ts\` (persist-on-change RHF engine), \`use-app-updater.ts\`, \`use-permissions.ts\`, \`recording-panel.tsx\`, \`application-panel.tsx\`, \`display-panel.tsx\`, \`data-panel.tsx\`, \`network-panel.tsx\`, \`permissions-panel.tsx\`, \`index.tsx\` (shell). NetworkPanel was already using RHF; all other tabs now use it too. 15 files changed, 1988 insertions, 1552 deletions.

<!-- lore:019f5b00-e8bb-7917-b24d-cb82d0614e97 -->
* **settings.tsx state categories: server-persisted, IPC-backed, and transient**: Settings state falls into 3 categories: (A) Server-persisted/persist-on-change — selectedDevice, hotkey, hotkeyMode, language, outputMode, soundEnabled, historyPaused, historyRetention, customRetentionDays, audioPlaybackMode — written via \`PUT /settings/:key\`. (B) IPC/window-backed — pillPosition, autoUpdate, launchAtStartup, showOnLaunch, theme — read/written via \`window.api\`. (C) Transient/not settings — devices list, updateAvailable/Downloaded/downloading/updateError, micStatus/accessibilityStatus, activeSection. IPC-backed fields are seeded from \`window.api\` reads, not the settings cache. Audio playback mode has a legacy fallback chain: \`audio\_playback\_mode\` → \`pause\_playback\_while\_recording\` → \`audio\_ducking\_enabled\`.

<!-- lore:019fb9b5-1590-7436-9e1c-7f9ee1254d11 -->
* **shared lib/query.ts with global QueryClient defaults (staleTime, retry)**: Created \`renderer/src/lib/query.ts\` exporting \`ONE\_HOUR = 60 \* 60 \* 1000\` and \`createQueryClient()\` with defaultOptions: \`staleTime: ONE\_HOUR\`, \`retry: 1\`, \`refetchOnWindowFocus: false\`. Updated \`dashboard.tsx\` and \`plugins/helpers.ts\` to use these. This ensures consistent caching across the renderer and removes per-query staleTime/retry noise. No breaking changes — existing queries adopt defaults; overrides remain explicit.

<!-- lore:019f4680-6d6f-719d-949d-609756e748a7 -->
* **Shared STT package: core+injected-model, lives in desktop monorepo, cloud consumes published build**: Architectural decisions (confirmed by user): (1) Ownership model: 'Core + injected-model execution' — package provides prompt/token logic; callers inject the AI SDK model instance. (2) Location: lives in desktop monorepo \`packages/\`; cloud consumes the published npm build (not a local workspace dep). (3) Cleanup tone types and \`CLEANUP\_PRESET\_PROMPTS\` fold into the shared package. Critical constraint: \`@freestyle-voice/validations\` and \`@freestyle-voice/utils\` are both \`private:true\` — shared package MUST be a leaf dependency with no dep on either validations package, so cloud/third parties can install it without private deps.

<!-- lore:019fd579-92ea-7ef9-b783-d0b8bb74f837 -->
* **shell.tsx UpdateBanner z-index layering vs glass-topbar**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/shell.tsx, \`UpdateBanner\` (from update-banner.tsx) is rendered with wrapper classes \`relative z-50 mt-14 w-\[calc(100%-3rem)] self-center\` (max-w-2xl was removed in PR #555 to span full content width for a blocking, non-dismissible update strip). The \`glass-topbar\` div (containing GitHub/Discord icons via SiGithub/SiDiscord) is \`absolute z-40\`. Despite UpdateBanner having a higher z-index, \`mt-14\` pushes it below the topbar visually, so there's no overlap/clash with the top-right icons — don't assume z-50 > z-40 implies visual collision here; check the mt-14 offset too. UpdateBanner itself has no close/dismiss affordance by design (always non-dismissible).

<!-- lore:019fd724-49a8-7af4-b6c5-fde3c301c2d3 -->
* **showSettingsWindow(path) is main-process-only — pill has no navigate/openSettings IPC**: The pill (mainWindow, pill.html→app.tsx) and dashboard (settingsWindow, index.html→dashboard.tsx) are separate BrowserWindow processes. showSettingsWindow(path) (apps/electron/src/main/index.ts:1857) is the sole mechanism to open/navigate the dashboard: creates settingsWindow if absent, else loadURL(getDashboardURL(path)) if path given (full page reload, not a hash-only nav) then shows+focuses. It's only called from main-process code (menu, tray, dialogs — e.g. cloud:prompt-sign-in→"/settings/models", cloud:prompt-upgrade→"/today?upgrade=1") and is never exposed via preload/window.api. To let the pill (renderer) trigger dashboard navigation, add a new ipcMain.on handler + preload send-method (mirror setRemixChatFocus's pattern at preload/index.ts:164-165) that calls showSettingsWindow(path) from main — there is currently no existing channel for this.

<!-- lore:019fd6a4-764f-7c96-962c-48da7e5865a6 -->
* **SignInSplit shared between onboarding CloudStep and LoginGate**: components/sign-in-split.tsx (SignInSplit + SignInButton) is shared verbatim between onboarding's CloudStep (first onboarding screen) and login-gate.tsx's LoginPage (rendered when useCloudAuth() reports no user). Same two-column layout: left has logo/title/subtitle/children/error/ToS links; right is an ink-panel video demo carousel (3 DEMO\_SLIDES showcasing Freestyle Remix use cases). Only difference is titlePrefix/subtitle text and children content (bare SignInButton in LoginGate vs full CloudStep signed-in state in onboarding). Reuse this component for any future sign-in-gated screen instead of duplicating the split layout.

<!-- lore:019fcbff-5d6c-7c5c-9d24-1477f49d312e -->
* **specs/voice-pill-motion.md documents pill animation contract**: Complex animation features in apps/electron (e.g. the voice pill in pages/app.tsx) get a dedicated spec doc under specs/ (e.g. specs/voice-pill-motion.md). Sections include rationale ("Why"), invariants (animations are renderer-driven, so a handler always exists on the other side to execute them), verification methodology (getBoundingClientRect() sampling per animation frame to confirm exact values, e.g. bar-to-bar gap holding at exactly 6.0px), and a standalone vanilla-JS prototype rebuilt at true geometry for isolated/offline testing without a real mic. Follow this pattern for future non-trivial motion/animation work rather than only relying on inline PR description prose.

<!-- lore:019fb9a9-d885-79d0-830e-5c9e1a5d88ea -->
* **stream.ts fold cleanup prefs into config fingerprint**: Added \`cleanup\` (provider+model+intensity+customPrompt) to the fingerprint key in \`resolveStreamConfig()\` in \`apps/server/src/routes/stream.ts\`. When any cleanup pref changes, \`sameConfig\` becomes false at the caller in \`connectUpstream\`, closing the warm upstream and opening a fresh session with the current cleanup settings. This implements the "stop and start a new one" behavior for cloud cleanup changes without a full app restart.

<!-- lore:019f6596-4fc5-7fbb-a2ab-2f53306793bc -->
* **stream.ts: remove middleware guard blocking WebSocket upgrade**: The \`GET /stream\` route returns 500 because the middleware guard \`if (!getFlag("streaming\_audio"))\` blocks the WebSocket upgrade handshake. \`upgradeWebSocket\` in \`@hono/node-server\` v2 requires the middleware to call \`next()\` even for upgrade requests; returning a 400 JSON response prevents the WS handshake. Remove the flag guard from the middleware and gate the route itself (or the factory) instead. This ensures the WS upgrade reaches the \`upgradeWebSocket\` handler, which properly handles the handshake and fires the \`onConfig\` callback that sets \`supportsSessionTransportRef\`.

<!-- lore:019fd277-3d9b-7b1c-9611-a796ed01c29d -->
* **Streamer WebSocket auth via ?token= query param, not header**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/streamer.ts opens its streaming WebSocket to \`${base}/stream\` with the auth token passed as a \`?token=\` query parameter, not an Authorization header — because the browser/Node WebSocket API provides no way to set custom headers on the handshake request. This is the reason the streaming server route must accept token-via-query-param auth in addition to header auth used by regular REST calls.

<!-- lore:019fd2e7-b634-7718-9889-8b042a8122ca -->
* **Streamer.ts sessions yield one final transcript, never interim/partial**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/streamer.ts is designed so a streaming session 'never shows in-progress text' (explicit code comment) — it yields a single final transcript per session, not incremental partials. A 'Live partial transcript' field exists but today is consumed only by the remix chat card, not the main dictation UI. The Streamer instance is persistent across recording sessions (created once in app.tsx, not per-session); destroy() is wired at pill unmount. getWavBlob() lets the streaming path fall back to a REST WAV upload (via accumulated pcmChunks) if the WebSocket fails.

<!-- lore:019fcbf0-d172-7f4d-83eb-ef66c79f7c2c -->
* **StreamerCallbacks.onPartial restored optional for RemixChat live transcript**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/streamer.ts's \`StreamerCallbacks.onPartial\` (fires on partial transcript chunks) was removed entirely by main's dictation refactor (no longer needed by the main pipeline) but restored as OPTIONAL (\`onPartial?:\`, not required as in the original) during the PR #534 merge, because remix's \`getRemixStreamer\`/RemixChat live-transcript display still depends on it. Kept optional so main's existing dictation streamer instantiations (which omit it) still typecheck. \`onCleaned\` was intentionally not restored — nothing in the merged code path consumes it.

<!-- lore:019f6503-5b97-7f35-bdad-9fe53d36bd7b -->
* **Streaming audio: experimental feature flag gates the /api/stream WebSocket route**: The streaming audio feature is gated behind a \`streaming\_audio\_enabled\` setting key (added to \`SETTINGS\_KEYS\` in \`settings-keys.ts\`). The server-side \`/api/stream\` WebSocket route checks \`readSetting('streaming\_audio\_enabled') !== 'true'\` and returns 403 if disabled. The renderer only opens the WebSocket when the flag is enabled. The Experimental section in Settings (new sidebar tab) provides the toggle UI. This allows the full streaming infrastructure to exist in the codebase without being active for users until explicitly enabled.

<!-- lore:019f6596-4fc7-7c76-b0e3-33a88de56132 -->
* **Streaming audio: scoped to Freestyle Cloud only, behind experimental flag**: Streaming audio (WebSocket route \`/stream\`) is gated by two conditions: (1) \`getFlag("streaming\_audio")\` from \`config.freestyle.json\` must be true; (2) \`resolveStreamConfig()\` in \`stream.ts\` returns \`null\` (falls back to batch) for any provider that is not \`FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_PROVIDER\_ID\`. Deepgram/ElevenLabs/Soniox \`streaming: true\` model metadata was removed — streaming does NOT expand BYOK model availability. Experimental panel toggle always visible regardless of current provider (user directive: don't hide from non-Freestyle-Cloud users). \`duration\_ms\` in streaming path covers wall-clock from \`start\` message → \`onFinal\` (includes recording time); batch path covers POST arrival → response only.

<!-- lore:019fc2df-26b6-731e-bc85-4e0954908040 -->
* **Streaming translate guard checks post-fallback languages (stt-session.ts)**: In the Freestyle Cloud streaming Durable Object (stt-session.ts), the client 'start' message's \`translate\` field has no runtime schema validation (Zod-free, TS-only type) — any truthy JSON value passes through. The guard \`this.translate = !!msg.translate && this.languages?.length === 1\` evaluates against \`this.languages\`, the POST-FALLBACK value (\`payloadLanguages ?? storedLanguages\` from synced member\_preferences), not the raw payload. This lets desktop/mobile omit \`languages\` from the start message while still sending \`translate: true\` — translate still activates if the stored language list has exactly one entry. Gotcha: if the stored language is the Hinglish alias "hi-en", \`normalizeLanguages\` expands it to \`\["hi","en"]\` (length 2), silently disabling translate even though the user configured a single language.

<!-- lore:019f64f6-14da-7347-8634-a572edaca3f3 -->
* **Tone page: no-model banner across all tone tabs**: Extracted inline 'no cleanup model' hint into a top-level CleanupNoModelBanner component shown above the tone selector tabs whenever cleanup is enabled but no model is configured. Removed the now-dead hasCleanupModel prop from CleanupTonePanel. This banner appears in Personal, Work, Email, and Everything-else tabs so users get consistent guidance.

<!-- lore:019fd6e8-06d1-7b44-8811-e4293033a130 -->
* **Tone/cleanup config data flow: settings → prompt assembly**: Tone page (apps/electron/.../pages/tone.tsx) writes settings via SETTINGS\_KEYS (apps/electron/src/shared/settings-keys.ts: cleanup\_intensity, cleanup\_custom\_prompt, cleanup\_personal/work/email/overall\_tone, cleanup\_app\_assignments, llm\_cleanup) to SQLite via PUT /api/settings/:key, validated by packages/validations (settings.ts, cleanup-tones.ts). Server reads via apps/server/src/lib/post-process.ts \`getEffectiveCleanupTones()\` (batched read) + \`getRewritePromptContext()\` (apps/server/src/lib/editor/rewrite-context.ts — resolves destination personal/work/email/overall via cleanup\_app\_assignments then built-in app/pattern tables). \`buildRewritePrompt()\` (editor/prompts.ts) assembles the final system+prompt from apps/server/src/lib/editor/prompt-config.ts content. A plugin/agent wanting this config reads raw keys via \`ctx.settings.get("cleanup\_intensity"|...)\` and parses with packages/validations, or intercepts the \`beforeCleanup\` SDK hook to see/edit the fully-resolved destination+prompt before the LLM call runs.

<!-- lore:019fd6cc-2465-7c6d-a3d9-a38dd8f9adb4 -->
* **Tone/cleanup config pipeline: settings → prompt-config → prompts → rewrite-context**: Settings keys (cleanup\_intensity, cleanup\_custom\_prompt, cleanup\_personal\_tone, cleanup\_work\_tone, cleanup\_email\_tone, cleanup\_overall\_tone, cleanup\_app\_assignments, llm\_cleanup) live in SQLite, defined in apps/electron/src/shared/settings-keys.ts, validated by packages/validations/src/{settings,cleanup-tones,cleanup-presets}.ts. Server flow: apps/server/src/lib/post-process.ts getEffectiveCleanupTones() batch-reads keys → apps/server/src/lib/editor/rewrite-context.ts getRewritePromptContext() resolves destination (user app assignments > built-in app names > substring patterns > "overall") + personalSurface (discord detection) → apps/server/src/lib/editor/prompts.ts buildRewritePrompt() assembles {system, prompt} from base preset (cleanup-presets.ts LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH; "custom" = user text, fallback to low) + destination tone block (skipped if tone==="off") + language block. Content source of truth (presets, tone blocks, routing tables) is apps/server/src/lib/editor/prompt-config.ts, overridable by cloud config (6h TTL), falling back to bundled defaults on fetch failure — "cloud shape can never break prompt assembly" is an explicit design invariant there.

<!-- lore:019fd750-2efc-7d76-a1d9-150c3e78fb8c -->
* **Tone/cleanup config schema ownership (cleanup-tones.ts, settings.ts, cleanup-presets.ts)**: packages/validations/src/cleanup-tones.ts defines 4 per-destination tone enums, each defaulting to "off": personal (polished|casual|very\_casual|off), work (direct|friendly|formal|off), email (casual|warm|formal|off), overall (casual|neutral|professional|off) — plus cleanupAppAssignmentSchema (match field trimmed+lowercased) and areAllCleanupTonesOff(). packages/validations/src/settings.ts owns cleanupIntensitySchema (low|medium|high|custom, default "medium") and cleanupCustomPromptSchema (max 20000 chars). packages/validations/src/cleanup-presets.ts holds the LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH preset prompt bodies (CLEANUP\_PRESET\_PROMPTS); "custom" has no fixed body — UI seeds it from the current preset on switch. All persist as raw strings in SQLite \`settings\` table via SETTINGS\_KEYS (apps/electron/src/shared/settings-keys.ts): cleanup\_intensity, cleanup\_custom\_prompt, cleanup\_personal\_tone, cleanup\_work\_tone, cleanup\_email\_tone, cleanup\_overall\_tone, cleanup\_app\_assignments, llm\_cleanup.

<!-- lore:019fd6e5-2d0c-7274-bf7a-7442c632b559 -->
* **Tone/cleanup config settings keys and validation schemas**: Cleanup/tone settings are 8 SQLite-persisted keys (apps/electron/src/shared/settings-keys.ts SETTINGS\_KEYS): llm\_cleanup (on/off toggle), cleanup\_intensity (low|medium|high|custom), cleanup\_custom\_prompt (max 20000 chars), cleanup\_personal\_tone, cleanup\_work\_tone, cleanup\_email\_tone, cleanup\_overall\_tone (per-destination, each its own enum), cleanup\_app\_assignments (JSON array, max 200, app/site→destination overrides, \`match\` lowercased both sides). Validated in packages/validations: settings.ts (cleanupIntensitySchema, DEFAULT\_CLEANUP\_INTENSITY='medium') and cleanup-tones.ts (per-destination enums, cleanupAppAssignmentSchema, areAllCleanupTonesOff() gates whether app-context capture is needed). Written via PUT /api/settings/:key (apps/server/src/routes/settings.ts). UI at apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/tone.tsx (5 tabs: cleanup/personal/work/email/everythingElse); cleanup-relevant key writes additionally call window.api.sendCleanupContextChanged().

Server reads via apps/server/src/lib/post-process.ts getEffectiveCleanupTones() (batched readSettings). <!-- lore:019fd753-dbfd-7f3a-8ac1-51a9d74feed7 -->
* **Tone/cleanup config system: layered architecture**: Tone/cleanup config flows through 5 layers: (1) settings keys — apps/electron/src/shared/settings-keys.ts (cleanup\_intensity, cleanup\_custom\_prompt, cleanup\_{personal,work,email,overall}\_tone, cleanup\_app\_assignments, llm\_cleanup), persisted in SQLite via PUT /api/settings/:key; (2) schemas — packages/validations/src/settings.ts (intensity enum) + cleanup-tones.ts (per-destination tone enums + app assignments) + cleanup-presets.ts (LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH prompt bodies); (3) server read — apps/server/src/lib/post-process.ts getEffectiveCleanupTones() batches all 6 keys; (4) destination resolution — apps/server/src/lib/editor/rewrite-context.ts getRewritePromptContext(): user app-assignments > built-in app-name match > pattern substring match > 'overall' fallback; (5) prompt assembly — apps/server/src/lib/editor/prompts.ts buildRewritePrompt(), content sourced from prompt-config.ts (cloud-overridable). Plugin seam: beforeCleanup hook can override destination/system/prompt/skip before buildRewritePrompt runs.
