<!-- lore:019fd744-425c-7f05-a690-8f72a4edc6c0 -->
* **Tone/cleanup config: schema locations + how a plugin reads it**: Tone/cleanup config lives in 3 packages/validations schemas: settings.ts (cleanupIntensitySchema: low|medium|high|custom, default 'medium'; cleanup\_custom\_prompt), cleanup-tones.ts (per-destination personal/work/email/overall tone enums, each incl. 'off', default 'off'; cleanupAppAssignmentSchema for app→destination routing), cleanup-presets.ts (LOW/MEDIUM/HIGH prompt bodies; 'custom' has no fixed body, UI seeds it from current preset). All persisted in SQLite \`settings\` table under keys from apps/electron/src/shared/settings-keys.ts (cleanup\_intensity, cleanup\_custom\_prompt, cleanup\_{personal,work,email,overall}\_tone, cleanup\_app\_assignments, llm\_cleanup). Server batch-reads via getEffectiveCleanupTones() in apps/server/src/lib/post-process.ts. Assembly: rewrite-context.ts resolves destination → prompts.ts buildRewritePrompt() combines base preset + tone block + language block using prompt-config.ts as content source. For a plugin/agent to load this config: capture \`ctx.settings\` in setup(), call \`ctx.settings.get('cleanup\_intensity')\` etc with raw string keys, parse with @freestyle-voice/validations parsers; use ctx.settings.getOwn/ctx.storage for the plugin's own state instead.

<!-- lore:019fd708-09ec-7385-876e-2464b13a0a77 -->
* **Tone/cleanup config: settings keys, pipeline, cloud override**: SQLite stores cleanup\_intensity (low|medium|high|custom, default medium — NO 'off'; enable/disable is the separate llm\_cleanup setting) and per-destination tones cleanup\_personal/work/email/overall\_tone (each has 'off', unlike intensity), plus cleanup\_app\_assignments. Read path: post-process.ts getEffectiveCleanupTones() → rewrite-context.ts getRewritePromptContext() (resolves destination: user assignments → built-in app names → pattern substrings → 'overall') → prompts.ts buildRewritePrompt() assembles {system,prompt} from cleanup-presets.ts base + destination tone block (empty if 'off') + language block. Prompt content lives in prompt-config.ts, cloud-overridable via Freestyle Cloud /v2/config (6h TTL), always falling back to bundled defaults on bad/stale data.

<!-- lore:019fd75f-1eb4-70a9-b07d-4e7d79059adb -->
* **Tone/cleanup config: SETTINGS\_KEYS + intensity has no 'off'**: Cleanup config lives in SQLite \`settings\` table, keyed by string constants in \`SETTINGS\_KEYS\` (apps/electron/src/shared/settings-keys.ts): cleanup\_intensity, cleanup\_custom\_prompt, cleanup\_personal\_tone, cleanup\_work\_tone, cleanup\_email\_tone, cleanup\_overall\_tone, cleanup\_app\_assignments, llm\_cleanup. Schemas in packages/validations/src/settings.ts + cleanup-tones.ts. \`cleanupIntensitySchema\` = low|medium|high|custom — deliberately has NO 'off' value; cleanup on/off is a separate toggle (\`llm\_cleanup\`, Models page). By contrast each per-destination tone (personal/work/email/overall) DOES have an 'off' value meaning 'base preset runs, no destination style block applied' (see destination-style.ts / buildDestinationToneBlock). \`areAllCleanupTonesOff()\` checks all 4; used to decide if app-context capture is needed.

<!-- lore:019f3c03-ef14-7103-b99d-9c15d991a785 -->
* **transcribe.ts three-mode cloud path: freestyleCleanupActive flag and combined-mode guard**: \`apps/server/src/routes/transcribe.ts\`: \`freestyleCleanupActive = !skipPostProcess && defaults.llm?.provider === FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_PROVIDER\_ID && readSetting('llm\_cleanup') === 'true'\`. Combined mode calls \`transcribeWithFreestyleCloud\` with \`mode: 'combined'\`, then \`applyFinalRewrites\`. \*\*Empty-transcript guard\*\*: after the cloud call, if \`!rawText.trim() || control ≠ running\`, call \`suppressedResponse()\` and return before history/telemetry. \*\*Cleaned fallback\*\*: use \`result.cleaned || rawText\` (not \`??\`) — empty string from cloud falls back to raw. Same guard pattern required in \`stream.ts\` combined cloud branch. Returns \`{ raw, cleaned, model, provider\_category, durationMs }\`.

<!-- lore:019f5cc0-3424-7cee-acd4-41a8354f38dc -->
* **TranscriptionProvider interface and STT core: types.ts and @freestyle-voice/stt**: \`TranscriptionProvider\` interface at \`apps/server/src/lib/streaming/types.ts:23-26\`: \`{ readonly providerId: string; transcribe(opts: TranscribeOptions): Promise\<TranscribeResult> }\`. \`TranscribeOptions\`: audio (Uint8Array), model (string), apiKey (string), language? (ISO-639-1), bias? (AsrVocabularyBias|null). \`TranscribeResult\`: text (string), segments? (Array<{text,startSecond,endSecond}>), durationInSeconds?. \`CLOUD\_TRANSCRIBE\_TIMEOUT\_MS = 120\_000\`. \`@freestyle-voice/stt\` (\`packages/stt/\`) is model-agnostic: \`transcribe()\` wraps \`experimental\_transcribe\` from \`ai\`; \`postProcess()\` wraps \`generateText\` — callers inject pre-built model instances. \`postProcess()\` never throws — falls back to raw text via \`onError\`.

<!-- lore:019fd6c1-32de-750d-a506-bf16de2d4421 -->
* **Transcriptions tab = history.tsx, Remix screen = remix.tsx (no shared component)**: The app's 'Transcriptions' tab is NOT a file named transcriptions.\*; it's implemented in apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/history.tsx (aka the History/Today page). The Remix screen is apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/remix.tsx (default export RemixPage), registered on the /remix route in apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/shell.tsx. Both pages share visual patterns (DragSpacer, tutorial hero, right-side stats rail) by convention only — each reimplements its own hero/stats block inline in the page file; there is no shared TutorialHero or StatsRail component. UI code comments claiming one page 'mirrors' the other describe copy-paste visual parity, not shared code — don't assume changing one propagates to the other.

<!-- lore:019fd6e3-9200-7095-a52b-3aec8e02658e -->
* **Transcriptions tab tutorial hero dismissal: history.tsx + useDismissible**: The "Transcriptions" tab in the UI is implemented as apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/history.tsx (no separate transcriptions.tsx file). Its dismissible tutorial hero (TutorialDemo + X close button, history.tsx:576-587) tracks dismissal via TWO overlapping mechanisms: (1) legacy usePersistentState<"0"|"1"> localStorage key "today.heroDismissed", and (2) server-backed useDismissible(KNOWN\_NOTIFICATION\_KEYS.TODAY\_TUTORIAL\_HERO = "today.tutorial\_hero", defined in packages/validations/src/notifications.ts) persisted via SQLite through GET/PUT/DELETE /api/dismissed-notifications/:key. Combined state: \`heroDismissed = storedHeroDismissed || legacyHeroDismissed === "1"\`. A one-time migration effect (guarded by migrationAttemptedRef) calls persistHeroDismissal() if the legacy flag is "1" but the server dismissal isn't yet set, so old local dismissals survive migration. New dismissible UI should use only useDismissible going forward — the legacy path is backward-compat only.

<!-- lore:019fbc97-18df-7072-b4aa-9bc96694c7de -->
* **translateMode requires exactly one resolved language, not synced to cloud**: \`translate\_mode\` setting (SETTINGS\_KEYS.translateMode) only makes sense when \`language !== "auto"\`: desktop disables the Switch when language is \`"auto"\` (settings.tsx:1005) and auto-resets translate to false when language is reset to auto (settings.tsx:533). Server's \`getTranslateModeSetting()\` (lib/language.ts:50-56) short-circuits to \`false\` if no language is set. Translate is sent to cloud ONLY on the streaming \`/v2/stream\` WS start message (providers/freestyle-cloud.ts:113, only when both \`translate\` and \`language\` are truthy) — the batch \`/v2/transcribe\` path never sends translate at all. \`translate\_mode\` is absent from preferences-sync.ts's FIELD\_MAP, so it does not sync across devices/cloud (unlike \`language\` which does).

<!-- lore:019faf4f-7dfa-7d82-abff-1140d5a54e9b -->
* **Two-token auth system: cloud session token (server-only) vs server bearer token (renderer-exposed)**: Desktop app has two distinct auth tokens with different exposure models. (1) Cloud Session Token (OAuth access\_token+refresh\_token from Freestyle Cloud sign-in): stored server-side in SQLite \`sessions\` table (single row, id=1) via apps/server/src/lib/sessions.ts; managed entirely by the embedded Hono server process; NEVER exposed to renderer/IPC — deliberate security boundary since renderer loads web content (plugins, remote pages) that could exfiltrate it via XSS or a malicious plugin. GET /api/auth/status returns only {authenticated, user}; token fields are always stripped before serialization. (2) Server Token (bearer auth for the configured local/remote Freestyle server): stored in settings.json (not SQLite, not SETTINGS\_KEYS), managed by Electron main process, deliberately exposed to renderer via \`server:token\` IPC since it's user-configured and needed for direct API/WS calls (WS uses ?token= query param since browsers can't set Authorization headers on upgrade).

<!-- lore:019fb9b5-15a1-7858-9a7c-8045079ff2a4 -->
* **un-memoized derivations in use-models.ts fixed with useMemo**: Memoized expensive derivations in \`renderer/src/pages/models/use-models.ts\`: \`buildSettingsVoiceItems\`, \`groupByProvider\`, \`Set\` constructions moved into \`useMemo\` with stable deps. This resolves a performance medium noted in the audit and prevents repeated recomputation on every render. No behavior change — outputs are identical; only caching improved.

<!-- lore:019fd642-e194-7cfa-a480-7ddcec63a064 -->
* **UpdateBanner capped at max-w-5xl (shell.tsx:350), non-dismissible by design**: UpdateBanner in apps/electron/src/renderer/src/shell.tsx:350 uses \`w-\[calc(100%-3rem)] max-w-5xl self-center\` — wide but NOT fully unbounded. An initial fix (PR #555) removed max-w-2xl entirely for a full-width banner; user rejected that in favor of capping at max-w-5xl while keeping the 1.5rem side gutters. The banner has no close/'Later' button (update-banner.tsx) — non-dismissible/blocking is an explicit user design choice, not an oversight. When widening this or similar blocking UI elements, prefer a capped max-width over removing the max-width constraint entirely, and preserve the non-dismissible behavior unless told otherwise.

<!-- lore:019fd575-d8f0-760e-97b5-955fedf5f7c3 -->
* **UpdateBanner layout: full-width via removing max-w-2xl in shell.tsx**: UpdateBanner (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/components/update-banner.tsx) is rendered in shell.tsx (~line 350) wrapped in a div with classes \`relative z-50 mt-14 w-\[calc(100%-3rem)] max-w-2xl self-center\`. To make it span full content width, remove only \`max-w-2xl\` — keep \`w-\[calc(100%-3rem)]\` (gutter), \`mt-14\`, \`z-50\`, \`self-center\`. No internal changes needed since UpdateBanner's flex layout already stretches to its container. The banner has no close/dismiss affordance by design — it's intentionally non-dismissible/blocking until the user acts (download/restart). Its \`z-50\` sits visually below the topbar's \`z-40\` glass bar only because of the \`mt-14\` vertical offset, avoiding overlap with the GitHub/Discord icons in the top-right corner.

<!-- lore:019fd574-fbd2-7781-a258-3d8ab76a6f66 -->
* **UpdateBanner mounted in shell.tsx, width gated by max-w-2xl wrapper**: The updater UI (\`update-banner.tsx\`, apps/electron/src/renderer/src/components/) is rendered inside \`shell.tsx\` (~line 350) with the width controlled entirely by the WRAPPER div's className, not the component itself: \`w-\[calc(100%-3rem)] max-w-2xl self-center mt-14 z-50\`. To make the banner full-width, drop \`max-w-2xl\` and keep the rest — the component's internal flex layout already stretches to fill its container. The banner sits at \`z-50\` but visually below the topbar's \`z-40\` GitHub/Discord icons because of the \`mt-14\` top offset, so widening it doesn't clash with those icons. There is no dismiss/'Later' affordance in the component — it is non-dismissible/blocking by default already.

<!-- lore:019fd57e-a5f1-751d-a551-6bde3b583cb4 -->
* **UpdateBanner width set by shell.tsx wrapper, not component itself**: UpdateBanner (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/components/update-banner.tsx) is width-agnostic internally — its layout uses \`flex justify-between\` which stretches to fill whatever parent width it's given. Actual width/max-width is controlled entirely by the wrapping div's className in shell.tsx (~line 350, e.g. \`w-\[calc(100%-3rem)] max-w-2xl self-center\`). To change banner width (e.g. make it full-width), edit the shell.tsx wrapper className, not update-banner.tsx. The banner also has no close/"Later" affordance by design — it's non-dismissible, confirmed as intentional when making it full-width blocking (PR #555).

<!-- lore:019fd5b7-b225-7b2e-bf0e-c18ffa185bb5 -->
* **UpdateBanner width set via shell.tsx:350 max-w class**: The dashboard UpdateBanner (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/components/update-banner.tsx) is rendered in shell.tsx:350, wrapped in a div whose Tailwind classes control its width: \`w-\[calc(100%-3rem)]\` is the fixed 1.5rem side-gutter, while a \`max-w-\*\` class caps overall width (was \`max-w-2xl\`, widened to \`max-w-5xl\` per user request in PR #555 for a fuller-width banner). The banner is intentionally non-dismissible (no close/'Later' affordance) — its internal layout is \`justify-between\`, so version text and action button auto-spread to container edges without any change needed inside update-banner.tsx itself when resizing the outer wrapper.

<!-- lore:019fd580-6f41-7f9d-8e10-f4571d4288d0 -->
* **UpdateBanner: shell.tsx renders it, main/index.ts fires update events**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/components/update-banner.tsx listens to window.api events (onUpdateAvailable, onUpdateDownloading, onUpdateDownloaded, onUpdateError) fired from apps/electron/src/main/index.ts (restartAndUpdate/updateReady logic ~line 2083). It's rendered inside shell.tsx alongside the aside nav and glass-topbar. As of Aug 2026 (PR #555) the banner spans the full content width (\`w-\[calc(100%-3rem)]\` gutter, no \`max-w-\*\` cap) and is non-dismissible by design — no close/'Later' affordance exists. When touching update UI, check both files: main/index.ts owns event emission, update-banner.tsx owns rendering, shell.tsx owns layout/width.

<!-- lore:019f57a4-9357-7385-98bf-635c264ed5b7 -->
* **Usage query staleTime: 1 hour, manual refresh only — never auto-invalidated**: The \`useCloudUsage\` React Query hook (\`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/use-cloud-usage.ts\`) uses \`staleTime: ONE\_HOUR\` and is \*\*never invalidated\*\* anywhere in the codebase. Usage data is only refreshed on manual refetch (the Refresh button in the UI) or after the 1-hour TTL. This is a deliberate design choice: \`onTranscriptionDone\` only invalidates \`\['today-history']\` and \`\['history']\`, not usage. There is no branching based on local vs cloud models — the query is simply never auto-refreshed.

<!-- lore:019fd751-eecb-75fd-ad33-e2b1a847df81 -->
* **use-dismissible.ts hook — SQLite-backed, per-key write serialization**: useDismissible(key) (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/hooks/use-dismissible.ts) backs in-app dismissible dialogs/banners (changelogs, feature prompts, tutorial heroes) via GET/PUT/DELETE /api/dismissed-notifications/:key, stored in the same SQLite DB as settings/vocabulary — so dismissals are shared across every renderer pointed at that server, not per-device. Cached under queryKeys.dismissedNotifications. Writes are serialized per-key via writeQueues/writeGenerations Maps to prevent races between rapid dismiss→reset calls: optimistic cache update fires immediately, but rollback on failure only applies if writeGenerations.get(key) still matches the request's generation, so a stale failure never reverts an unrelated concurrent mutation. Invalid/empty keys are no-ops. Known keys enumerated in KNOWN\_NOTIFICATION\_KEYS (packages/validations/src/notifications.ts), e.g. TODAY\_TUTORIAL\_HERO = "today.tutorial\_hero".

<!-- lore:019fd6a4-7655-7d9a-9e9b-2e093b4744a9 -->
* **useCloudAuth/CloudAuthProvider auth-state source of truth**: auth-context.tsx: CloudAuthProvider (wraps the whole Electron app in dashboard.tsx) calls refreshInternal() → getClient().api.auth.status.$get(), sets user from \`data.user ?? null\`. useCloudAuth() exposes {user, loading, signingIn, error, sessionExpired, refresh, signIn}; throws if used outside the provider. Mount does a retry-loop (up to 15 polls) until the server is reachable before clearing loading, then revalidates on window 'focus' and every 5 minutes. login-gate.tsx's LoginGate reads {user, loading}: loading→AuthLoadingFrame, !user→LoginPage (SignInSplit+SignInButton), else renders children. This is the single source of truth for gating any authenticated route.

<!-- lore:019f5f46-eb99-7587-9b11-f9ff372445d3 -->
* **useCloudUsage hook: 1-hour cache, manual refresh only, no post-transcription invalidation**: \`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/use-cloud-usage.ts\` (57 lines). Query key \`\["cloud-usage"]\`, \`staleTime: ONE\_HOUR\`, \`enabled: signedIn\`, \`retry: 1\`. Returns \`{ balance, updatedAt, isFetching, refresh }\`. Explicitly does NOT refetch after every transcription — \`onTranscriptionDone\` in \`today.tsx\` only invalidates \`\["today-history"]\`, never \`\["cloud-usage"]\`. Consumers surface a manual refresh button (\`aria-label="Refresh cloud usage"\`). \`usagePercent(balance)\` helper: returns 0 if \`limit===0\`, else \`Math.round(((limit - remaining) / limit) \* 100)\`. Server route: \`GET /api/usage\` reads session token via \`getSessionToken()\`, calls \`fetchCloudUsage(token)\` (10s timeout); 401 if no token, 502 on failure. \`GET /usage\` also doubles as TLS prewarm endpoint in \`prewarmFreestyleCloudConnection()\` (5s timeout, fire-and-forget).

<!-- lore:019fd6e7-79dd-7571-a6f9-3bce06098f39 -->
* **useDismissible + tutorial hero dismiss pattern (history.tsx vs remix.tsx)**: Dismissible in-app UI (tutorial banners, feature nudges) uses \`useDismissible(key)\` (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/hooks/use-dismissible.ts), backed by GET/PUT/DELETE \`/api/dismissed-notifications/:key\` against the same SQLite DB as settings — shared across every renderer pointed at that server. Keys live in \`KNOWN\_NOTIFICATION\_KEYS\` (packages/validations/src/notifications.ts, e.g. \`TODAY\_TUTORIAL\_HERO = "today.tutorial\_hero"\`). history.tsx's tutorial hero (X close button ~line 576-587) layers this server-backed dismissal on top of a legacy localStorage flag (\`usePersistentState("today.heroDismissed")\`) via a one-time migration effect, since dismissal predates the server-backed system. Writes are serialized per-key with generation counters so a dismiss→reset race can't roll back an unrelated concurrent mutation. remix.tsx's tutorial demo (RemixDemo) explicitly comments that its UI 'mirrors' history.tsx's tutorial/stats UI but has NO dismiss button and shares none of this logic — to make it dismissible, add a new key to notifications.ts and replicate history.tsx's wiring.

<!-- lore:019fd6c1-32c3-7f66-ad33-2cc6e5355cf4 -->
* **useDismissible hook — dismissed-notifications persistence pattern**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/hooks/use-dismissible.ts exports useDismissible(key), backed by GET/PUT/DELETE /api/dismissed-notifications/:key — SQLite-backed (same DB as settings/vocabulary), so dismissals are shared across every renderer pointed at that server. Cached under queryKeys.dismissedNotifications via React Query; writes are serialized per-key via writeQueues/writeGenerations Maps so rapid dismiss→reset doesn't race — rollback on failure only reverts if the generation still matches ('never an unrelated concurrent mutation'). Keys are centrally defined in packages/validations/src/notifications.ts as KNOWN\_NOTIFICATION\_KEYS (e.g. TODAY\_TUTORIAL\_HERO = "today.tutorial\_hero"). history.tsx additionally keeps a legacy usePersistentState localStorage flag ("today.heroDismissed") with a one-time migration effect that calls persistHeroDismissal() when the legacy flag is "1" but the server dismissal isn't set yet — this dual-layer pattern exists for backward-compat with pre-server-sync installs.

<!-- lore:019fd6be-7b88-7ff9-b913-89b9bd29183c -->
* **useDismissible hook — server-backed dismissible notifications**: useDismissible(key) (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/hooks/use-dismissible.ts) backs dismissible in-app UI (tutorial cards, banners, nudges) via GET/PUT/DELETE /api/dismissed-notifications/:key, stored in the same SQLite DB as settings — dismissals are shared across every renderer pointed at that server. Keys come from KNOWN\_NOTIFICATION\_KEYS registry (packages/validations/src/notifications.ts, e.g. TODAY\_TUTORIAL\_HERO = 'today.tutorial\_hero'). dismiss()/reset() do optimistic cache updates (queryKeys.dismissedNotifications) with per-key write serialization (writeQueues/writeGenerations Maps) so rapid dismiss→reset calls can't race; rollback only reverts if the write generation still matches, avoiding reverting an unrelated concurrent mutation. history.tsx (the 'Transcriptions' tab) additionally keeps a legacy localStorage flag ('today.heroDismissed' via usePersistentState) with a one-time migration effect that calls the server dismiss function if the legacy flag is set but server dismissal isn't recorded yet.

<!-- lore:019fd6b5-5d8d-740d-9774-65c9fd111d23 -->
* **useDismissible hook — server-backed notification dismissal**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/hooks/use-dismissible.ts wraps GET/PUT/DELETE /api/dismissed-notifications/:key, backed by the same SQLite DB as settings/vocabulary — dismissals are shared across every renderer pointed at that server. Keys are registered in packages/validations/src/notifications.ts as KNOWN\_NOTIFICATION\_KEYS (e.g. TODAY\_TUTORIAL\_HERO = "today.tutorial\_hero"). Cached via queryKeys.dismissedNotifications \[\[019fd23b-3e39-7d2f-b4ba-f832811ca013]]. dismiss()/reset() optimistically update the cache then enqueue PUT/DELETE, serialized per-key via writeQueues/writeGenerations Maps; on failure, rollback only reverts if writeGenerations.get(key) still matches the request's generation, so a rollback never clobbers an unrelated concurrent mutation on the same key. Keys are centralized in \`KNOWN\_NOTIFICATION\_KEYS\` (packages/validations/src/notifications.ts), e.g. TODAY\_TUTORIAL\_HERO = "today.tutorial\_hero". Writes are serialized per-key via writeQueues/writeGenerations Maps to prevent rapid dismiss→reset races; on failure, optimistic-update rollback only reverts if the generation still matches (comment: "never an unrelated concurrent mutation"). history.tsx's tutorial hero additionally keeps a legacy \`usePersistentState("today.heroDismissed")\` localStorage flag with a one-time migration effect pushing it into the server-backed store. remix.tsx's RemixDemo tutorial has NO dismiss button — not yet wired to this pattern at all.

<!-- lore:019fd763-9fc7-7beb-be99-4418305c376e -->

Backed by GET/PUT/DELETE /api/dismissed-notifications/:key, stored in the same SQLite DB as settings/vocabulary — so dismissals are shared across every renderer pointed at that server, not per-machine. Keys come from KNOWN\_NOTIFICATION\_KEYS (packages/validations/src/notifications.ts, e.g. TODAY\_TUTORIAL\_HERO = "today.tutorial\_hero"). Backed by GET/PUT/DELETE /api/dismissed-notifications/:key, stored in the local server's SQLite DB (same DB as settings/vocabulary), so dismissals are shared across every renderer pointed at that server. Optimistic cache updates via queryKeys.dismissedNotifications, with per-key write serialization (writeQueues/writeGenerations Maps) so rapid dismiss→reset calls don't race — rollback on failure only reverts if the write generation for that key still matches, never an unrelated concurrent mutation. Valid keys come from KNOWN\_NOTIFICATION\_KEYS in packages/validations/src/notifications.ts (e.g. TODAY\_TUTORIAL\_HERO = "today.tutorial\_hero"); invalid/empty keys are no-ops. Reuse this hook (not localStorage) for any new dismissible card.

Keys are registered in packages/validations/src/notifications.ts KNOWN\_NOTIFICATION\_KEYS (e.g. TODAY\_TUTORIAL\_HERO = "today.tutorial\_hero"). Keys are registered in KNOWN\_NOTIFICATION\_KEYS (packages/validations/src/notifications.ts, e.g. TODAY\_TUTORIAL\_HERO="today.tutorial\_hero"). <!-- lore:019fd6c2-dece-7537-8311-8b9b8fe47ca8 -->

Keys come from KNOWN\_NOTIFICATION\_KEYS in packages/validations/src/notifications.ts (e.g. TODAY\_TUTORIAL\_HERO = "today.tutorial\_hero"). <!-- lore:019fd6d2-e624-772f-92fe-34d0759a8c89 -->
* **useDismissible hook: SQLite-backed dismissible notification pattern**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/hooks/use-dismissible.ts backs in-app dismissible UI (tutorial cards, changelogs, feature nudges) via GET/PUT/DELETE /api/dismissed-notifications/:key, stored in the same SQLite DB as settings/vocabulary — so dismissals are shared across every renderer pointed at that server, not per-machine. Keys are enumerated in packages/validations/src/notifications.ts KNOWN\_NOTIFICATION\_KEYS (e.g. TODAY\_TUTORIAL\_HERO = "today.tutorial\_hero"). Writes are serialized per-key via writeQueues/writeGenerations Maps: dismiss()/reset() optimistically update the React Query cache (queryKeys.dismissedNotifications), cancel in-flight queries, then fire PUT/DELETE; on failure, rollback only applies if writeGenerations.get(key) still matches the request's generation, so a rollback never reverts an unrelated concurrent mutation to the same key. history.tsx's tutorial hero (lines ~576-587) also keeps a legacy usePersistentState("today.heroDismissed") localStorage flag for backward compat, migrated once into the server-backed dismissal via a migrationAttemptedRef effect.

<!-- lore:019fd3b2-6c4b-765a-8e2d-27cb9daaac4a -->
* **useEndpointConnect / EndpointConnectState shared custom-endpoint testing hook**: apps/electron/.../models/use-endpoint-connect.ts defines EndpointConnectState (testing, connected, error, models: string\[], test(), clearStatus) and EndpointConnectConfig (urlKey, apiKeyKey, defaultUrl, probe fn). runEndpointTest() strips trailing slashes, persists URL/API key via client.api.settings\[':key'].$put or $delete, calls config.probe(client, {url, api\_key}), and on success stores result.models plus triggers onDone() (data refresh). use-models.ts instantiates two per-endpoint instances: m.localLlm (local-llm, probes local server) and m.openaiStt (openai-stt, probes POST /api/settings/openai-stt/test). Both feed shared EndpointConnectForm UI (model-list.tsx) via LocalLlmConnect/OpenaiSttConnect wrappers. Any new custom-endpoint UI (e.g. a dedicated 'Custom endpoint' picker option) should reuse this hook/config pattern rather than duplicating test/persist logic.

<!-- lore:019f3cb4-43d2-73a0-acc3-c619fd2cf988 -->
* **v2/transcribe vs v2/post-process: appAssignments schema asymmetry (JSON string vs real array)**: \`/v2/transcribe\` receives \`appAssignments\` as a JSON-encoded string (parsed via \`parseCleanupAppAssignments\` in the route handler). \`/v2/post-process\` receives \`appAssignments\` as a real array (\`cleanupAppAssignmentsSchema\`). Desktop's \`appendCleanupFormFields\` serializes to string for transcribe; \`postProcessWithFreestyleCloud\` sends the array directly for post-process. Similarly, \`customPrompt\` in post-process must be \`|| undefined\` (not \`?? null\`) because \`postProcessV2BodySchema\` uses \`.optional()\` not \`.nullish()\`, while \`appContext\` uses \`.nullish()\` and correctly accepts null. These schema asymmetries between the two v2 routes are a recurring source of payload bugs.

<!-- lore:019f4680-6d79-76d9-8142-fdad34b6aa72 -->
* **Vercel AI SDK usage: both cloud and desktop use ai@^6 + @ai-sdk/groq@^3**: Both cloud (\`packages/stt\`) and desktop (\`apps/server\`) use \`ai@^6.0.191\` and \`@ai-sdk/groq@^3.0.39\`. Cloud \`packages/stt\`: uses \`experimental\_transcribe\` from \`"ai"\` in \`transcribe.ts\`; \`generateText\` from \`"ai"\` in \`post-process.ts\`; \`createGroq\`/\`GroqLanguageModelOptions\` from \`@ai-sdk/groq\`. Desktop additionally has \`@ai-sdk/anthropic@^3.0.79\`, \`@ai-sdk/elevenlabs@^2.0.33\`, \`@ai-sdk/google@^3.0.79\`, \`@ai-sdk/mistral@^3.0.37\`, \`@ai-sdk/openai@^3.0.65\`.

<!-- lore:019f240c-9973-77a6-b821-f300a6d4bba7 -->
* **Voice Commands plugin: design decisions — hybrid detection, tool-calling agent, LLM proxy**: Voice Commands plugin at \`plugins/voice-commands/\` (\`@freestyle-voice/commands@0.0.1\`). Key decisions: (1) \*\*Detection\*\*: hybrid — cheap deterministic prefilter first, then multi-step tool-calling LLM agent. (2) \*\*Hook point\*\*: \`afterTranscribe\` — fires after STT, before LLM cleanup; empty-text suppression works free via \`deliverOutput\`. (3) \*\*LLM access\*\*: plugin routes through host LLM endpoint via \`PluginLlm\` capability on \`PluginContext\` (added to SDK); plugin never manages own credentials. (4) \*\*consumed flag\*\*: \`AfterTranscribeOutput.consumed?: boolean\` — when true, transcribe.ts/stream.ts skip cleanup and return empty text. (5) \*\*Action types v1\*\*: macOS Shortcuts (macOS-only), HTTP webhook, Open URL/app, Shell command/script. (6) \*\*Platform scope\*\*: cross-platform core, macOS Shortcuts gated. PR #379 (draft) on \`feat/voice-commands-plugin\`.

<!-- lore:019fd6e0-bdc0-722a-ba39-73a26c7866d4 -->
* **Voice model picker pipeline: PickerOption → useModels.voiceItems → buildVoiceRows**: apps/electron/.../pages/models/: reusable \`PickerOption\`/\`PickerModalHeader\` (picker-option.tsx) render tier choices inside \`TranscriptionPicker\` (voice) and \`CleanupTierPicker\` (LLM, model-list.tsx). Actual model rows come from \`useModels()\` (use-models.ts) which exposes \`voiceItems: VoiceItem\[]\` (built via \`buildSettingsVoiceItems\`/\`buildVoiceItems\` in utils.ts) and per-endpoint \`EndpointConnectState\` objects (\`openaiStt\`, \`localLlm\`) from \`useEndpointConnect()\`. \`buildVoiceRows()\`/\`Row\` construction (model-list.tsx) reads ONLY \`m.voiceItems\` — it does NOT read \`EndpointConnectState.models\`. \`EndpointConnectForm\` (model-list.tsx) is the shared URL+API-key+Test UI used by both \`LocalLlmConnect\` and \`OpenaiSttConnect\`; \`useEndpointConnect\`'s \`runEndpointTest()\` persists url/key via settings API and probes via a server route (e.g. \`/openai-stt/test\`), returning \`models: string\[]\` for display only.

<!-- lore:019fcbf3-3090-744d-83a3-89a4df61893f -->
* **Voice pill (app.tsx) animation states: data-show/data-exit + exitingRef guard**: The voice pill in apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx drives motion via CSS attribute selectors (\`data-show\`, \`.pill-card\[data-show="false"]\`, \`.pill-capsule\[data-exit="cancelled"]\`), not React-driven inline styles. Exit races (delivered/cancelled/quiet all firing near-simultaneously) are resolved by \`exitingRef\` gating \`dismissPill\` so the first ending wins — later dismiss calls are no-ops. Smooth transitions like 'settle before sweep' are implemented as a phase inside the requestAnimationFrame draw loop, not React state, to avoid re-render overhead in the animation hot path. Motion correctness is verified in tests by sampling \`getBoundingClientRect()\` across frames (e.g. bar-to-bar gap must hold exactly at a fixed px value, distinguishing outside-in collapse from a naive group-scale clump).

<!-- lore:019f1672-dce0-7a9d-a3b9-0ffd97bdc276 -->
* **Voice pill audio: startRecording tone/ducking ordering and duckingPromiseRef race fix**: In \`app.tsx\`, \`startRecording\` plays \`playTone('start')\` immediately before ducking (so tone isn't attenuated at \`DUCKED\_VOLUME=0.15\`). Ducking runs concurrently with \`getUserMedia\` via \`duckingPromiseRef.current = prepareSystemAudio(...).catch(()=>{})\`. \`restoreSystemAudioSafely()\` awaits \`duckingPromiseRef.current\` then calls IPC restore — prevents cancel/commit racing a still-in-flight duck subprocess leaving volume stuck at 15%. \`commitRecording\` IIFE: \`await restoreSystemAudioSafely()\` then \`playTone('stop')\`. \*\*Critical guard\*\*: restore is gated on \`duckingPromiseRef.current\` (whether this session actually ducked), NOT on \`\_audioPlaybackMode\` — checking current mode is a regression trap because user can toggle duck→off mid-session, causing commit to skip restore and strand volume at ~15%. All 6 restore call sites route through \`restoreSystemAudioSafely\`.

<!-- lore:019fcbea-629b-77fe-8107-408f889742b0 -->
* **Voice pill motion architecture: renderer-driven, settle-in-draw-loop**: specs/voice-pill-motion.md (apps/electron voice pill) documents: animations are renderer-driven — there is always a corresponding handler on the main-process side to execute what the renderer requests (no fire-and-forget UI states). The settle-before-sweep transition (letting the last live audio level settle before switching to the idle sweep) is implemented as a phase inside the requestAnimationFrame draw loop, not React state, to avoid re-render overhead in the animation hot path. Rejected: animating a disc around the checkmark via a width transition — rejected because animating width forces a full layout pass; kept to opacity/transform-only animations instead.

<!-- lore:019f14f1-8ab1-795c-8f1d-e04dafa02ef7 -->
* **Voice pill programmaticTarget: suppresses spurious move events after setPosition calls**: \`programmaticTarget\` and \`programmaticCleanupTimer\` (declared at \`index.ts:323–324\`) track in-flight programmatic window moves. \`markProgrammaticTarget(x,y)\` sets the target and arms a 1-second safety timeout to clear it in case the OS never delivers a settle event. \`setProgrammaticPosition(win,x,y)\` calls \`win.setPosition()\` then \`markProgrammaticTarget()\`. The \`move\` listener (lines 463–508) ignores events while \`programmaticTarget\` is set, preventing manual-drag saves from firing after \`showPill()\` repositions the window. \`repositionPillForDisplayChange()\` (lines 1677–1689) re-clamps the pill via \`getAppWindowPosition()\` + \`setProgrammaticPosition()\` on \`display-removed\` and \`display-metrics-changed\` events.

<!-- lore:019f14f1-8aa8-75ae-925e-b7684ba88e2f -->
* **Voice pill window positioning: getAppWindowPosition() hardcodes primary display — fix uses cursor screen**: Always investigate root cause thoroughly before making any code changes. When given a GitHub issue or bug report, the user expects a complete read-only investigation phase first — identifying exact file paths, line numbers, root causes, and a fix plan — before any code is modified. User directive (stated explicitly): 'I need a thorough investigation. DO NOT make any changes.' Only after investigation is complete and root cause confirmed does implementation proceed. Never jump straight to editing code.

<!-- lore:019fcc4f-7ad1-7c76-806d-71acc064b9c0 -->
* **Voice pill: .pill-surface (enter) vs .pill-capsule (exit) animation systems**: The voice pill's enter and exit animations are two independently-evolved systems that must coexist, not be unified. \`.pill-surface\` (blur+rise+overshoot via \`--pill-rise\`/\`riseBy()\`) is the canonical ENTER/summon animation, added by the remix-prototype branch. \`.pill-capsule\[data-exit=...]\` (delivered/cancelled/quiet, check-mark draw, bar-collapse) is the canonical EXIT animation, added by main. During the PR #534 merge, main also added a competing \`.pill-capsule\` enter transform (scale 0.88 + \`pill-bar-enter\` keyframes) which was deliberately stripped because remix's summon design was chosen as canonical for enter. Extend enter via \`.pill-surface\`/\`riseBy\`; extend exit via \`.pill-capsule\[data-exit]\`. Never reintroduce a \`.pill-capsule\` enter transform — it silently overrides \`.pill-surface\`'s transform due to CSS cascade order (main's rules load later).

<!-- lore:019fcc0c-5b4d-7f8b-b99b-5d85e77e7f73 -->
* **Voice pill: arrival/listening/handover/departure motion model**: apps/electron's 'voice pill' (pages/app.tsx) implements a 4-beat state machine: arrival (enter animation), listening, handover (mic→processing sweep), departure (delivered/cancelled/quiet). Spec lives in specs/voice-pill-motion.md. Key constraints: (1) all transitions are renderer-driven — every animated state change needs a corresponding JS handler to execute it; CSS-only animations aren't expected to fire unprompted. (2) Settle/sweep timing (e.g. CHECK\_LEAVE\_MS=110, handover settle 180ms) is implemented as a 'settle' phase inside the draw loop, not React state, to avoid re-render overhead in the animation loop. (3) Departure motion differs by outcome: 'delivered' collapses bars outside-in and draws a checkmark SVG (CHECK\_PATH\_LENGTH via strokeDasharray, held visible); 'cancelled' plays half the entrance animation with no checkmark.

<!-- lore:019fd3a1-d070-765e-96fd-9d5fd8fbe2dc -->
* **voiceItems is the single source of truth for voice model rows**: model-list.tsx's \`buildVoiceRows(m: UseModels, h: VoiceHandlers)\` builds ALL voice picker rows purely from \`m.voiceItems\` (apps/electron/.../use-models.ts, computed via \`buildVoiceItems\`/\`buildSettingsVoiceItems\` in utils.ts). transcription-picker.tsx, model-list.tsx, and index.tsx all read \`m.voiceItems\` as the sole model list. Any new voice source (e.g. a custom-endpoint's discovered models) must be merged into \`voiceItems\` to appear as selectable rows — separate state slices like \`EndpointConnectState.models\` (use-endpoint-connect.ts) are NOT automatically picked up by row-building.

<!-- lore:019fd6cc-4bfb-797c-a5dc-9219cd736641 -->
* **voiceItems vs EndpointConnectState.models — two unlinked model lists**: apps/electron use-models.ts exposes two separate structures for voice models that are NOT automatically linked: \`voiceItems: VoiceItem\[]\` (built by buildVoiceItems/buildSettingsVoiceItems in utils.ts, consumed by model-list.tsx's \`buildVoiceRows\` to render selectable Rows), and \`openaiStt\`/\`localLlm\`: \`EndpointConnectState\` (from \`useEndpointConnect\` in use-endpoint-connect.ts, holds probe results incl. \`models: string\[]\`, \`connected\`, \`error\`). Testing a custom/BYOK OpenAI-compatible endpoint populates \`EndpointConnectState.models\` but this is never merged into \`voiceItems\`. Any feature letting users pick a specific model discovered by an endpoint probe must explicitly bridge \`EndpointConnectState.models\` into \`voiceItems\` (or a new Row source) — they're independently maintained lists, not one derived from the other.

<!-- lore:019fd6d3-2046-74e2-b2bf-6435c5da38ae -->
* **voiceItems/buildVoiceRows pipeline — model-list.tsx, use-models.ts, transcription-picker.tsx**: Voice model rows flow through one pipeline: use-models.ts's useModels hook computes \`voiceItems: VoiceItem\[]\` (kind: local/byok/cloud) via buildSettingsVoiceItems/buildVoiceItems (utils.ts), consumed by transcription-picker.tsx (PickerOption rows), model-list.tsx's \`buildVoiceRows(m, handlers)\` (builds selectable Row\[] from \`m.voiceItems\` only), and index.tsx (find/select/delete). Custom OpenAI-compatible endpoint testing is a SEPARATE hook, \`useEndpointConnect\` (use-endpoint-connect.ts), returning \`EndpointConnectState\` with its own \`models: string\[]\` (discovered model IDs) — this array is never merged into \`voiceItems\`/Row. Any feature letting users pick a specific discovered custom-endpoint model must explicitly wire \`EndpointConnectState.models\` into the voiceItems/Row pipeline; there is no existing bridge.

<!-- lore:019ee7ed-0eb5-7c3a-9a59-edca37276c37 -->
* **Winston logger registry: configureAppFileLogging pattern for Electron**: Loggers are created at module import time (before \`app.whenReady()\`), so file transport cannot be added inside \`createAppLogger()\`. Pattern: \`packages/utils/src/logger.ts\` tracks all created loggers in a module-level \`Set\`; \`configureAppFileLogging({ filePath })\` attaches a shared \`winston.transports.File\` retroactively to all existing loggers and to future ones. \`getAppLogFilePath()\` returns the current session log path. Called from \`apps/electron/src/main/index.ts\` after \`app.whenReady()\`. Per-startup filenames: \`freestyle-YYYYMMDD-HHmmss-{pid}.log\` under \`app.getPath('logs')\`. Retain 10 most recent session logs. \`@freestyle/utils\` must NOT import Electron — path is passed in by main.

### Decision

<!-- lore:019fafcb-75f1-7dd1-a345-0a1fbe301326 -->
* **Auth/org cloud endpoints: explicit routes over generic catch-all proxy**: Decision: keep explicit per-route handlers in apps/server/src/routes/{auth,org,usage,billing}.ts calling dedicated freestyle-cloud.ts helpers, rather than a generic catch-all proxy (e.g. auth.all('/proxy/\*', ...)) that injects the bearer token and forwards raw. Rejected catch-all because only ~3-4 of 11 routes are pure forwards — most (device/token, sign-out, profile) have essential local side effects (SQLite session persistence, default model assignment, PostHog identity) or transforms (field filtering, hardcoded callback URLs) a catch-all can't express. It also loses cloudJson()'s retry-on-stale-socket logic and 401/429/5xx error classification, and would forward cloud responses to the renderer unfiltered. Each new proxied endpoint (e.g. org list/active/set-active) is only ~15 lines, so boilerplate savings don't justify losing control. See \[\[019faf91-ce07-7e39-a8f5-788aaef1692b]].

<!-- lore:019fafc1-0318-7f84-927e-10f9e4947171 -->
* **Cloud proxy routes: explicit handlers over catch-all, even for orgs feature**: When adding new Freestyle Cloud-backed features (e.g. org list/active/set-active), chose explicit per-route handlers (freestyle-cloud.ts helper + dedicated Hono route, ~15 lines each) over a generic catch-all proxy (\`auth.all('/proxy/\*', ...)\`). Of the 11 existing auth/usage/billing routes, only ~3-4 (GET /accounts, POST /unlink-account, GET /usage) are pure forwards; the rest have essential local side effects (session persistence in SQLite, default-model assignment, PostHog identity lifecycle) or response/request transforms that a catch-all would lose, along with cloudJson()'s retry logic and 401/429/5xx error classification. Rejected catch-all because it would only cover a minority of routes while making the remaining explicit routes inconsistent with the proxied ones, and would strip response filtering (e.g. account rows are trimmed to providerId only before reaching renderer). See \[\[019faf91-ce07-7e39-a8f5-788aaef1692b]].

<!-- lore:019fb9ae-e04e-7170-aa00-211df5437ff5 -->
* **Cloud-sync user configuration: merge-with-cloud-wins policy**: Decision: implement bidirectional cloud sync for portable user configuration (Dictionary + Vocabulary + Tone + portable settings). Cloud wins on conflict (last-write-wins merged by server). Exclude api\_keys, machine-specific settings (hotkeys, model paths, proxy/CA), and non-portable state. Persist server-side in cloud DB (better-auth session scoping). Desktop only stores a last-synced timestamp and triggers background sync; no local conflict resolution UI. Privacy: default opt-in with clear disclosure; disable toggle stops cloud writes but allows reads. Implementation owned by cloud service (separate repo); desktop adds minimal sync trigger and settings UI.

<!-- lore:019fd3b2-6c2e-709d-96b9-960b3d4cff63 -->
* **Custom endpoint models route through openai/\<model-id> override, not new provider**: Root cause of 'Test connection shows N models found but nothing selectable' bug: model-list.tsx's buildVoiceRows() builds picker Row\[] purely from m.voiceItems (use-models.ts), never from EndpointConnectState.models — the array populated by runEndpointTest() after a successful /openai-stt/test probe. So testing a custom OpenAI-compatible endpoint persists base\_url/api\_key and shows 'Connected · N models found' but the active voice model never changes. Planned fix (Aug 2026, in progress): extract a dedicated 'Custom endpoint' PickerOption (voice only, per user choice) with a new view="custom", render EndpointConnectState.models as selectable rows, and on select configure \`openai/\<model-id>\` — reusing the existing \`openai\` provider's baseURL override (openai.ts) rather than adding a new provider, since streaming/utils.ts's stripProviderPrefix already routes \`openai/\<id>\` correctly through it.

<!-- lore:019fd70c-f982-7ec0-b690-2debcd535cb1 -->
* **Custom endpoint picker option split from BYOK 'Your API key' (voice only)**: Decision: extract the custom OpenAI-compatible endpoint form (OpenaiSttConnect) out of the 'Your API key' (BYOK) PickerOption in transcription-picker.tsx into its own dedicated 'Custom endpoint' PickerOption with a new voiceView='custom' state (extends the 'tiers'|'all'|'local'|'cloud' union in model-modal.tsx). Scoped to voice only for now — CleanupTierPicker (LLM cleanup) custom-endpoint support deferred, per explicit user choice. Discovered models render as selectable rows; selecting one configures \`openai/\<discovered-id>\`, reusing the existing 'openai' provider's baseURL override (server streaming/providers/openai.ts) rather than a new provider — confirmed compatible via stripProviderPrefix in server streaming/utils.ts.

<!-- lore:019fd6f6-bd31-7835-9300-92c57ec4beb0 -->

Decisions: (1) discovered models list as selectable rows, not just informational text — rejected leaving the list read-only; (2) voice-only for now — CleanupTierPicker (LLM cleanup) custom-endpoint support deferred, rejected doing both simultaneously; (3) selecting a discovered model configures it as \`openai/\<model-id>\`, reusing the existing \`openai\` provider's baseURL override (OpenAITranscriptionProvider, server streaming/providers) rather than a new dedicated provider — confirmed viable because transcription's \`stripProviderPrefix()\` strips the 'openai/' prefix before routing, so any discovered id round-trips correctly.

Scoped voice-only for now — CleanupTierPicker (LLM) custom-endpoint support is deferred, not built. After a successful Test probe, discovered model IDs will be rendered as selectable rows (not just 'N models found' text); selecting one configures the model as \`openai/\<model-id>\`, reusing the existing \`openai\` streaming provider's baseURL override (OpenAITranscriptionProvider reads openai\_stt\_base\_url/openai\_stt\_api\_key) rather than adding a new provider — rejected alternative: a dedicated custom-endpoint provider, unneeded since stripProviderPrefix already makes \`openai/\<id>\` route correctly through the overridden client.

<!-- lore:019fd398-c90a-7c19-b195-f6d6a9b620a5 -->
* **Custom STT endpoint models addressed as openai/\<id> via provider base-URL override**: Decided discovered models from a custom OpenAI-compatible endpoint test will be configured as \`openai/\<discovered-id>\` — reusing the existing \`openai\` streaming provider (openai.ts \`OpenAITranscriptionProvider\`, which already reads \`openai\_stt\_base\_url\`/\`openai\_stt\_api\_key\` settings and builds \`createOpenAI({baseURL})\` when set) — rather than introducing a new provider ID. Rationale: \`stripProviderPrefix()\` (server/streaming/utils.ts) strips the \`openai/\` prefix before routing, so any discovered model id round-trips correctly with zero new provider plumbing. Scope decision: voice/transcription only for now (user chose 'voice only for now (Recommended)'); LLM cleanup (\`CleanupTierPicker\`) custom-endpoint support explicitly deferred.

<!-- lore:019fc2c4-8a0e-79f5-a5af-35d1f5d1438c -->
* **Desktop stopped sending synced cleanup prefs to Freestyle Cloud**: Since cloud now reads/merges stored member\_preferences, desktop (apps/server) stopped sending fields that are cloud-synced: intensity, customPrompt, tones (personalTone/workTone/emailTone/overallTone), appAssignments, and the user's saved (non-overridden) languages/vocabulary — on transcribe, post-process, AND streaming-start requests to Freestyle Cloud. Still sent inline every request (never synced/stored, request-scoped only): appContext, systemFragments (plugin-derived), skipPostProcess/mode, translate (local setting, not in FIELD\_MAP), and per-request beforeTranscribe plugin overrides of language/vocabulary (only saved defaults are synced, overrides never are). Removed CloudCleanupTones/appendCleanupFormFields from freestyle-cloud.ts. Changed: lib/freestyle-cloud.ts, routes/transcribe.ts, lib/post-process.ts, routes/stream.ts, lib/streaming/{types,providers/freestyle-cloud}.ts. apps/mobile has its own independent StreamCleanupPreferences and was NOT touched.

<!-- lore:019fc2d6-ccf7-7b47-a54e-8905ca76b7a6 -->
* **Desktop/mobile drop synced cleanup prefs from cloud payloads**: Aug 2026 decision: apps/server (desktop) and apps/mobile stopped sending intensity, customPrompt, tone fields (personalTone/workTone/emailTone/overallTone), appAssignments, and saved languages/vocabulary in Freestyle Cloud transcribe/post-process/stream requests. Rationale: these are now synced to the cloud's member\_preferences row (via preferences-sync.ts FIELD\_MAP + vocabulary push), and the cloud merges payload ?? stored ?? default per-field \[\[019fc2c7-f09b-775b-a268-04664b9796c3]], so re-sending them is redundant. Still sent inline (never synced, must be per-request): appContext, systemFragments (plugin-derived), skipPostProcess/mode, translate (local-only setting), and any beforeTranscribe plugin override of language/vocabulary (overrides aren't persisted). Rejected alternative: threading an explicit override-vs-saved flag through the streaming provider to distinguish cases — too invasive; instead the raw-cloud transcribe path always forwards opts.language since it's harmless when redundant.

<!-- lore:019fb9ae-e0ae-75f4-8706-4226ff884c49 -->
* **Do not move existing SQLite settings to cloud — keep local-only settings local**: Decision: keep existing SQLite-backed settings (serverUrl, token, disabled\_plugins, etc.) local-only and do NOT sync them to the cloud. Those settings are machine-specific and should remain local. Only the explicitly allowlisted portable settings (dic+voc+tone) are synced. This avoids leaking secrets and preserves per-machine configuration.

<!-- lore:019fd730-8be6-7e69-8e34-0ce0e7e5b751 -->
* **Fix for stale expanded Remix session: force endRemix on reuse, not a second client timer**: To close the gap where an expanded, left-open RemixChat bypasses the server's idle-freshness check on reopen \[see companion gotcha entry], the chosen fix is: track a \`lastRemixActivityAt\` ref bumped on message send/onFinish, and in the reuse branch (app.tsx:2602-2616) force \`endRemix({hide:false})\` before reopening if idle > REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS, so RemixChat unmounts+remounts and re-triggers its server-freshness check. Rejected alternative: add a second client-side \`setTimeout(onClose, 20\*60\*1000)\` mirroring MINI\_SETTLED\_DISMISS\_MS — rejected because it introduces a second idle constant that can drift out of sync with the server's REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS (remix-store.ts:8), duplicating the source of truth instead of reusing it.

<!-- lore:019fb9ab-782b-790b-8b9c-aab4ceecaab5 -->
* **fix/llm-settings-type-and-timeout-constant**: Applied two fixes in apps/server/src/routes/models.ts: (1) Added Record\<string, string | undefined> annotation to the settings lookup to preserve type safety on key accesses. (2) Replaced the duplicated timeout literal with REGISTRY\_FETCH\_TIMEOUT\_MS constant (3000) for consistency and maintainability. Changes are backward compatible and all checks (typecheck, lint, server tests) pass.

<!-- lore:019fbc9b-3618-7c35-a0ab-730e1dc11a01 -->
* **Freestyle Cloud: languages\[] supersedes legacy language field**: In the Freestyle Cloud backend repo (/Users/am/dev/freestyle-voice/cloud, apps/server — distinct from the desktop's local embedded server), \`languages: string\[]\` is canonical everywhere: DB column \`member\_preferences.languages\` (json, nullable, no default — migration 0014 renamed it from singular \`language\`), validation (\`z.array(z.string().max(10)).max(5)\`), and transcribe/post-process/streaming payloads. Singular \`language\` survives only as \`@deprecated\` legacy input, coalesced into \`languages\` at every entry point (transcribe.ts, post-process.ts, stt-session.ts) via \`payloadLanguages ?? (legacyLanguage ? \[legacyLanguage] : undefined)\`, each tagged \`TODO(legacy): drop once all clients send languages\` (~1 month removal target). \`resolvePreferences()\` precedence: request payload wins when defined, falls back to stored member preference only when payload is undefined — never merged. \`languages\` uses PATCH replace semantics (\`JSON\_REPLACE\_FIELDS\`), unlike \`vocabulary\` which deep-merges.

<!-- lore:019fd6e7-bc33-729c-94e9-65df931c8021 -->
* **get\_tones added as REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS client tool, not server tool**: Remix agent's tone-config access (get\_tones) was implemented as a client tool in REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS (packages/validations/src/remix.ts), executed in the Electron renderer via getClient().api.settings.$get() + validations parsers — not as a server tool with execute(). Rejected: a server tool calling getEffectiveCleanupTones() in-process would only work for the BYOK path (runRemixAgentLocally); on the default Freestyle Cloud path the agent runs on a cloud Worker (separate repo) that cannot read the desktop's local SQLite settings, so the tool would be invisible for Cloud users. A client tool executes in the renderer regardless of which backend runs the agent loop, and the renderer always reaches the local server (which always runs), so it works identically on both paths — same pattern as get\_context/get\_clipboard. Deliberately excludes cleanup\_app\_assignments; agent picks destination tone by judgement instead of explicit app routing.

<!-- lore:019fd6d9-e2d6-74fd-8fa1-aeaf6b96e31a -->
* **get\_tones is a client tool, not server tool, for Cloud/BYOK parity**: Adding get\_tones (Remix agent reads tone/cleanup settings): rejected a server tool with \`execute\` calling getEffectiveCleanupTones() in-process, because that only works on the BYOK path (runRemixAgentLocally runs in-process) — on the default Freestyle Cloud path the agent loop runs on a separate cloud Worker that cannot reach the desktop's local SQLite settings, making the tool invisible for Cloud users. Chose a CLIENT tool instead (declared in REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS, no execute): the renderer's executeTool() reads settings via getClient().api.settings.$get() against the always-running local server, working identically on both paths since the renderer always talks to the local server regardless of where the agent loop executes. Scope deliberately excludes cleanup\_app\_assignments (routing) — returns only intensity + 4 destination tones + custom prompt — per explicit user request.

<!-- lore:019fd6d3-b151-7956-9cb8-5393a88fdf96 -->
* **get\_tones Remix tool implemented as client tool, not server tool**: Added \`get\_tones\` to REMIX\_CLIENT\_TOOLS so the Remix agent can read the user's saved cleanup/tone prefs: intensity + personalTone/workTone/emailTone/overallTone + customPrompt (deliberately excludes cleanup\_app\_assignments — scope decision). Rejected: a server-side tool with \`execute\` calling getEffectiveCleanupTones() in-process — works for BYOK (runRemixAgentLocally) but is invisible on the Freestyle Cloud path since the cloud Worker can't reach the desktop's local SQLite settings, and Cloud is the default/common path. Chosen: a client tool executed in the renderer via \`getClient().api.settings.$get()\` against the always-running local server — identical on both paths since the renderer always talks to the local server regardless of where the agent loop executes. Tone values of 'off' or a failed read mean 'no preference' — agent must not invent one.

<!-- lore:019fd2db-6b55-7886-9236-d035a5d936f7 -->
* **getModelCostCached() replaces blocking getModelCost() on transcribe hot path**: post-process.ts's cost calc previously awaited async \`getModelCost()\` inline before \`postProcess()\` returned \`costUsd\` — on a models.dev registry cache-miss this made a blocking network fetch (up to REGISTRY\_FETCH\_TIMEOUT\_MS) on the user-perceived response path. Rejected fix: fully deferring cost computation to run async-after-response, patching history+analytics afterward — rejected because it changes the response contract (client currently gets costUsd synchronously) and is riskier/more invasive. Chosen fix: \`getModelCostCached()\` (synchronous, cache-only, returns null on cold/expired cache) used on the hot path, plus \`prewarmModelCostRegistry()\` (fire-and-forget) called from the existing \`POST /api/transcribe/pre-warm\` route \[\[019fb9c2-efac-7d3d-81d1-bf8317fba26c]] alongside whisper/MLX/cleanup-LLM prewarming. Tradeoff accepted: the first dictation after cold startup (before prewarm fires) records costUsd:0; subsequent dictations are correct once warm. Old async \`getModelCost()\` was deleted entirely (only production caller migrated).

<!-- lore:019fbc43-f5df-79a8-a31b-6d516c560ede -->
* **Migrated to shared client**: migrated to shared client.

<!-- lore:019fafc5-aaa2-7cc4-9b53-466e8cc15b44 -->
* **Mobile subscriptions: native IAP (expo-iap) chosen over Stripe**: Chose native In-App Purchase via expo-iap (raw StoreKit) over @stripe/stripe-react-native for iOS subscription sales because Apple mandates IAP for digital goods — Stripe is legally non-viable for this use case on iOS. Removed the Stripe web-checkout path entirely (deleted subscription.ts and the @better-auth/stripe client plugin). Trust model: client-side transaction is only finished after server-side receipt verification (POST /v1/iap/verify, implemented in Freestyle Cloud's separate service.freestylevoice.com repo) — unverified purchases are redelivered via restore rather than lost. Backend verification + store notification webhooks, and App Store Connect/Play Console product setup, are external dependencies not yet live; client shows "not available yet" until deployed. A fresh expo prebuild/EAS build is required after adding these native modules (ios/android dirs are gitignored).

<!-- lore:019fd70e-6cfe-73eb-8a3e-13d1dd83fe00 -->
* **Onboarding CloudStep auto-advances signed-in users past Continue button**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/onboarding.tsx: Step type is "permissions"|"cloud"|"language"|"draft"|"remix" but initial state is "cloud" (first screen), despite type ordering listing "permissions" first — don't assume type order = runtime order. CloudStep (~1125-1198) previously forced even already-authenticated users (via useCloudAuth()/auth-context.tsx) to click "Continue" (~1188-1191) to reach "permissions". Fixed with a one-shot guard ref \`autoAdvancedCloud\` (~line 135) plus an effect after finishSetup (~line 707): once \`!cloudLoading\` and \`step === "cloud"\` with a truthy \`cloudUser\`, it fires \`onboarding\_cloud\_step\_completed\` analytics and calls \`setStep("permissions")\` automatically, skipping the button. The guard prevents re-firing if the user navigates back to the cloud step. LoginGate (login-gate.tsx) deliberately excludes the \`/onboarding\` route so signed-out users can still reach onboarding without being redirected to the login page.

<!-- lore:019fd72d-653f-7ee1-b862-720d305dc54a -->
* **Onboarding never asks user to choose an on-device ASR model**: First-time onboarding (onboarding.tsx) auto-picks the on-device model instead of prompting a choice: Qwen3 ASR (MLX) is the hero pick when the machine can run it (RECOMMENDED\_MLX\_DEF="qwen3-0.6b-8bit"), whisper.cpp's Balanced model is the universal fallback (RECOMMENDED\_WHISPER\_DEF="small-q5\_1", builds its own binary, no Python required). Code comment (lines 104-108): rationale is that it downloads in the background while the user picks language/hotkey — "first-time users never choose a model." \`ModelSelectorOverlay\` exists for later manual override but is not part of the default first-run path. Any onboarding redesign proposing an upfront model-choice step contradicts this deliberate simplification.

<!-- lore:019efae2-43a6-73c7-8bae-eb57025f9ba6 -->
* **Package release versioning: manual explicit semver, OIDC auth, issue-label publish flow**: Chose manual explicit semver (over \`auto\` conventional-commits analysis) for SDK and plugin releases — user selects version explicitly at release time. Chose OIDC trusted publishing (over long-lived \`NPM\_TOKEN\`) for npm auth — requires one-time bootstrap publish per package. Chose reuse of existing issue-label publish flow (over a separate \`workflow\_run\` trigger) for consistency with the Electron app pipeline. \`scripts/bump-version.sh\` only bumps \`apps/electron\` — it is NOT invoked for package releases.

<!-- lore:019fd0cb-e4a7-7a47-824f-9d61f9d74559 -->
* **Pill display re-anchoring: show on cursor's display first, async re-anchor to focused window**: getAppWindowPosition() picked the display via \`screen.getDisplayNearestPoint(screen.getCursorScreenPoint())\` — correct math, wrong signal: with vertically-stacked monitors, dictating on the top monitor via keyboard while the mouse rests on the bottom monitor placed the pill at the bottom display's bottom-center (looks like 'top of bottom screen'). Rejected fix: synchronously fetching the focused window's bounds before showing the pill — all native frontmost-window lookups (macOS AppleScript, Windows PowerShell GetForegroundWindow/GetWindowRect) are async subprocess calls with 1.5-3s timeouts, which would reintroduce the exact hot-path latency Issue 1 was fixing. Chosen fix: \`showPill()\` still shows instantly on the cursor's display, then fires \`anchorPillToFocusedDisplay()\` (fire-and-forget) which resolves the focused window's display and calls \`setProgrammaticPosition\` to re-anchor only if different and the pill isn't expanded/isn't on a 'custom' position. Linux has no bounds API — falls back to cursor's display permanently.

<!-- lore:019fd2db-6b5c-7a38-95fd-0d7b0e2942e0 -->
* **Pill hot-rect 120ms cursor poll: kept, not replaced with native events**: apps/electron/src/main/index.ts's \`pillHotPollTimer\` (\`setInterval(120ms)\` calling \`screen.getCursorScreenPoint()\`) manages click-through for the pill's hot-rect — it only runs while \`showRemixCard\` is true and self-terminates via \`stopPillHotPoll()\` the instant the cursor enters the rect. On macOS/Windows, \`win.setIgnoreMouseEvents(true, {forward:true})\` already forwards mousemove to the renderer; the poll exists specifically as the Linux fallback (\`forward: process.platform !== 'linux'\`). Investigated as a perf-improvement candidate but decided AGAINST replacing with native mouse-tracking events: footprint is small/bounded/self-terminating (not actually hot-path), while a native fix would require new Linux-native mouse-tracking code plus tri-platform click-through regression testing — high implementation risk for negligible measured benefit.

<!-- lore:019f6a23-92dd-7970-9d9c-70e15e80e588 -->
* **pluginSlug() has no hash suffix — distinct package names always get distinct slugs**: Chose to remove the hash suffix from \`pluginSlug()\` in \`packages/sdk/src/ui.ts\` over (1) migration (rename existing dirs on upgrade) and (2) fallback in \`discoverPackage()\` (try without hash). Rationale: distinct npm package names always produce distinct slugs after sanitization; hash-based collision prevention was over-engineering. New behavior: \`@freestyle-voice/plugin-audio-transcription\` → \`freestyle-voice-plugin-audio-transcription\` (no \`-\<hash>\` suffix). The \`shortHash()\` FNV-1a helper (lines 81-89) was removed as unused. Callers span: \`apps/server/src/routes/plugins.ts\`, \`apps/server/src/lib/plugins/installer.ts\`, \`apps/server/src/lib/plugins/ui-assets.ts\`, \`packages/sdk/src/loader.ts\`, and multiple test files — all expect the no-hash form.

<!-- lore:019fb9f9-a345-7684-bfc6-d8203a3bc60b -->
* **Proposed shared cloud-fetch helper: inject auth/baseUrl, don't hardcode desktop/mobile specifics**: Design for a runtime-agnostic \`cloudJson\<T>(path, opts)\` shared helper (candidate location: extend \`@freestyle-voice/validations\` or new \`@freestyle-voice/cloud-client\` package) to replace duplicated fetch boilerplate in both apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts and apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/\*.ts. Key constraints: (1) auth differs — desktop uses Bearer token, mobile uses Cookie — so auth must be injected via a \`getAuthHeaders(): Record\<string,string>|null\` callback, not hardcoded; (2) URL resolution differs — desktop reads \`process.env.FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL\`, mobile reads \`expo-constants\` — inject a \`baseUrl\`/\`cloudUrl()\` fn rather than importing expo-constants into the shared package; (3) org-slug caching differs (per-token on desktop vs single-slug on mobile) — keep app-side; (4) desktop's error taxonomy (FreestyleCloudAuthError/UsageError/RequestError + stale-socket retry) is richer than mobile's (only CloudAuthError) — the shared helper should adopt desktop's taxonomy. Both runtimes support fetch + AbortSignal.timeout, so a pure-fetch implementation works on both without axios/ky/undici.

<!-- lore:019faf56-de8f-71b7-b107-4dae92d9cf05 -->
* **Rejected catch-all cloud auth proxy — kept explicit per-endpoint routes in apps/server**: Considered replacing apps/server's individual cloud-auth route handlers (auth.ts, billing.ts) with a single catch-all proxy (\`auth.all('/proxy/\*', ...)\`) injecting the bearer token and forwarding to Freestyle Cloud. Rejected: only 2 of 10 endpoints (GET /usage, POST /auth/unlink-account) are pure forwards — the other 8 have local side effects (SQLite session writes, PostHog identify, default-provider switching on sign-in/out) or request/response transforms (hardcoded callback URLs, field mapping) a catch-all would lose or need hooks to preserve anyway. Kept explicit routes: ~15 lines each, full control over transforms/side effects/error handling via cloudJson()'s retry + error classification. If org support later adds many \*pure\* proxy endpoints, revisit a hybrid (explicit routes for side-effect endpoints + catch-all for pure ones).

<!-- lore:019faf89-ec0d-7a2a-85dc-781eb42e93b1 -->
* **Rejected catch-all cloud proxy — kept explicit per-route auth/usage/billing/org handlers**: Considered replacing apps/server's 11 auth/usage/billing routes with a generic \`ALL /api/auth/proxy/\*\` forwarder (read token, inject bearer, forward to cloud, return raw response) to cut boilerplate for future org endpoints. Rejected: only ~3-4 of 11 routes are pure proxies (accounts, unlink-account, usage); the rest (device/token, sign-out, profile) have essential local side effects — SQLite session persistence, default-model assignment, PostHog identity lifecycle — that a generic proxy would silently drop. A catch-all also loses cloudJson()'s retry/error-classification logic (401→AuthError, 429→UsageError, 5xx→RequestError) and returns cloud responses unfiltered to the renderer. Each new explicit route is only ~15 lines. Kept explicit-route pattern; new org endpoints (GET /org/list, GET /org/active, POST /org/set-active) follow it.

<!-- lore:019fd6e9-22cc-7939-9dfb-a8a7482c5fcc -->
* **Remix client tools kept primitive; REMIX\_MAX\_STEPS=16**: Remix agent's client tools (packages/validations/src/remix.ts) are deliberately primitive OS-level actions — select\_all, copy, paste, press\_key, undo — not high-level ops like "replace\_text". Rejected: high-level tools would need per-app awareness (browsers, canvas apps, native editors have no universal text-replace API); primitive keystroke/clipboard actions work uniformly via accessibility injection across any frontmost app. Tradeoff: a single canvas-app edit costs 6-8 tool calls before verification, so remix-agent.ts sets REMIX\_MAX\_STEPS=16 for stopWhen: stepCountIs(...) — a run that hasn't converged in 16 steps is considered lost. Don't lower this cap without accounting for the primitive-tool design; it's not an arbitrary safety limit.

<!-- lore:019fd763-4a5d-706c-afc6-2c9c4528b222 -->
* **Remix idle timeout: bump REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS + activity-tracked forced remount**: To give Remix pill sessions a genuine 20-min idle timeout with a fresh session on the next event: (1) bump REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS from 15min to 20min at remix-store.ts:8 — sufficient for the common case since sessions self-dismiss via existing minimized-idle timers (MINI\_SETTLED\_DISMISS\_MS=3s, MINI\_IDLE\_DISMISS\_MS=7s) before an expanded, un-dismissed chat becomes an issue. (2) For the edge case of an EXPANDED chat left open indefinitely (see \[\[gotcha: freshness check only fires on mount]]): preferred fix is a lastRemixActivityAt ref bumped on onFinish/message-send, forcing endRemix({hide:false}) before reopening if idle >20min so RemixChat remounts and re-triggers the server's isFresh() check — keeps the server as single source of truth. Rejected: a second client-side setTimeout(onClose, 20min) mirroring the MINI\_SETTLED\_DISMISS\_MS pattern, because it introduces a second idle constant that can drift from REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS.

<!-- lore:019fd744-c840-77c7-84f2-beaba4cf7324 -->
* **Remix idle-timeout fix: bump server constant, defer client activity-tracking**: For the 20-min Remix session idle timeout, chose bumping REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS 15→20min (remix-store.ts:8) alone as the minimal fix, since RemixChat self-dismisses via existing timers (MINI\_SETTLED\_DISMISS\_MS/MINI\_IDLE\_DISMISS\_MS) in the common case, forcing remount + re-check of server freshness. Rejected Option B — adding a client-side setTimeout(onClose, 20\*60\*1000) mirroring the MINI\_SETTLED\_DISMISS\_MS pattern — because it introduces a second idle constant that can drift from the server's REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS. Preferred future fix for the known edge case (chat left open/expanded indefinitely never remounts, so freshness never re-checked) is Option A: track a lastRemixActivityAt ref bumped on onFinish/message-send, and force endRemix({hide:false}) before reopening if idle >20min, keeping the server's isFresh() as the single source of truth.

<!-- lore:019fd74d-634e-79c6-a2ec-6d82faa6bc01 -->
* **Remix idle-timeout fix: track lastRemixActivityAt, not a second client timer**: To close the gap where a left-open expanded Remix chat bypasses the server idle check, chosen approach: track a \`lastRemixActivityAt\` ref bumped on onFinish/message-send, and force \`endRemix({hide:false})\` before reopening if idle exceeds REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS, so RemixChat remounts and re-triggers the server's isFresh() check. Rejected: adding a second client-side \`setTimeout(onClose, IDLE\_MS)\` mirroring the MINI\_SETTLED\_DISMISS\_MS pattern — this would duplicate REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS as a second constant that could drift out of sync with the server value. Keeping the server's isFresh() as the single source of truth avoids that drift.

For the 'left-open expanded chat bypasses freshness check' edge case, chose: track a \`lastRemixActivityAt\` ref (bumped on \`onFinish\`/message-send) and force \`endRemix({hide:false})\` before reusing a session if idle >20min, so RemixChat remounts and re-consults the server's \`isFresh()\`. Rejected: adding a second client-side \`setTimeout(onClose, 20\*60\*1000)\` mirroring the existing \`MINI\_SETTLED\_DISMISS\_MS\` pattern (remix-chat.tsx:242-244) — rejected because it introduces a second idle constant that can drift from the server's \`REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS\`; keeping idle-detection server-side (single source of truth) avoids that drift.

<!-- lore:019fd752-ef82-742d-bfd4-0e1bae8a6dd3 -->
* **REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS bumped 15→20min, edge-case fix via activity ref not dup timer**: For the remix pill's 20-min idle timeout: chose to bump \`REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS\` 15→20 min at remix-store.ts:8 as the minimal fix, since sessions normally self-dismiss via existing minimized-idle timers (MINI\_SETTLED\_DISMISS\_MS=3s, MINI\_IDLE\_DISMISS\_MS=7000ms in remix-chat.tsx) well before staying open 20 minutes. For the 'left open expanded indefinitely' edge case (see \[\[gotcha entry]]), rejected adding a second client-side \`setTimeout\`-based idle-close mirroring the minimized-dismiss pattern — it would introduce a duplicate idle constant that could drift from the server's single source of truth. Preferred instead: track \`lastRemixActivityAt\` (bumped on onFinish/message send) and force \`endRemix({hide:false})\` before reopening if idle >20min, so RemixChat remounts and re-consults the server's \`isFresh()\` check in remix-store.ts.

<!-- lore:019fd735-406d-7215-8282-6359357844d1 -->
* **REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS bumped 15min→20min (remix-store.ts)**: Changed apps/server/src/lib/remix-store.ts's REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS from 15\*60\*1000 to 20\*60\*1000 per explicit user request. This alone is sufficient for the common case: RemixChat fully unmounts between sessions via existing dismiss timers (minimized+settled 3s, minimized+load-failed 7s, Escape/close button), and its mount effect (remix-chat.tsx:106-131) re-checks server freshness on every remount. Known gap left unfixed: an actively-open EXPANDED chat left idle indefinitely never remounts, so it bypasses the freshness check — see the related gotcha on mount-only freshness checks. Rejected fully fixing this now via a client-side wall-clock timer (mirroring MINI\_SETTLED\_DISMISS\_MS) because it would introduce a second idle constant that could drift from the server's; preferred future fix is to force a remount via endRemix when idle is detected, keeping the server's isFresh() as single source of truth.

<!-- lore:019fd736-7027-73e7-8365-a0719b715375 -->
* **REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS kept desktop-only, not exported from validations**: Remix thread idle-timeout constants (\`REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS\` in apps/server/src/lib/remix-store.ts, and a planned client-side \`REMIX\_SESSION\_IDLE\_MS\` in apps/electron) are deliberately kept as two separate, cross-referenced constants rather than one shared export in packages/validations/src/remix.ts. Rejected: adding it to remix.ts because that file is mirrored byte-for-byte into the cloud repo \[\[019fd6c7-0be3-749e-af92-34c79cacaa61]], and the idle-timeout behavior is desktop-only (pill session lifecycle) — exporting it there would force an unnecessary cloud-repo mirror update for a value the cloud never uses. \`getActiveThread()\` treats a thread as fresh only if \`Date.now() - last\_active\_at < REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS\`; \`last\_active\_at\` only advances on \`useChat.onFinish\` → \`POST /thread/sync\`, not on every render.

Keep \`w-\[calc(100%-3rem)]\` for the 1.5rem side gutters. First attempt removed the max-w constraint entirely (letting it stretch to the full \`w-\[calc(100%-3rem)]\` content width); Aditya Mathur rejected that and requested a capped \`max-w-5xl\` instead, so the final className is \`relative z-50 mt-14 w-\[calc(100%-3rem)] max-w-5xl self-center\`. update-banner.tsx itself needs no changes — its internal \`justify-between\` flex layout already fills whatever width the wrapper gives it. Width is controlled solely by shell.tsx's wrapper className, not by editing the UpdateBanner component. Banner's z-50 sits below glass-topbar's absolute z-40 (offset by mt-14), so it never visually clashes with the top-right GitHub/Discord icons.

<!-- lore:019fd573-5605-74b4-ab15-5fbb178232c8 -->

Decision: drop \`max-w-2xl\` so the banner spans the full content gutter width, and keep it non-dismissible/blocking (no close/'Later' affordance) rather than adding a dismiss option — user explicitly chose 'full-width banner across content' + 'non-dismissible/blocking' over alternatives when asked. No changes needed inside update-banner.tsx itself since its internal flex already stretches to fill the container. Banner sits below glass-topbar's icons (GitHub/Discord) due to the \`mt-14\` offset despite both having z-index near 40-50, so no visual clash.

When touching this banner, keep the \`w-\[calc(100%-3rem)]\` gutter and use a \`max-w-\*\` cap rather than dropping the cap entirely; the uncapped version was tried and explicitly rejected.

Initial fix dropped \`max-w-2xl\` outright per a literal 'make it full screen' request, but the user corrected this — the actual want was a wider but still-capped banner, not a truly unconstrained one. Design choice: full-width blocking banner spanning content (not a modal/full-screen overlay), non-dismissible with no close/'Later' affordance — user explicitly picked 'full-width banner across content' + 'non-dismissible/blocking' when offered alternatives. Lesson: when a user says 'full screen' for a width tweak, verify the exact target (e.g. ask for a max-w-Nxl value) rather than removing the cap entirely.
