The regenerated project has a single "Copy Files" phase with one build file and the \`.appex\` properly parented. Verification: \`grep -A5 "PBXCopyFilesBuildPhase" ios/Freestyle.xcodeproj/project.pbxproj\` shows one phase and one build file entry.

<!-- lore:019fd0a2-fd47-727e-ac97-cd3fae8b02c9 -->
* **Dictation recording-start delay is getUserMedia round-trip, not pill animation**: Investigation (Aug 2026) traced hotkey→pill→recording in apps/electron. Root cause of perceived recording-start delay: \`startRecording()\` (app.tsx:1486) only calls \`setPillState('recording')\` after \`await acquirePromise\` resolves (app.tsx:1573) — the getUserMedia round-trip (recorder.ts:56/67). Because \`releaseStream()\` fires on every commit/cancel (mic released for privacy \[\[019fb9b0-198d-78a1-8ff4-62537b6c7359]]), there's no warm stream to reuse via \`hasLiveStream()\`, so every press pays fresh getUserMedia latency. Secondary, first-press-only delay: \`showPill()\` (index.ts:990) defers the \`hotkey:down\` IPC behind \`pillReadyPromise\` until \`did-finish-load\` on a cold pill window (index.ts:1007-1031); warm path shows synchronously. The pill's CSS 'summon' animation (React \`entered\` state, app.tsx:2703-2711) looks like a plausible culprit but is fully decoupled — it never gates recording. The \`setTimeout(r,100)\` at app.tsx:630 is unrelated (belongs to post-transcription \`drainQueue\`).

<!-- lore:019f5b6a-ef18-7cbe-b33d-09e4f04c3bb7 -->
* **Dictionary/vocabulary import: add CSV/TSV support**: Extend the existing JSON import for Dictionary and Vocabulary to also accept CSV and TSV formats. Provide a small inline converter in the import UI and validate via \`@freestyle-voice/validations\`. This makes it easy to paste word lists from any competitor app (e.g., Anki, spreadsheets) without requiring JSON. Keep the existing JSON import for programmatic use. Confidence: 0.9 — low risk, consistent with existing JSON import and validations package.

<!-- lore:019f6a1a-df33-7b88-b735-fa48e00a7ded -->
* **discoverPlugins() dedup is by dir field — pluginSlug mismatch causes duplicate entries**: Trap: \`discoverPlugins()\` in \`apps/server/src/lib/plugins/ui-assets.ts:50-85\` deduplicates by \`dir\` field (not slug or name). Phase 1 walks \`plugins\` setting via \`discoverPackage()\` (Node resolution then \`\<localPluginsDir>/\<pluginSlug(specifier)>/package.json\`); Phase 2 scans local plugins dir via \`discoverLocalDir()\` (all subdirs). If \`link-plugin.mjs\` creates a dir with a different name than \`pluginSlug()\` produces (SDK appends 7-char FNV-1a base36 hash; script does NOT), both phases find different dirs for the same plugin → duplicate entries (one normal, one 'missing'). Fix: ensure \`link-plugin.mjs\` dir naming exactly mirrors \`packages/sdk/src/ui.ts:105-113\` including the \`shortHash(name)\` suffix.

<!-- lore:019faf56-de96-7005-9b4c-9feb4fd06e35 -->
* **DropdownMenuItem card hover rounding clashes with adjacent separator**: Trap: a DropdownMenuItem styled as a card (rounded-xl) sitting directly above a DropdownMenuSeparator shows a rounded corner poking into the separator on hover/focus, because the item's full border-radius applies on all corners. Fix: add \`rounded-b-none\` to the item so the bottom edge stays square where it meets the separator. Seen in apps/electron/src/renderer/src/components/cloud-profile.tsx (user card DropdownMenuItem, line ~158). Applies to any dropdown item adjacent to a separator that uses full corner rounding.

<!-- lore:019fb22f-638b-7835-b614-f7f4e14af1e6 -->
* **DropdownMenuSubContent Portal wrap alone doesn't fix z-index stacking — check DOM sibling stacking contexts**: Trap: a Radix \`DropdownMenuSub\` submenu appearing invisible/behind page content looks like a missing \`Portal\` wrap (since \`DropdownMenuContent\` uses Portal but \`DropdownMenuSubContent\` in dropdown-menu.tsx didn't) — but adding the Portal did NOT fix it when the symptom is 'submenu appears but behind content' (a stacking-context issue, not a missing-portal issue). Root cause: a DOM sibling wrapper (e.g. \`glass-content\` in shell.tsx) has \`relative\` positioning, creating its own stacking context, and paints after the body-portaled dropdown in DOM order despite the dropdown's \`z-50\`, because the sibling itself had no explicit z-index (implicit order wins). Fix: give the competing sibling wrapper an explicit lower \`z-index\` (e.g. \`z-0\`) so portaled \`z-50\` content reliably paints above it. Diagnostic tip: always ask the user to describe the exact symptom (invisible/clipped vs appears-behind) before choosing between a Portal fix and a z-index fix — they require different solutions.

<!-- lore:019f0340-6c29-7aef-ba9a-fcc1fd8ed21f -->
* **DRY: use canonical defaults from validations**: DEFAULT\_PRESET removed; default values now come from DEFAULT\_HISTORY\_FILTERS via spread in resetFilters to avoid duplication. Keeps localStorage plumbing minimal and consistent with validation schema.

<!-- lore:019fd2db-6b70-735a-bcf6-834f648ab16e -->
* **Editing multi-line import blocks: re-read exact state, insert one line at a time**: Trap: adding a single new import to a file with an existing multi-line import block (e.g. adding one new import to transcribe.ts's import list) is easy to botch by reconstructing/rewriting the whole block from memory — repeatedly caused accidental deletion of unrelated adjacent imports (stripProviderPrefix, getMlxModelStatus, a whole plugins/pipeline.js import block) across several attempts in one session, each only caught via typecheck/test failures afterward. Fix: before editing an import block, re-read the file's exact current content and make ONE precise minimal single-line insertion rather than touching the surrounding lines.

<!-- lore:019fcc75-1faf-7727-a7d0-4ab7e8fa9190 -->
* **Elapsed-time readout gating: recording||initializing plus zero startTimeRef trap**: Voice pill elapsed-time readout (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx) was gated on \`recording || initializing\`, but \`startTimeRef\` is only set once the mic actually opens. On first press of a fresh process, startTimeRef was still 0, so \`Date.now() - 0\` instantly exceeded the 60s threshold, showing a stray huge timestamp (e.g. "29763131:43") at the pill's right edge on every first startup. Looked like a display bug but was a state-gating bug: including \`initializing\` in the gate let the clock read before the ref was populated. Fix: gate strictly on \`recording\` alone AND require a non-zero start time, and reset the ref between sessions to avoid staleness.

<!-- lore:019f3176-10b7-741a-87a2-fd4fd33acac7 -->
* **electron typecheck: use package name @freestyle-voice/electron, not 'electron'**: Trap: running \`turbo typecheck --filter=electron\` fails with 'No package found with name electron' — looks like a filter syntax issue. Fix: the electron app's package name is \`@freestyle-voice/electron\`, so use \`--filter=@freestyle-voice/electron\`. Alternatively, run \`pnpm --filter @freestyle-voice/electron run typecheck\` directly. The typecheck script itself runs two sub-scripts: \`typecheck:node\` (tsconfig.node.json) and \`typecheck:web\` (tsconfig.web.json) — both must pass.

<!-- lore:019fd662-8fc3-7ce4-8692-c677eb52317d -->
* **EndpointConnectState.models never becomes selectable rows**: In apps/electron .../models/use-endpoint-connect.ts, runEndpointTest() (custom OpenAI-compatible STT test) sets EndpointConnectState.models via setModels(result.models ?? \[]) after a successful probe, but this array only feeds the 'Connected · N models found' text in EndpointConnectForm/OpenaiSttConnect (model-list.tsx) — it's never merged into voiceItems (use-models.ts) or turned into ModelRow entries. buildVoiceRows() (model-list.tsx:100) builds rows purely from m.voiceItems, so testing a custom endpoint shows a model count but no way to pick a specific discovered model; the active voice model stays whatever default (e.g. openai/whisper-1) was set, just redirected via base-URL override. Looks correct because 'Connected + count' implies success. Fix direction: render each discovered model id as a selectable row post-Test, configuring \`openai/\<model-id>\` as the active voice model.

<!-- lore:019fd721-e1b2-7be4-9dd4-b251833e9ce2 -->
* **EndpointConnectState.models never merged into voiceItems (custom STT picker)**: useEndpointConnect's runEndpointTest() (apps/electron/.../models/use-endpoint-connect.ts) sets discovered model IDs into EndpointConnectState.models on a successful probe, but voice picker rows are built exclusively from m.voiceItems via buildVoiceRows (model-list.tsx) — the two arrays are never merged. Looks correct because the UI shows 'Connected · N models found', implying selection works, but there's no code path turning EndpointConnectState.models into selectable Row objects. Result: pasting a custom OpenAI-compatible endpoint just displays a model count with no way to pick a specific model; the actually active voice model stays whatever was previously configured (e.g. openai/whisper-1), now silently redirected to the custom base URL. Fix in progress (Aug 2026): new 'Custom endpoint' PickerOption + view rendering discovered models as selectable rows, configuring openai/\<discovered-id> to reuse the base-URL-overridden openai provider.

<!-- lore:019fd393-85e2-7087-af61-9796a8f8c864 -->
* **EndpointConnectState.models never merged into voiceItems rows**: apps/electron model-list.tsx/use-models.ts has two disconnected data structures for voice models: (1) \`m.voiceItems: VoiceItem\[]\` (use-models.ts:81), built by \`buildVoiceRows()\` (model-list.tsx:100) from \`AvailableModel\[]\` + whisper/mlx status — this is what every model Row (ModelRow, transcription-picker sections) renders from. (2) \`m.openaiStt\`/\`m.localLlm: EndpointConnectState\` (via \`useEndpointConnect\`, use-endpoint-connect.ts) tracks BYOK connect-form state; on successful probe it sets \`models: string\[]\` and shows 'Connected · N models found'. These are never wired together — testing a custom OpenAI-compatible endpoint discovers models but the discovered list is purely informational text, with no selectable rows. Looks fixed because 'N models found' implies success, but the actually active voice model stays whatever default (e.g. openai/whisper-1) with only the base URL redirected. Any 'let user pick a discovered model' feature must bridge EndpointConnectState.models into voiceItems/Row.

<!-- lore:019fd6d5-d75c-7173-91fe-9a4cc219702b -->
* **EndpointConnectState.models never wired into voiceItems/buildVoiceRows**: In apps/electron, custom OpenAI-compatible/local-LLM endpoint testing (use-endpoint-connect.ts \`runEndpointTest\`) stores discovered model IDs in \`EndpointConnectState.models\`, but model-list.tsx's \`buildVoiceRows\` (line ~100) builds all picker rows purely from \`m.voiceItems\` (computed in use-models.ts via buildSettingsVoiceItems/buildVoiceItems), which never reads \`EndpointConnectState.models\`. Looks fine because the connect form's job (persist url/key, confirm reachability via \`/openai-stt/test\` or \`/local-llm/test\`) succeeds and shows 'Connected · N models found' — but there's no wiring to turn those N discovered models into selectable rows. Result: user can verify a custom endpoint works but cannot pick which model it uses; the active voice model stays whatever was previously configured (e.g. openai/whisper-1), just redirected through the base-URL-overridden \`openai\` provider (apps/server streaming/providers/openai.ts).

<!-- lore:019fd764-6cc3-7187-970f-4945d3c4e502 -->
* **EndpointConnectState.models not wired into voiceItems/Row selection**: In apps/electron model-list.tsx, useEndpointConnect's runEndpointTest() (use-endpoint-connect.ts) sets EndpointConnectState.models: string\[] from the probe result, but voice rows are built exclusively from m.voiceItems (via buildVoiceRows in model-list.tsx, sourced from use-models.ts's voiceItems useMemo) — the two arrays are disconnected. After testing a custom OpenAI-compatible endpoint, the UI shows 'Connected · N models found' with no way to pick a specific model; the actually active voice model stays whatever it was (e.g. openai/whisper-1), just redirected to the new base URL. Looks fixed because the connect flow 'succeeds' and shows a model count, masking that the count is purely informational. Fix: merge discovered custom-endpoint models into selectable rows, configured as \`openai/\<discovered-id>\` (reuses existing openai provider's base-URL override; stripProviderPrefix handles routing).

<!-- lore:019fceba-d4ee-7034-bc69-8bb16302e8af -->
* **endRemix hide:false leaks focusable pill, dictation pastes into itself**: endRemix({hide:false}) — used by dictation's onHotkeyDown self-heal (app.tsx), deliverRemixResult, and preset auth/usage errors — deliberately skips hidePill() to avoid hiding the pill mid-flow. But hidePill() was the only place that reset mainWindow.setFocusable(false), remixPressed, and route-key registration after a remix chat card called setRemixChatFocus(true)→win.setFocusable(true). Looked safe because deliverRemixResult's paste path (remix:paste IPC) calls hidePill() itself — only the dictation self-heal path was unsafe: dictation would record fine but paste into the still-focused pill window instead of the user's document, appearing as 'everything goes to remix'. Fix: added dedicated \`remix:release\`/\`releaseRemixHold()\` IPC that resets focusable+remixPressed+route-keys without hiding the pill, called from endRemix's hide:false branch; also made \`showPill()\` defensively reset \`setFocusable(false)\` on every show as a backstop.

<!-- lore:019fcea3-1004-74b3-a71f-53e15855de3d -->
* **endRemix({hide:false}) leaves pill window focusable — breaks dictation after remix chat**: Bug: after using the remix hotkey chat and returning to dictation, all subsequent hotkey presses seem to go to remix / paste into the pill instead of the frontmost app. Root cause: dictation's onHotkeyDown self-heal (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx:2399) calls \`endRemix({hide:false})\` when tearing down an active remix session, which deliberately skips \`hidePill()\`. But \`hidePill()\` (main/index.ts:1420-1451) is the ONLY place that resets \`mainWindow.setFocusable(false)\` and \`remixPressed\`. If the remix chat card previously ran \`setRemixChatFocus(true)\`→\`win.setFocusable(true)\` (remix-chat.tsx:104), the window stays focusable/focused after remix teardown, so dictation still records but the transcript pastes into the pill window itself rather than the OS-focused app. \`deliverRemixResult\`'s paste path is safe because main's \`remix:paste\` handler (index.ts:2918) calls \`hidePill()\` itself — only the dictation self-heal path is unsafe. Also \`showPill()\` (index.ts:996) never defensively resets focusability on show. Fix proposed but not yet applied: reset focusability inside \`endRemix({hide:false})\`, or make \`showPill()\` call \`setFocusable(false)\` defensively.

<!-- lore:019fd752-ef76-75ae-89ab-71de5617fe6b -->
* **Expanded remix chat left open bypasses REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS re-check**: If a user leaves an EXPANDED (non-minimized) remix chat open indefinitely, \`remixRef\` stays non-null and \`chatView\` stays truthy, so RemixChat never unmounts and its once-per-mount freshness check never re-runs. The next \`remix:open-chat\` event then takes the 'reuse' branch (app.tsx:2604-2606, \`patchRemix({phase:'chat'})\` since \`remixRef.current\` exists) instead of creating a new session — bypassing REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS entirely regardless of its value. Looks like bumping the idle constant alone fixes staleness, but it only covers the common case where chat has already self-dismissed via the existing 3s/7s minimized-idle timers (MINI\_SETTLED\_DISMISS\_MS/MINI\_IDLE\_DISMISS\_MS in remix-chat.tsx:242-244). Preferred fix: track a \`lastRemixActivityAt\` ref and force \`endRemix({hide:false})\` before reopening if idle, so RemixChat remounts and re-checks server freshness — rather than adding a second client-side idle timer that could drift from the server's \`isFresh()\` constant.

<!-- lore:019fd730-8bd4-74f0-a79a-17fb17d64eae -->
* **Expanded RemixChat left open bypasses server freshness check on reopen**: Trap: bumping REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS alone looks sufficient for 'new session after N min idle' because the freshness check exists server-side — but it's only evaluated in RemixChat's one-time mount effect (remix-chat.tsx:106-131). If a user leaves an EXPANDED chat open (not minimized/dismissed), \`remix\` stays non-null and RemixChat never unmounts, so the mount effect never reruns. The next \`remix:open-chat\` event hits the reuse branch (app.tsx:2602-2616, \`patchRemix({phase:'chat'})\`) instead of creating a fresh session — silently skipping the idle check entirely, even if the thread is hours stale. Only auto-close paths that unmount RemixChat are: minimized+settled 3s dismiss (MINI\_SETTLED\_DISMISS\_MS), minimized load-failure 7s dismiss (MINI\_IDLE\_DISMISS\_MS), and manual Escape/close — none apply while expanded and idle-but-open.

<!-- lore:019fcea0-efdf-7e7e-9263-d8e04628ac9d -->
* **failRemix() leaves remixRef set — only endRemix() clears remix mode**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx, remixRef.current (the sole discriminator for 'remix mode active', showRemixCard = remix !== null) is reset to null in exactly one place: endRemix() (:1920 → setRemix(null) at :1933). failRemix() (:1945) deliberately does NOT clear remixRef — it sets phase:'error' and leaves the session object in place (:1953) so the error card can render. This looks like a bug (remix 'stuck' after failure) but is intentional; the error phase auto-dismisses via a separate timeout (REMIX\_WARNING\_MS=4500ms) that itself calls endRemix(). If a future refactor changes failRemix to also null remixRef, the error-card render path breaks since it depends on remix !== null.

<!-- lore:019fb22f-6386-7960-bcad-b57dde979b2b -->
* **flex-1 on Tooltip-wrapped label expands hover hit-area beyond visible text**: Trap: \`flex-1\` on a truncated label span (e.g. model-used label in history.tsx FeedItem) looks like normal flex layout, but when wrapped in \`Tooltip\`/\`TooltipTrigger\`, flex-1 stretches the trigger element to fill the row — the tooltip then fires just from moving the cursor over empty space far past the visible text. Fix: use \`max-w-fit\` (with \`min-w-0 truncate\`) so the trigger sizes to its content instead of stretching. To still push trailing elements (action buttons, duration, cost) to the right edge of the row, add a separate \`\<div className="flex-1" />\` spacer sibling right after the tooltip, rather than putting flex-1 on the tooltip trigger itself.

<!-- lore:019fc2d6-cd25-777e-b04c-598eab886780 -->
* **Freestyle Cloud v2 payload asymmetry: appAssignments string vs array, customPrompt null vs undefined**: In apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts, /v2/transcribe (multipart form) receives \`appAssignments\` as a JSON-encoded STRING (parsed cloud-side via parseCleanupAppAssignments), while /v2/post-process (JSON body) receives \`appAssignments\` as a real ARRAY (validated by cleanupAppAssignmentsSchema) — same field, different wire encoding depending on endpoint transport. Also, \`customPrompt\` in the post-process payload must be built with \`|| undefined\`, not \`?? null\`, because postProcessV2BodySchema uses \`.optional()\` (rejects null) — whereas \`appContext\` uses \`.nullish()\` and does accept null. Looks like these fields should behave uniformly since they're 'the same' conceptual value, but each endpoint's schema differs. This is a recurring source of payload bugs; check the exact schema (multipart-string vs JSON-array, optional vs nullish) per field before adding new cloud request params.

<!-- lore:019f5b6f-8331-792c-b1bf-14b4430fd5dd -->
* **FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL=localhost:8787 causes fetch failed 500 when cloud Worker isn't running**: Trap: \`apps/electron/.env.local\` sets \`FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL=http://localhost:8787\` (local \`wrangler dev\` URL). When the cloud Worker isn't running, every transcription POST to that URL is refused — undici surfaces this as \`TypeError: fetch failed\` with no visible cause in the log, and the route returns 500. It looks like a cloud service outage or auth failure, but it's just a missing local process. Fix: either start \`wrangler dev\` in the \`cloud\` repo, comment out \`FREESTYLE\_CLOUD\_URL\` to fall back to production (\`https://service.freestylevoice.com\`), or switch to a local voice model. The real cause (ECONNREFUSED) is on \`err.cause\` — the \`formatError\` utility now walks the cause chain to surface it.

<!-- lore:019fd0f2-2c50-7788-8763-9f4e4ee8fc84 -->
* **GET /api/settings triplicated — cleanup-app-context bypasses settings-all cache**: apps/electron renderer fetches /api/settings via 3 separate mechanisms: (1) cached useQuery(settingsQueryOptions()) key \["settings-all"] — ~10 consumers dedupe into one request; (2) a raw uncached getClient().api.settings.$get() on mount (app.tsx:2373); (3) a raw uncached fetch on EVERY recording start via cleanup-app-context.ts:45 refreshNeedsAppContextForCleanup(), called from app.tsx:1522 inside startRecording. #3 is worst: a fresh network GET of the full settings map fires on every hotkey press despite identical data being fresh in the 1h React-Query cache. A sync variant, applyNeedsAppContextForCleanup(settings), already exists to warm this cache from a snapshot but isn't used on the hot path. Same pattern recurs smaller-scale: tone.tsx:347 re-fetches models.available.$get() raw instead of reading the \["models","available"] cache; history.tsx:445 fetches settings/:key under a separate \["setting",...] key duplicating the settings-all map. Fix direction: read from queryClient cache instead of firing a new network request.

<!-- lore:019fd277-3d4b-7969-a080-28c404c3fa23 -->
* **getClient() allocates new Hono client on every call (api.ts:140)**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/api.ts's getClient() constructs \`new hc\<AppType>(...)\` fresh on every invocation rather than caching a singleton. It's called inline dozens of times across settings.tsx (9×), tone.tsx, history/vocabulary/dictionary pages — each call re-parses base URL/headers. Not catastrophic but wasteful on interactive paths. Fix: memoize the client and rebuild only when the \`server:changed\` IPC event fires (the existing mechanism that invalidates React Query on server URL/token change) rather than reconstructing per call site.

<!-- lore:019fd278-4382-706c-80a4-477524505628 -->
* **getClient() in api.ts creates a new hc\<AppType> on every call**: apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/api.ts:140-142 getClient() builds a fresh \`hc\<AppType>(getApiBase(), {headers})\` on every invocation; called inline across settings.tsx (9x), tone.tsx, history, vocabulary, and dictionary pages. Looks harmless since hc() is a lightweight proxy, but it's invoked dozens of times per render cycle across the app. Fix: memoize the client, rebuilding only on the \`server:changed\` IPC broadcast (the same convention refreshApiBase() already uses for cache invalidation \[\[019fb9e6-a5fa-7ae3-bdf2-5fd4a50ece29]]). Verified via full file read (Aug 2026 perf audit); not yet fixed.

<!-- lore:019fcbf3-30e6-7f3c-a3db-1dce5be72193 -->
* **git diff main..HEAD inflated/inaccurate when local main branch is stale**: Trap: when reviewing a fork PR locally, \`git diff main..HEAD\` can show a wildly inflated diff (e.g. 40 files/+888/-2104) that looks like the true PR scope but is actually noise from a stale local \`main\` that hasn't fetched recent upstream commits. Fix: always cross-check the diff size against the PR's actual metadata from the GitHub API/UI (file count, +/- lines) before trusting a local diff — trust the platform-reported scope, not local git state, when main may be behind origin.

<!-- lore:019f1697-9d46-7703-82a6-67f628a78aaa -->
* **git rebase --continue hangs when git launches interactive editor — use GIT\_EDITOR=true**: Trap: \`git rebase --continue\` after resolving conflicts launches \`$EDITOR\` to confirm the commit message, blocking indefinitely in a non-interactive agent context — looks like a hang with no output. Fix: prefix with \`GIT\_EDITOR=true\` (\`GIT\_EDITOR=true git rebase --continue\`) to accept the default commit message without opening an editor. Also: \`.lore.md\` may be re-modified by a background process during the timeout, requiring re-staging (\`git add .lore.md\`) before rebase can continue.

<!-- lore:019f40a9-8d17-752c-ba21-10d4a88534f3 -->
* **history.tsx Reset button: was setting 'all-time' instead of restoring initial defaults**: Trap: Reset button called \`setActivePreset('all-time')\` which looks like 'clear all filters.' But the initial default is \`'weekly'\` (Last 7 days), not all-time. Fix: \`resetFilters\` restores actual initial defaults — \`preset='weekly'\`, cleared custom dates, \`page=0\`, \`diffMode=false\`, \`showAiEdits=true\`. Reset button uses \`Eraser\` icon (not \`RotateCcw\`) with \`ghost\` variant and destructive/danger theme (red text, subtle red hover).

<!-- lore:019fd200-9a48-7b68-ba03-64612f7aa037 -->
* **HistoryPage full-page spinner is a second paint-blocking layer after auth resolves**: Even after the auth loading gate clears \[\[019fd0cb-e4a7-7a47-824f-9d61f9d74559]]-adjacent flow, history.tsx:549-555 renders a full-page centered spinner (no skeleton/chrome) gated on \`isLoading\` of the \["history",...] query. That query is a \`Promise.all(\[/api/history, /api/history/stats])\` (history.tsx:381-385) — genuinely parallel, not sequential — but its resolution is a second serialization point stacked on top of the auth gate, so first content paint of /today waits on two sequential blockers, not one. \`placeholderData: keepPreviousData\` (history.tsx:396) only smooths subsequent filter/page navigations; it does nothing for the cold first mount where there's no prior cache and the spinner always shows.

<!-- lore:019fd21d-3e58-7c49-a60c-0b3523367369 -->
* **HistoryPage spinner re-gates /today content even after auth resolves**: Looks like auth resolving (\`LoginGate\` loading=false) should immediately show /today content, but it doesn't: \`history.tsx:549-555\` renders its own full-page centered spinner (no skeleton/chrome) gated on \`isLoading\` of the \`\["history",...]\` query, which waits on \`Promise.all(\[GET /api/history, GET /api/history/stats])\` (history.tsx:381-385, fetched together not serially). So first paint has two serialization points: (1) the auth gate, (2) this Promise.al spinner. \`placeholderData: keepPreviousData\` (history.tsx:396) avoids the spinner on filter/nav changes but not cold first mount. AppShell sidebar chrome (shell.tsx) renders immediately regardless of plugins/settings query state — the blocking spinner is confined to the \`\<Outlet/>\` content area.

<!-- lore:019f5fb8-4ec8-7e10-94b9-60512e0293d5 -->
* **i18n key removal: check all locale files including template.json**: Trap: removing an unused i18n key from \`en.json\` looks complete, but \`apps/electron/src/renderer/src/locales/template.json\` is a separate file that also contains the key and must be updated. Fix: always search for the key across all locale files (\`en.json\`, \`template.json\`, and language files \`de\`, \`es\`, \`fr\`, \`it\`, \`ja\`, \`pt\`) before committing. Missing keys in non-English locales are non-blocking (i18next falls back to \`en\`), but \`template.json\` must stay in sync with \`en.json\`.

<!-- lore:019fcbdf-3799-7839-bf9a-7785fe1e846b -->
* **Inline borderRadius style silently overrides animated .pill-card CSS rule**: In the voice pill (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx), inline style borderRadius: 20 on the card outranked the CSS rule .pill-card\[data-show="false"] { border-radius: 60px } — inline styles always beat stylesheet rules regardless of selector specificity. Looked fine on screen because the concurrent scale animation masked the frozen corner. Fixed in PR #541. Rule: any property the stylesheet animates on this component must never also be set inline, anywhere in the tree.

<!-- lore:019fcbf3-30d5-7e24-a746-b87aa95d6eef -->
* **Inline style attrs silently override animated CSS rules (pill border-radius)**: Trap: an inline style like \`borderRadius: 20\` on a React element looks harmless alongside a CSS class that also animates border-radius (\`.pill-card\[data-show="false"] { border-radius: 60px }\`) — it looks like the class rule will just apply on top. Fix: inline styles always win over stylesheet rules regardless of animation/transition, so the corner froze at 20px through the whole pill morph while a concurrent scale animation visually masked the bug, making it look correct on screen. Rule: never set inline style for a CSS property that a stylesheet also animates — pick one source of truth.

<!-- lore:019fcc75-1f91-7600-8ebe-3b26d269d5d5 -->
* **Inline style overrides CSS transition on .pill-card border-radius**: In apps/electron voice-pill code (pages/app.tsx), an inline style \`borderRadius: 20\` on the pill card silently outranked the animated CSS rule \`.pill-card\[data-show="false"] { border-radius: 60px }\`, so the corner stayed pinned at 20px through the whole morph. It looked correct on screen because the accompanying scale animation masked the frozen corner radius. Fix: never set inline styles for a CSS property that a stylesheet rule also animates — let the CSS own it entirely. Rule applies broadly to any animated property (transform, opacity, border-radius, etc.) in this codebase's pill/overlay animation code.

<!-- lore:019fcc74-3e5c-717c-ac23-537a898bf955 -->
* **Inline style overrides CSS-animated property in voice pill (app.tsx)**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx pill card, an inline \`style={{ borderRadius: 20 }}\` silently outranked the animated CSS rule \`.pill-card\[data-show="false"] { border-radius: 60px }\`, so the corner stayed pinned at 20px through the whole morph. Looked correct on screen because the scale animation masked the flat corner. Rule going forward: anything the stylesheet animates (border-radius, opacity, transform, etc.) must NOT also be set inline on the same element — inline styles always win CSS specificity battles. Fixed in PR #541 (design/voice-pill-motion, author Isaac Zhang).

<!-- lore:019f5fb8-4ecb-7535-9e81-b486cc791fa7 -->
* **Interactive rebase on shared branch: .lore.md merge conflicts — always take current version**: Trap: when rebasing \`feat/\*\` branches to drop commits, \`.lore.md\` frequently causes merge conflicts because it is updated by the memory system across sessions. Fix: always resolve \`.lore.md\` conflicts by taking the current branch version (\`git checkout --theirs .lore.md\`) and continuing the rebase. The \`.lore.md\` file is managed externally and should never be manually merged — the current branch's version is always authoritative.

<!-- lore:019f7b20-dc28-792c-a773-1606d91d4b9d -->
* **iOS 18+ keyboard extension: openURL selector hack fails — use SwiftUI Link**: On iOS 18+, \`UIApplication.shared.open(\_:options:completionHandler:)\` and \`perform(sel\_registerName("openURL:"))\` both fail silently when called from a keyboard extension. The only Apple-sanctioned mechanism is a SwiftUI \`Link(destination: url)\` hosted via \`UIHostingController\`. This also applies to \`openURL:\` triggered from the \`MicLink\` SwiftUI view used to launch dictation; use \`Link\` instead of gesture-based \`openURL\` calls.

<!-- lore:019f7aff-e631-7a11-92aa-ef2d4fb0e3d1 -->
* **iOS keyboard extension AVAudioSession: .record category fails — must use .playAndRecord**: Trap: \`AVAudioSession.setCategory(.record, mode: .measurement, options: \[.duckOthers])\` looks correct for mic-only capture and works in the main app. In a keyboard extension process, this combination causes \`setActive(true)\` to throw OSStatus \`561015905\` (\`cannotStartPlaying\`). Fix: use \`.playAndRecord\` + \`.default\` mode + \`\[.defaultToSpeaker, .allowBluetooth]\` options — this is the only category that reliably activates in the extension sandbox. Also guard \`inputNode\` format after activation: if \`sampleRate == 0 || channelCount == 0\`, throw before starting \`AVAudioEngine\`. Use a typed \`CaptureError\` enum (cases: \`session category\`, \`session activate\`, \`input format 0Hz/0ch\`, \`engine start\`) with \`NSLog\` to surface the exact OSStatus — a generic \`catch\` hides the real failure step.

<!-- lore:019f7e80-c7b6-74f9-ba3b-b086b57943e0 -->
* **iOS keyboard extension: .touchUpInside causes dropped keystrokes — use .touchDown for char keys**: On iOS, character keys in the keyboard must use \`.touchDown\` (not \`.touchUpInside\`) to avoid dropped keystrokes during fast typing. Delete uses \`.touchDown\` with 0.4s delay → 0.1s repeat → 0.5s word repeat. Also replace \`UIButton(type: .system)\` with a custom \`KeyButton\` subclass to eliminate system fade/tint animations that add lag.

<!-- lore:019f7bf9-db28-715e-81f1-146c3d40c026 -->
* **iOS keyboard extension: hasDictationKey=true suppresses system dictation button — must be set in init AND viewWillAppear**: Trap: setting \`hasDictationKey = true\` only in \`viewDidLoad\` looks sufficient but does NOT work — the system dictation button reappears. Fix: set it in \`init(nibName:bundle:)\`, \`required init?(coder:)\`, \`viewDidLoad\`, AND \`viewWillAppear(\_:)\`. Confirmed by StackOverflow accepted answer (score +8) and OP aditional note. Counter-intuitively, the property name is \`hasDictationKey = true\` (not false) — it tells iOS the extension provides its own dictation, suppressing the system button. Apps Wispr Flow and Typeless both use this approach. No Info.plist change needed — pure Swift on \`UIInputViewController\`.

<!-- lore:019f7c04-6286-72b6-89bd-cf5ffafea2d0 -->
* **iOS keyboard extension: textDidChange must NOT call applyColors() — causes per-keystroke lag**: Trap: \`textDidChange\` calling \`applyColors()\` looks correct for keeping key colors in sync with text state. But \`applyColors()\` rebuilds the SwiftUI mic host (\`micHost.rootView = MicLink(...)\`) AND recursively re-colors the entire key tree on every keystroke — causing severe input lag. Fix: \`textDidChange\` should call only \`applyAutoCap()\` (lightweight autocap state update). Full recoloring belongs in \`traitCollectionDidChange\` and \`viewDidLoad\` only — one-time events, not per-keystroke. Secondary lag factor (deferred): per-key drop shadow without \`shadowPath\` causes an offscreen render pass per button; fix requires UIButton subclass \`layoutSubviews\` override.

<!-- lore:019fc26e-0631-7860-92cf-121750580e39 -->
* **iOS KeyboardViewController.swift statusLabel truncates streamed text — wrap in fixed-height UIScrollView**: In apps/mobile/ios-keyboard/KeyboardViewController.swift, the streaming transcript renders into a UILabel \`statusLabel\` (fed by App Group state \`DictationBridge.partialTranscript\` via 0.3s polling). Setting \`numberOfLines = 2\` looks like a safe cap but the default \`.byTruncatingTail\` lineBreakMode silently ellipsizes long streamed text instead of scrolling. Fix: set \`numberOfLines = 0\` and wrap the label in a \`UIScrollView\` (\`statusScroll\`), with a \`scrollStatusToBottom()\` call after each update to keep the newest text visible. Gotcha within the fix: pinning the label to \`statusScroll.contentLayoutGuide\` does NOT give the scroll view a frame height — \`contentLayoutGuide\` only defines \`contentSize\`. Using \`heightAnchor.constraint(lessThanOrEqualToConstant: 30)\` alone leaves the scroll view's frame height ambiguous/underconstrained. Must use \`equalToConstant: 30\` (fixed height) instead; short text stays top-aligned via the \`overflow > 0\` guard resetting \`contentOffset\` to \`.zero\`. Note: transcript UI is pure UIKit (UILabel/UIScrollView); only the mic button uses SwiftUI via UIHostingController.

<!-- lore:019fc273-3a65-7fb3-8c54-fa3d4f0b3128 -->
* **KeyboardViewController statusScroll height ambiguity (contentLayoutGuide)**: iOS keyboard extension's streaming transcript label (\`statusLabel\`, apps/mobile/ios-keyboard/KeyboardViewController.swift) truncated to 2 lines with '…' because \`numberOfLines = 2\` used default \`.byTruncatingTail\`. Fixed by wrapping in \`statusScroll\` (UIScrollView): \`numberOfLines = 0\`, label pinned to \`statusScroll.contentLayoutGuide\` with width locked to \`frameLayoutGuide.widthAnchor\`, auto-scroll via \`scrollStatusToBottom()\`. Gotcha: pinning \`statusScroll.heightAnchor\` with only \`lessThanOrEqualToConstant\` looks correct (content should self-size) but a UIScrollView's frame height is NOT derived from its \`contentLayoutGuide\` — that guide only defines \`contentSize\`, not frame height. With just an upper bound and no other height constraint, the scroll view's height is ambiguous/underconstrained. Fix: use \`equalToConstant: 30\` (fixed ~2-line height) instead.

<!-- lore:019fd03f-b23f-705e-8579-caa336ec7ef5 -->
* **LazyMotion strict: m.\* components need to render inside a LazyMotion provider tree**: In apps/electron's vendored beUI components, preview-rail.tsx uses \`m.\*\` from motion/react but doesn't wrap itself in its own \`\<LazyMotion strict>\` provider — it only works because its sole consumer chain (message-scroller.tsx → remix-chat.tsx) happens to render inside remix-chat.tsx's \`\<LazyMotion features={domMax} strict>\`. It looks safe because it currently renders fine, but if any future consumer renders PreviewRail (or another m.\*-using vendored component) outside that provider tree, \`strict\` mode will throw at runtime. When reusing these vendored components elsewhere, verify/document the LazyMotion provider dependency first.

<!-- lore:019fd756-4d43-75f4-9f01-2e324cbebd98 -->
* **listThreads N+1 query fixed via window-function batch fetch**: In apps/server/src/lib/remix-store.ts, listThreads() called threadPreview(row.id) per thread to grab the first user message for the preview — an unbounded per-thread SELECT. For a page of 30 threads this issued 30+ extra queries pulling full JSON blobs, even though the outer query was paginated (looks fine because 'only 30 threads', but each threadPreview scan itself had no LIMIT). Fixed with one batched query using SQLite's ROW\_NUMBER() window function to fetch the earliest message row per thread in a single pass. Behavior change: old code skipped non-user leading rows to find the first \*user\* message; new version takes the single earliest row and returns "" if it isn't from the user (renders as "Untitled conversation") — accepted since threads always start with a user instruction.

<!-- lore:019f0348-3334-7e1e-9748-87f2d7bbec74 -->
* **loadPlugins() JSDoc omitted built-ins — builtins are always prepended first**: Trap: \`loadPlugins()\` JSDoc in \`packages/sdk/src/loader.ts\` (lines 121-123) described load order as 'entries → local files → flatten presets → setup → sort by enforce', which looks complete. Fix: built-in plugins (passed via \`LoadPluginsOptions.builtin\`) are prepended BEFORE all other plugins, then the enforce-sort runs. The JSDoc was updated to include this. Built-ins bypass npm import/factory flow but \`setup()\` is still called. Built-ins are never filtered by disabled lists.

<!-- lore:019fcc78-8112-7ce2-b4b5-a8a77a38e104 -->
* **Local main stale vs contributor fork remote inflates git diff**: Trap: when reviewing a PR whose remote origin is actually a contributor's fork (e.g. tigerisaac/freestyle for freestyle-voice/freestyle), \`git diff main..HEAD\` after \`gh pr checkout \<PR>\` can show wildly inflated results (40 files/+888/-2104) because local \`main\` is stale relative to upstream — looks like the PR is huge but isn't. Fix: trust the PR's own file list/diff from the GitHub API (\`gh pr diff\` or the PR page) over a local \`git diff main..HEAD\`, or fetch/rebase local main from upstream before diffing. Confirmed actual scope for PR #541 was 5 files (+715/-122), matching GitHub's reported diff.

<!-- lore:019fd74b-8054-71f6-b974-a36af8b60e5b -->
* **Long-lived expanded RemixChat bypasses server thread-freshness check on reuse**: Trap: bumping REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS (remix-store.ts:8) alone looks sufficient for a session-idle-timeout feature because the pill's existing dismiss timers (MINI\_SETTLED\_DISMISS\_MS=3s, MINI\_IDLE\_DISMISS\_MS=7s, remix-chat.tsx:242-244) usually unmount RemixChat well before the idle window. But if a user leaves an EXPANDED (non-minimized) chat open indefinitely, \`remixRef.current\`/\`chatView\` stay non-null so RemixChat never unmounts, and its mount-effect freshness check (remix-chat.tsx:106-131) never re-runs. The next \`remix:open-chat\` event then takes the 'reuse' branch (app.tsx:2604-2606, \`patchRemix({phase:'chat'})\`) instead of remounting, skipping the server \`isFresh()\` check entirely regardless of elapsed time. Fix (chosen over adding a second client-side idle constant, which risks drifting from the server value): track \`lastRemixActivityAt\` bumped on \`onFinish\`/send, and force \`endRemix({hide:false})\` before reuse if idle exceeds the threshold, so RemixChat remounts and re-checks server freshness.

<!-- lore:019fd731-5084-7177-a366-8190b1744377 -->
* **Long-open expanded RemixChat bypasses idle-timeout freshness check**: Trap: if a user leaves an EXPANDED Remix chat window open indefinitely (never dismissed), \`remix\` stays non-null and RemixChat stays mounted — so the mount-time freshness check (remix-chat.tsx:106-131) never re-runs. The next \`remix:open-chat\` event takes the session-REUSE branch (app.tsx:2604-2606, \`patchRemix({phase:"chat"})\`) instead of remounting, silently bypassing the server's \`isFresh()\`/\`REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS\` idle check entirely — the idle timeout only fires between mount/unmount cycles, not while mounted. Looks safe because minimized sessions DO auto-dismiss (MINI\_SETTLED\_DISMISS\_MS=3s, MINI\_IDLE\_DISMISS\_MS=7s in remix-chat.tsx:242-244) — but no timer exists for an actively expanded chat. Fix (recommended): track \`lastRemixActivityAt\`, force \`endRemix({hide:false})\` before reopening if idle >threshold so RemixChat remounts and re-checks server freshness — avoids introducing a second, possibly-drifting client-side idle constant (rejected alternative: a standalone client setTimeout mirroring MINI\_SETTLED\_DISMISS\_MS).

<!-- lore:019fb9ab-5db7-7f4e-bc1c-4caefeb7f127 -->
* **macOS CGEvent tap suppresses compound hotkeys — key swallowing and dictation activation**: On macOS, Freestyle registers a CGEvent tap (native key listener) that suppresses the configured compound hotkey so it doesn't leak into foreground apps. KeyCastr will show only the modifier (e.g., \`⌥\`) when the hotkey is pressed — this is expected suppression, not a bug. Dictation may fail to activate if: (1) Accessibility permission is missing or was revoked (check System Settings > Privacy & Security > Accessibility), (2) the app fell back to \`globalShortcut\` toggle mode (no key-up events), or (3) the native listener registered but the NSEvent monitor didn't (Sequoia changes). Troubleshooting: check Logs → Reveal Logs for \`macos-key-listener\` errors; verify Accessibility is enabled; try the default Globe/Fn hotkey; ensure full quit (Cmd+Q) and re-add Freestyle to Accessibility if permissions were reset.

<!-- lore:019f2449-2f8d-7095-ae2f-f75b1151f5cb -->
* **macOS glass/vibrancy removal: transparent:true at line 473 is for voice pill — do NOT remove it**: Trap: commit \`9191962\` added \`transparent: true\` to \`createSettingsWindow()\` as part of the glass effect. When reverting glass, it's tempting to remove ALL \`transparent: true\` occurrences. Fix: \`transparent: true\` at ~line 473 in \`createAppWindow()\` (voice pill window) is pre-existing and intentional — the pill window requires transparency. Only remove the darwin-gated block (\`backgroundColor: '#00000000'\`, \`transparent: true\`, \`vibrancy: 'under-window'\`, \`visualEffectState: 'active'\`) from \`createSettingsWindow()\` (~line 577). \`body { background: transparent }\` in \`globals.css\` base layer is also pre-existing — leave it.

<!-- lore:019fd0fd-5e78-73eb-b3ca-ea73912f173c -->
* **main/index.ts pill window-move listener: display-match + 10px tolerance avoids false drag detection**: The pill window's move listener (main/index.ts ~855-894) must distinguish user drags from programmatic repositioning (setProgrammaticPosition, anchorPillToFocusedDisplay). Comparing only against the last programmatic target seems sufficient, but a trailing 'window moved' event can fire after the cursor already moved to another monitor, making a programmatic settle look like a manual drag — this previously caused pillPosition to falsely latch to "custom" and freeze the pill on one screen. Fix: ignore moves that exactly match programmaticTarget or are mid-animation, AND compare the new position against presetPositionForDisplay computed for the window's actual current display (via screen.getDisplayMatching on the window's own bounds, never the cursor's display) with a 10px tolerance before treating it as a real drag.

<!-- lore:019fd719-5c61-741b-8e3d-a61e9b16f6a2 -->
* **mainWindow is the pill; settingsWindow is the dashboard**: In apps/electron/src/main/index.ts, the variable named \`mainWindow\` (~line 384) is actually the PILL window — it loads \`getPillURL()\` → pill.html → app.tsx, not the dashboard. The dashboard window is \`settingsWindow\`, built via \`buildSettingsWindow()\`/shown via \`showSettingsWindow()\`, loading \`getDashboardURL()\` → index.html → dashboard.tsx. This naming looks backwards ("mainWindow" suggests the primary dashboard UI) but is historical — don't assume 'main window' in code/comments refers to the dashboard; it refers to the pill.

<!-- lore:019f5b5e-925f-7ba2-b475-045bf614400a -->
* **max models.dev cache TTL from 10 min to 6 hours**: Increased \`models.dev\` API response cache TTL from 10 minutes to 6 hours in \`apps/server/src/routes/models.ts:170\`. Reduces external API calls while keeping model lists reasonably fresh. Cache key is the full response; STT models from models.dev remain limited to Groq whisper models (others are excluded because we have no \`TranscriptionProvider\` implementation for those platforms). The hardcoded \`BUILTIN\_VOICE\_MODELS\` list remains the source of truth for STT providers not tracked by models.dev (Deepgram, ElevenLabs, OpenAI transcription).

<!-- lore:019f432c-9ca9-7349-ad8d-70e29be4f2c5 -->
* **Memory notes about code removals may be stale — always verify with git log before trusting**: Trap: A long-term memory entry recording a prior removal (e.g. \`showLocal\`, \`hasLocalVoice\`, On-device row) looks authoritative and suggests the change was already merged. Fix: always verify with \`git log --all --grep\` or \`gh pr list\` before trusting a memory note about a removal. In this case, memory note \`\[019f40e3]\` recorded On-device row removal but \`git log\` confirmed it was never committed or merged on any branch — the row remained live on \`main\`. Memory notes can reflect planned or in-progress work that was abandoned.

<!-- lore:019fbc59-606f-746d-b545-e3dbcccb4d97 -->
* **mergeCloudVocabularyTerms is one-way additive — cloud deletes never propagate down**: \`mergeCloudVocabularyTerms()\` (apps/server/src/lib/vocabulary.ts) inserts cloud vocabulary terms not already present locally (case-insensitive dedup) and NEVER deletes local terms — it looks like a full sync because it's wired into \`pullCloudPreferences()\` on every launch/sign-in/industry-change, but it's intentionally one-directional (cloud→local, additive only). Known limitation, explicitly out of scope for PR #520: cloud-side deletes don't propagate down, and local vocabulary edits don't push up. Full two-way sync is a deliberate follow-up, not an oversight. Batch writes use explicit \`BEGIN\`/\`COMMIT\` because \`node:sqlite\` has no \`.transaction()\` helper.

<!-- lore:019fb9f9-a33f-78b2-87ae-f77282c323c9 -->
* **Mobile /v1/usage vs desktop /usage endpoint mismatch — unverified**: Mobile's \`fetchCloudUsage()\` (apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/usage.ts:23) calls \`${cloudUrl()}/v1/usage\`, while desktop's cloud usage calls call \`/usage\` (apps/server/src/lib/freestyle-cloud.ts:518, 923). This looks like it could just be a documented mobile-only endpoint, but it may actually be a bug (wrong path) or the cloud service may alias both paths — unverified as of Aug 2026. Before building a shared cloud-fetch helper or unifying usage endpoints, confirm with the Freestyle Cloud service which path is canonical; do not assume either app's path is correct without checking the cloud service's route table.

<!-- lore:019fb9f9-a35c-7801-9c15-d01157126988 -->
* **Mobile app AGENTS.md: Expo v57 API surface changed — must consult versioned docs before coding**: apps/mobile/AGENTS.md states "Expo HAS CHANGED" and requires reading versioned docs at https://docs.expo.dev/versions/v57.0.0/ before writing any Expo-related code. This exists because Expo 57 (used here, alongside RN 0.86.0, React 19.2.3) has API differences from older Expo versions that general training knowledge may not reflect — code that looks correct based on older Expo patterns can be wrong or deprecated. Always check the pinned v57 docs before adding/modifying Expo APIs in apps/mobile.

<!-- lore:019fc2ee-7537-7d93-a111-027eac5d1675 -->
* **Mobile fetchCloudVocabularyTerms: undefined ≠ empty array**: In apps/mobile/src/lib/cloud/preferences.ts, \`fetchCloudVocabularyTerms()\` returning \`undefined\` means 'nothing synced yet' and callers MUST leave the local vocabulary list untouched — first launch or signed-out state must never wipe local terms. It's tempting to treat undefined the same as an empty result, but an explicit empty array \`\[]\` is a distinct signal meaning the user deleted all terms on another device, and that deletion must mirror down locally. Conflating the two (e.g. treating undefined as '\[], so clear local list') would wipe a fresh install's local vocabulary on first sync attempt.

<!-- lore:019f2988-db44-7956-a26e-847c2cca07c5 -->
* **Nested circle-in-circle icon artifact: CheckCircle2 inside a rounded-full span**: Trap: placing \`CheckCircle2\` (a circle-with-check glyph) inside a \`rounded-full\` span with a filled background creates a circle-inside-a-circle that looks muddy — it seems like a natural way to make a check indicator. Fix: use a bare \`Check\` icon (\`strokeWidth={3}\`) inside the filled \`rounded-full\` span instead. \`CheckCircle2\` is only appropriate as a standalone icon, not nested inside another circular container. Observed and fixed in \`tone.tsx\` cleanup and subset selector cards. \`CheckCircle2\` was fully removed from the import when the Cleanup tab was redesigned (Fix 6).

<!-- lore:019fb9b6-aadd-7c76-a8b3-0f245812043d -->
* **Network proxy/CA settings duplicated in Network tab**: The Network tab in Settings shows duplicate rows for \`network\_proxy\_url\` and \`network\_ca\_cert\_path\` because both the proxy/CA section and the legacy endpoint list each render their own GETs. The legacy endpoints (\`/api/settings/network\_proxy\_url\`, \`/api/settings/network\_ca\_cert\_path\`) were kept for backward compatibility and also queried by \`config.freestyle.json\` polling, causing 4–6 requests on page open. Consolidate by removing the legacy GET routes or deduplicating via a shared cache key; prefer the explicit proxy/CA section as the single source of truth.

<!-- lore:019faf89-ec1d-7c1f-93dc-504d3a933b0a -->
* **New /api/\* route prefixes must be added to TIMEOUT\_PREFIXES in apps/server index.ts**: Trap: adding a new top-level route group (e.g. \`/api/org\`) to the router looks complete once it's mounted and typechecks — but the 30s request-timeout middleware only applies to prefixes explicitly listed in \`TIMEOUT\_PREFIXES\` in \`apps/server/src/index.ts\`. Sibling groups like \`/api/usage\` are in the list; a newly added group can be silently missing, leaving those routes with no timeout guard. Fix: always add the new prefix to \`TIMEOUT\_PREFIXES\` alongside adding the route mount. Caught during PR review for the org endpoints (\`/api/org\` was missing).

<!-- lore:019fd2f8-87e3-72c4-805f-2f1838fe7f15 -->
* **New unconditional schema migrations must guard with tableExists() — test fixtures skip early versions**: tests/v20-merge-reconciliation.test.ts seeds an in-memory DB stamped at schema\_version=19 with only schema\_version/settings/sessions tables, skipping the v1/v2 migrations that normally create transcription\_history/model\_configs. Adding a new unconditional v21 migration (CREATE INDEX on transcription\_history(created\_at) and model\_configs(type,is\_default)) crashed with 'no such table' against these fixtures — looks safe because production DBs always run migrations in order from v0, but test fixtures don't. Fix: added tableExists(db, name) helper (queries sqlite\_master) in apps/server/src/lib/schema.ts and guard each new CREATE INDEX/ALTER with an existence check before executing — matches the existing defensive pattern used by v20's PRAGMA table\_info checks.

<!-- lore:019fd388-094d-7fce-b45d-18121aa392a5 -->
* **normalizeOpenaiBaseUrl strips only trailing /v1, not full paths**: In apps/server/src/routes/settings.ts, /local-llm/test and /openai-stt/test normalize a user-pasted base URL before probing \`${url}/v1/models\`. Old regex \`.replace(/\\/+$/,"").replace(/\\/v1$/,"")\` only stripped an exact trailing "/v1" — pasting a full endpoint URL like "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/audio/transcriptions" left "/v1/audio/transcriptions" intact, so the probe hit ".../v1/audio/transcriptions/v1/models" (404), surfaced to the user as an opaque 502. Looked correct because most users paste a bare base URL ending in /v1. Fixed with \`normalizeOpenaiBaseUrl()\` using \`.replace(/\\/v1(?:\\/\[^?#]\*)?$/, "")\` to strip /v1 and everything after it. Covered by apps/server/tests/openai-stt-base-url.test.ts.

<!-- lore:019f64f6-14dd-7c13-b11d-e9034080f164 -->
* **Onboarding model setup: Open model source button + i18n**: Onboarding uses the same \`ModelSetupPanel\` (updated in PR #388) that renders an "Open model source" button when \`model.state?.errorSourceUrl\` is present. The button opens the HF URL via \`window.api.openExternal()\` and is wired into the onboarding flow. i18n key \`onboarding.modelSetup.openModelSource\` added and translated into de/es/fr/it/ja/pt locales. The same error-source URL is propagated from \`download-guard.ts\` → \`whisper/models.ts\`/\`mlx-asr/models.ts\` → renderer state → \`ModelSetupPanel\`.

<!-- lore:019fd762-e373-711f-927e-5b815c21634c -->
* **onRemixOpenChat reuses stale Remix session without freshness recheck**: The Remix pill's 15-min idle freshness check (REMIX\_THREAD\_IDLE\_MS in apps/server/src/lib/remix-store.ts, isFresh()/getActiveThread()) only re-runs when \<RemixChat> remounts — the freshness GET fires once in a mount effect with empty deps (remix-chat.tsx). beginRemix (app.tsx hotkey-down path) always calls endRemix({hide:false}) before creating a new session, guaranteeing remount+recheck. But onRemixOpenChat (app.tsx, persistent-bar hover path) reuses an existing session via patchRemix(...) whenever remixRef.current is truthy — no teardown, no remount, no freshness recheck. If a user leaves an expanded chat open past the idle window and reopens via the bar, the stale session is reused indefinitely instead of starting fresh. As of Aug 2026 this gap is identified but not yet fixed; planned fix is tracking last-activity and calling endRemix before reuse if idle beyond the threshold.

<!-- lore:019fd388-0975-7df7-ba7f-068108c40798 -->
* **OpenAI-compatible STT connect test only checks /v1/models, not transcription capability**: The /local-llm/test and /openai-stt/test probes (apps/server/src/routes/settings.ts) only check \`${url}/v1/models\` responds 200 — a green "connected" status does NOT confirm the endpoint supports audio transcription. E.g. OpenRouter's /api/v1 is chat-model-oriented; a passing connectivity test there doesn't guarantee /v1/audio/transcriptions works for real dictation. Don't treat this test as an end-to-end capability check when debugging STT provider setup — it's only a base-URL/auth reachability check.

<!-- lore:019fd69b-a6b6-76f7-82da-8e93c9f13224 -->
* **OpenAI-compatible STT Test finds models but can't select them (voiceItems vs EndpointConnectState.models)**: apps/electron model-list.tsx's \`buildVoiceRows()\` renders selectable voice rows exclusively from \`m.voiceItems\` (computed in use-models.ts via buildSettingsVoiceItems/buildVoiceItems). \`EndpointConnectState.models\` (use-endpoint-connect.ts's \`runEndpointTest\`, populated from \`/openai-stt/test\`'s \`{ok, models: string\[]}\`) is a totally separate array only used to render 'Connected · N models found' text. Testing a custom OpenAI-compatible endpoint looks successful (positive N-models message) but the discovered model IDs are never merged into \`voiceItems\`, so the user has no way to pick one — only base\_url/api\_key get persisted, and the active model stays whatever it was before (e.g. openai/whisper-1), just now redirected to the custom URL. Any fix must merge discovered model IDs into voiceItems/rows, not just display the count.

<!-- lore:019fd667-c52e-7e24-bf40-b84f2a9d8787 -->
* **openai-stt test never wires discovered models into voiceItems**: In apps/electron's models picker, \`/openai-stt/test\` (settings.ts) returns \`{ok, models: string\[]}\`, stored by use-endpoint-connect.ts's \`runEndpointTest()\` into \`EndpointConnectState.models\`. But model-list.tsx's \`buildVoiceRows()\` only reads \`m.voiceItems\` (built via \`buildVoiceItems\`/\`buildSettingsVoiceItems\` in use-models.ts) — it never reads \`EndpointConnectState.models\`. Looks correct because the UI shows 'Connected · N models found', implying discovery worked, but there's no path merging discovered model IDs into voiceItems/rows: after testing a custom endpoint, users still can't pick a specific model — the active voice model stays whatever default was configured. Fix in progress (Aug 2026): render \`openaiStt.models\` as selectable rows in a new 'Custom endpoint' picker view; selecting one configures \`openai/\<model-id>\` as active voice model.

<!-- lore:019fd674-49d3-775e-a282-e2e97a2b04bb -->
* **openaiStt.models discovered but never wired into selectable voiceItems rows**: In use-endpoint-connect.ts, runEndpointTest()'s successful probe sets EndpointConnectState.models (string\[], e.g. m.openaiStt.models) — but model-list.tsx's buildVoiceRows() builds ALL voice picker rows purely from m.voiceItems (use-models.ts, derived via buildVoiceItems from available models + whisper/mlx status + defaultVoice + keyProviders). The two are separate structures; testing a custom OpenAI-compatible endpoint (settings.ts /openai-stt/test, probes \`${url}/v1/models\`) never merges its discovered models into voiceItems. Result: UI shows 'Connected · N models found' text but there's no row to select — the actual active voice model stays unchanged, just re-routed to the custom base URL. Looks correct because the test succeeds and displays a count. Fix (in progress): render connect.models as selectable rows; selecting one configures \`openai/\<model-id>\` (server's stripProviderPrefix in streaming/utils.ts strips the prefix before calling provider.transcription, so no new provider is needed).

<!-- lore:019fd6dc-956f-7cc0-987d-b54e39f314e9 -->
* **OpenaiSttConnect Test discovers models but never surfaces them as selectable rows**: EndpointConnectState.models (populated by runEndpointTest in use-endpoint-connect.ts after testing a custom OpenAI-compatible endpoint) holds discovered model IDs, but voice picker rows are built solely from m.voiceItems via buildVoiceRows (model-list.tsx) — a separate, unconnected data path. This makes 'Connected · N models found' look like the flow is complete, but nothing wires discovered models into selectable rows, so users can't pick one — the active model stays whatever it was, just redirected to the custom base URL. Fix (Aug 2026, WIP): add a dedicated voice-only 'Custom endpoint' PickerOption; selecting a discovered model configures \`openai/\<model-id>\`, reusing the existing \`openai\` provider's baseURL override since transcription strips the provider prefix before calling \`provider.transcription()\`.

<!-- lore:019fd733-c9f8-72d6-a846-49a2981e848f -->
* **OpenaiSttConnect test finds models but never lets user select one**: Testing a custom OpenAI-compatible STT endpoint (OpenaiSttConnect form, model-list.tsx) shows "Connected · N models found" and looks like it should let the user pick a model since it discovered them. It doesn't: \`EndpointConnectState.models\` (from use-endpoint-connect.ts's runEndpointTest, populated by \`/openai-stt/test\` route) is never merged into \`voiceItems\`, and \`buildVoiceRows\` (model-list.tsx:100) only reads \`m.voiceItems\` — so discovered model IDs are display-only text, and the actually active voice model stays whatever was previously configured (e.g. openai/whisper-1), just redirected to the custom base URL. Planned fix: after a successful test, render each discovered model ID as a selectable row; selecting one configures \`openai/\<model-id>\` (existing \`openai\` provider + baseURL override), since \`stripProviderPrefix\` correctly strips the \`openai/\` prefix before routing (server/streaming/utils.ts:74).

<!-- lore:019fd70a-ec76-76df-b8f0-75fdb26ee0ff -->
* **OpenaiSttConnect Test finds N models but none are selectable**: In apps/electron models UI, \`EndpointConnectState.models: string\[]\` (set by \`runEndpointTest\` in use-endpoint-connect.ts after the \`/openai-stt/test\` probe) is a completely separate array from \`voiceItems\`, which is what \`buildVoiceRows()\` (model-list.tsx) actually renders as pickable rows. Looks like a simple UI gap since the models array already exists, but nothing merges it into \`voiceItems\`/rows — so after testing a custom OpenAI-compatible endpoint, the UI only shows 'Connected · N models found' with no way to pick a specific one. The actually active voice model stays whatever was previously selected (e.g. openai/whisper-1), just silently redirected to the custom base URL. Fix requires either merging \`connect.models\` into selectable rows keyed \`openai/\<model-id>\`, or rendering them directly in a dedicated view.

<!-- lore:019fd71a-3493-7c43-9808-535ecc68819c -->
* **OpenaiSttConnect test results never merged into voiceItems — no model picker**: In apps/electron .../models/use-endpoint-connect.ts, runEndpointTest() (used by the custom OpenAI-compatible STT endpoint test) sets EndpointConnectState.models: string\[] via setModels(result.models ?? \[]) after a successful probe, but nothing downstream consumes that array. model-list.tsx's buildVoiceRows() builds all voice picker rows purely from m.voiceItems (a separate useMemo in use-models.ts) which the custom-endpoint test never populates. Result: pasting a custom OpenAI-compatible URL shows 'Connected · N models found' but there is no way to pick a specific discovered model — the actually configured model stays whatever it was (e.g. openai/whisper-1), just redirected to the new base URL. Looks correct because real feedback is shown ('N models found'), masking that nothing turns that data into a selectable row. Fix: render EndpointConnectState.models as selectable PickerOption rows in a new 'Custom endpoint' voice-picker view, configuring openai/\<model-id> on select.

<!-- lore:019fd6f6-bd1f-7f46-b7bb-142fb7731ac9 -->
* **OpenaiSttConnect test success doesn't create selectable model rows**: In model-list.tsx, useEndpointConnect's runEndpointTest() (use-endpoint-connect.ts) sets EndpointConnectState.models: string\[] on a successful /openai-stt/test probe, but voice model rows are built separately by buildVoiceRows(m.voiceItems) (model-list.tsx:100), which reads only use-models.ts's voiceItems array — never EndpointConnectState.models. Looks correct because 'Connected · N models found' renders and base\_url/api\_key do persist; but no code merges discovered model IDs into voiceItems, so users can't pick a specific model — the active voice model stays whatever was previously selected (e.g. openai/whisper-1), just redirected to the custom base URL via OpenAITranscriptionProvider's baseURL override. Fix: render discovered models as selectable rows; selecting one configures \`openai/\<model-id>\` (reusing the existing openai provider via stripProviderPrefix).

<!-- lore:019fafcb-7605-71bb-bce5-b42352cf647a -->
* **Org switcher menu: use optimistic mutation, not preventDefault, to keep dropdown responsive**: Trap: when a DropdownMenuItem onSelect triggers an async mutation (e.g. switching active org), Radix closes the menu before the mutation resolves — looks like the click did nothing. The fix that seems obvious is calling e.preventDefault() in onSelect to block the close until the mutation fires, but this leaves the menu open awkwardly, and disabling the active item to block re-clicks looks broken. Fix: let the menu close immediately (no preventDefault); use React Query's onMutate to optimistically write the new active org into the cache (from the already-fetched org list) for instant UI feedback; onError rolls back to the onMutate snapshot; onSettled always invalidates the query to sync with server truth. Keep the active item clickable but no-op instead of disabled. Pattern used in useSetActiveOrganization (apps/electron/src/renderer/src/lib/use-profile.ts).

<!-- lore:019fcbf0-d16c-740c-8d4f-86493d5586ff -->
* **permission-flow.test.ts: closeApp renamed to closePermissionApp (SIGKILL, avoids CI wedge)**: apps/electron/tests/permission-flow.test.ts's teardown helper was renamed \`closeApp\`→\`closePermissionApp\` in main (PR #516): \`app.close()\`'s native cleanup intermittently wedges macOS CI, producing two 60s Playwright timeouts. The fixture now uses \`childProcess.kill("SIGKILL")\` with a 5s race timeout instead, since this fixture only validates startup permissions, not production shutdown. Branches predating this rename keep the old \`closeApp\` (uses \`app.close()\` + 10s SIGKILL fallback) — merging both survives as duplicate definitions. Always standardize on \`closePermissionApp\` and delete the old \`closeApp\`.

<!-- lore:019fcbf1-d942-7f28-b77c-221f82ffbf47 -->
* **permission-flow.test.ts: use closePermissionApp, never app.close(), for teardown**: apps/electron/tests/permission-flow.test.ts's teardown helper was renamed \`closeApp\` → \`closePermissionApp\` in main (PR #516), switching from \`app.close()\` (10s SIGKILL fallback) to direct \`childProcess.kill("SIGKILL")\` (5s timeout). Reason (source comment): 'This fixture validates startup permissions, not production shutdown. Avoid app.close(), which runs native cleanup that intermittently wedges macOS CI and leaves Playwright waiting through two 60-second timeouts.' When merging older branches that still reference \`closeApp\`/\`app.close()\`, standardize all call sites on \`closePermissionApp\` and delete the duplicate old definition — don't keep both. Electron Playwright e2e tests run with \`workers: 1\` (serial only) per playwright.config.ts.

<!-- lore:019fc267-36a1-794b-9c67-a1ef1f92b6f5 -->
* **permission-flow.test.ts:183 flaky launch hang on macOS CI**: Trap: Playwright test "startup does not warn when Accessibility permission is granted" (apps/electron/tests/permission-flow.test.ts:183) intermittently hits \`Test timeout of 60000ms exceeded\` during app launch on macOS CI, plus a \`Worker teardown timeout\`, looking like a real startup regression. It is NOT — the same commit's PR run passed this exact test, and 10/11 other tests in the file pass on the same main run, confirming a flaky Electron launch hang rather than a code issue. Fix: re-run the job (\`gh run rerun --failed\`); consider adding Playwright \`retries: process.env.CI ? 2 : 0\` to absorb launch-hang flakiness (proposed, not yet implemented as of Aug 2026).

<!-- lore:019fcc47-e965-7ab2-8967-b8430c42aa40 -->
* **Pill CSS animation gotchas: data-show mount timing + inline style override**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx pill-card/pill-surface animations: (a) CSS enter transitions don't fire if the subtree mounts with data-show="true" already set on first paint — hold the attribute at its initial (hidden) value for one committed frame, then flip it, so the browser has a starting frame to transition from. (b) Inline styles silently outrank animated CSS rules on the same property — inline \`borderRadius: 20\` pinned the corner at 20px through a \`.pill-card\[data-show="false"] { border-radius: 60px }\` scale animation; the bug was masked because the scale animation visually distracted from the frozen corner. Fix: never set inline styles for any CSS property a stylesheet rule also animates.

<!-- lore:019fcbdf-37a6-7955-9c45-639840927671 -->
* **Pill elapsed-time readout showed garbage timestamp on first recording**: In app.tsx's voice pill, the elapsed-time readout was gated on \`recording || initializing\`, but \`startTimeRef\` is only set once the mic actually opens — on the first press of a fresh process it was still 0, so \`Date.now() - 0\` produced a huge value and the pill showed a stray full-timestamp readout (e.g. "29763131:43") instead of "0:00". Looked correct because the gating condition seemed to cover startup. Fixed in PR #541: gate on \`recording\` alone plus a non-zero start time, and reset the ref between sessions so staleness doesn't carry over.

<!-- lore:019fcc74-3e72-7cb9-80ea-1d11376a551d -->
* **Pill elapsed-time readout: startTimeRef=0 defaults instantly clear 60s threshold**: In apps/electron/src/renderer/src/pages/app.tsx, the pill's elapsed-time readout was gated on \`recording || initializing\`, but \`startTimeRef\` is only set once the mic actually opens. On the very first press of a fresh process, \`startTimeRef\` was still 0, so \`Date.now() - 0\` instantly exceeded the 60s threshold, showing a stray huge readout (e.g. "29763131:43") at the pill's right edge on every first startup. Fix (PR #541): gate on \`recording\` alone AND a non-zero start time, and reset the ref between sessions to avoid staleness. Any new timer/elapsed-time UI in this file must guard against a zero/unset ref, not just the recording-state flag.

<!-- lore:019fcc6d-261f-75f0-877e-59e8223ec4ba -->
* **Pill entrance animation: CSS transition doesn't fire when data-show mounts already true**: When React mounts a subtree with \`data-show="true"\` already set (as in the voice pill's entrance), CSS transitions never fire — there's no prior painted state to transition from, so the enter animation silently no-ops even though the transition rule exists. Fix used in apps/electron renderer pill code: hold the show-state at false for one committed frame (e.g. via rAF/setTimeout(0)) so the browser paints the initial state first, then flip to true on the next frame so the transition actually runs. Applies to any enter/mount animation gated by a boolean prop/attribute rather than a class toggle after mount.

<!-- lore:019fd277-3d85-7003-bf74-652a73f63228 -->
* **Pill hot-rect tracking polls cursor every 120ms (main/index.ts:262)**: While a pill hot-rect is set (for click-through detection), main/index.ts runs \`setInterval(..., 120)\` calling \`screen.getCursorScreenPoint()\` ~8×/sec on the main process to decide when to toggle \`setIgnoreMouseEvents\`. This is bounded (only active while hot-rect is set) but is a continuous main-thread poll rather than an event-driven approach. Flagged as a candidate for a native mouse-move event listener instead of polling, since Electron doesn't expose global mouse-move without native hooks.

<!-- lore:019fd0a0-84dc-7a75-9c0d-f698d6bf7a65 -->
* **Pill picks wrong monitor: display chosen by cursor position, not focused window**: Trap: \`getAppWindowPosition()\` (apps/electron/src/main/index.ts:647-649) selects the target display via \`screen.getDisplayNearestPoint(screen.getCursorScreenPoint())\` — a point-in-time mouse-cursor read. Comment at line 646 claims this shows the pill "where they are", which looks right for typical single-cursor-follows-focus workflows. But if the user works via keyboard on one monitor while the mouse cursor physically rests on another (e.g. two monitors stacked vertically), the pill renders on the display where the CURSOR is, not where the user is actually typing — e.g. pill appears at the seam/top of the bottom monitor while user works on the top one. Other code paths (index.ts:602, 663, 792) already use \`getDisplayMatching\` against a window's bounds for similar decisions — that pattern (focused-window bounds) would need to replace cursor-based selection to fix this, not attempted here (read-only investigation).

<!-- lore:019fcbdf-37bc-7558-8ed4-5e7ff1dad3ba -->
* **Pill preview harness: ws@8 required, explicit Chromium executablePath needed**: The voice-pill preview/test harness (companion to specs/voice-pill-motion.md) needs: (1) \`ws\` resolved from \`.pnpm\` pinned to \`ws@8\` — \`ws@7\` lacks the \`WebSocketServer\` export the harness uses; (2) an explicit \`executablePath\` for Chromium, since only \`chromium\_headless\_shell-\*\` exists in this environment, not the default Chromium binary. For genuine silence testing (the fake-audio-device emits a tone, not silence, so flatline detection never fires against it), generate real silence via \`ffmpeg -f lavfi -i anullsrc=r=48000:cl=mono -t 12 silence.wav\` and pass \`--use-file-for-fake-audio-capture=\<path>%noloop\` to Chromium.

<!-- lore:019f6ac9-caaa-7d98-af4d-4312b9431d96 -->
* **Plugin dev-link slug mismatch: hardcoded ROUTE\_BASE breaks settings page for dev packages**: Trap: plugin middleware and UI both hardcode the production slug (e.g. \`freestyle-voice-plugin-emoji\`) in \`ROUTE\_BASE\`/\`ROUTE\` constants. When the plugin is dev-linked as \`@freestyle-voice/plugin-emoji-dev\`, \`pluginSlug()\` derives \`freestyle-voice-plugin-emoji-dev\` — the hardcoded route never matches, causing 404 on the settings page. Looks correct because the production slug is right for published installs. Fix: derive \`routeBase\` dynamically in \`setup()\` from \`ctx.name\` (the installed package name at runtime). In the UI, extract the slug from the page's own URL (\`/api/plugins/\<slug>/ui/...\`) via a \`getRoute()\` helper instead of a hardcoded constant. This makes both middleware and UI work under any slug automatically.

<!-- lore:019f6a1a-df45-7ebc-850c-1e3947744834 -->
* **PluginPage blank screen: entry undefined renders empty div with no error — stale nav route cause**: Trap: navigating to \`/plugins/\<slug>/\<pageId>\` after a plugin is unlinked/uninstalled looks like it should show an error or loading state. \`PluginPage\` at \`plugin-page.tsx:62-68\` early-returns from \`useLayoutEffect\` if \`!slug || !pageId || !entry\`, rendering only \`\<div ref={placeholderRef} className="flex-1" />\` — blank screen with no error boundary, no loading indicator. Root causes: (1) stale nav item routes to a slug no longer in the plugin list; (2) \`PluginViewManager\` caches \`WebContentsView\` for unlinked dev plugin (\`view-manager.ts:97\`) and \`--unlink\` never calls \`invalidatePluginView()\`. Fix: add error/empty state to \`PluginPage\` when \`entry\` is undefined, and ensure unlink/uninstall always calls \`invalidatePluginView()\` and removes stale nav items.

<!-- lore:019efa02-1b7f-7631-a7a2-c2cbc71c686f -->
* **pluginSlug() test assertion must be updated manually after scope rename**: Error logging for server 500s: added local error logs so 500s are visible in console/log (previously only sent to telemetry). Added formatError() to index.ts to inline error+stack into Winston message (Winston printf only prints message arg). Added log.error() with full stack in transcribe.ts catch blocks (cloud combined mode and main transcribe path) and log.warn() in usage.ts for transient cloud 502s. Global onError in index.ts now logs \`METHOD /path -> status: \<error+stack>\` for all 500s before calling captureException. This uncovers the real cause of transcribe 500s (e.g., fetch failures) and usage 502s.

<!-- lore:019fd2aa-26fd-736b-ba42-848157470258 -->
* **PluginViewManager caches WebContentsView unbounded, no eviction cap**: apps/electron/src/main/plugins/view-manager.ts keeps one WebContentsView alive per visited plugin page in \`this.views\`, evicted only via explicit \`invalidate()\` or window close — there is no LRU/size cap despite existing detach-on-hide caching (avoids white-flash on hide/show). Looks safe because a session rarely visits many plugin pages, but a user clicking through many plugins in one session grows memory unbounded. Fix (not yet implemented): add an LRU/idle-eviction cap alongside the detach-on-hide logic.

<!-- lore:019fd309-6bec-7d6b-b264-153f1f0d3ae5 -->
* **PluginViewManager reads auth token per-request, not captured, to avoid leaking it**: apps/electron/src/main/plugins/view-manager.ts's PluginViewManager (authInstalledPartitions Set, ~line 35) reads the server auth token fresh on every request rather than capturing it once at view creation. Capturing once looks simpler/faster, but would risk a stale token surviving logout and would leak the local server's bearer token into any third-party API call a plugin page makes. Reading per-request and scoping injection strictly to the server's own origin (comment ~lines 124-127) ensures a plugin page talking to a third-party endpoint never receives the token.

<!-- lore:019fd277-3d7c-75a0-826b-ac899f50278a -->
* **PluginViewManager WebContentsView cache is unbounded (view-manager.ts)**: apps/electron/src/main/plugins/view-manager.ts caches every visited plugin's WebContentsView in \`this.views\` (keyed by slug) and only removes an entry via explicit invalidate() or window close — there is no LRU/eviction cap. A user who opens many different plugin pages over a session accumulates live WebContentsView instances (each with its own session partition \`persist:plugin-\<slug>\`) indefinitely. Looks safe because detach-on-hide avoids visual flash, but memory grows unbounded. Fix direction: add an eviction cap (LRU by last-shown time) when adding new views.

<!-- lore:019faf89-ec21-793e-b13a-d6a94ff42a61 -->
* **POST /api/client-error must never receive transcript or clipboard text**: The \`/client-error\` endpoint in apps/server (window.onerror, unhandled rejections, React error boundary reports) always persists reports to the local log file for diagnostics; PostHog reporting is gated by telemetry opt-out inside \`captureException\`. Only \`message\`, \`stack\`, \`source\`, and \`context\` fields are accepted by the schema — callers must never include transcript or clipboard text in any of these fields, since crash reports are more likely to be inspected/shared than regular logs. Same rationale as never logging transcript text elsewhere (\[\[019f5b5e-925f-7ba2-b475-045bf614400a]]).

<!-- lore:019fd28e-3bc5-7101-af73-0411174408ac -->
* **posthog.ts isEnabled() re-queries SQLite on every capture()**: apps/server/src/lib/posthog.ts's isEnabled() reads the telemetry\_enabled setting from SQLite on every capture()/captureException() call (posthog.ts:22-26) instead of caching it in memory. Every transcription/post-process event pays a DB round-trip just to check whether telemetry is on. Fix: cache the flag in memory, invalidated only when the setting is changed via PUT /api/settings.

<!-- lore:019f3cb4-43cc-7f1c-b96c-3a326a2b81ee -->
